### [1] [Objects as Audio-Visual Modal Sound Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05145)
*Zisen Shao,Zihao Wei,Derong Jin,Ruohan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AV-MSF，一种结合3D高斯泼溅与密集视觉特征的新型视听模态声场表示方法，通过少量冲击声录音和多视角图像实现物体级声学重建，在冲击声渲染上达到SOTA，并支持接触定位和声音编辑等下游应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代3D重建擅长几何与外观建模，但忽略了物体交互产生的丰富声学线索（如材质、刚度、结构属性）。现有冲击声建模方法要么依赖昂贵的物理仿真，要么需要大量数据集进行纯数据驱动学习，缺乏高效且泛化的表示。

Method: 基于3D高斯泼溅（3D Gaussian Splatting）集成密集3D视觉特征作为几何感知先验，使用紧凑且物理有意义的模态参数表示冲击声场，实现鲁棒的少样本重建（仅需少量冲击声录音和多视角图像）。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上达到SOTA冲击声渲染效果，超越基于物理和数据驱动的基线方法；展示了接触定位和物体声音编辑等下游应用。

Conclusion: AV-MSF是一种高效、物理可解释的物体级视听声场表示，通过少样本学习实现高质量的冲击声建模，并可拓展至多种交互式声学应用。

Abstract: While modern 3D reconstruction excels at modeling object geometry and appearance, it largely ignores the rich acoustic cues revealed through physical interaction. Object impact sounds convey material, stiffness, and structural properties that complement vision, yet existing impact sound modeling approaches either rely on expensive physics-based simulation or require large datasets to generalize in a purely data-driven manner. We introduce Audio-Visual Modal Sound Field (AV-MSF), a novel object-level acoustic representation reconstructed from multi-view images and only a few impact sound recordings. AV-MSF builds on 3D Gaussian Splatting integrated with dense 3D visual feature to provide a strong geometry-aware prior, and represents the impact sound field using compact, physically meaningful modal parameters, enabling robust few-shot reconstruction. Experiments on two real-world datasets show that AV-MSF achieves state-of-the-art impact sound rendering, outperforming both physics-based and data-driven baselines. Furthermore, we demonstrate downstream applications enabled by our representation, including contact localization and object sound editing.

</details>


### [2] [UAV3DCrop: Benchmarking 3D Reconstruction in Repeated Multi-Angle UAV Crop Surveys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06404)
*Junxiong Zhou,Xuechen Li,Chonghao Qiu,Lang Qiao,Xiaowei Jia,Qi Yang,Chishan Zhang,Leikun Yin,Nanshan You,Vipin Kumar,David Mulla,Ce Yang,Zhenong Jin,Licheng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UAV3DCrop是一个用于精准农业的3D作物监测公开基准，包含88,830张高分辨率RGB图像（91个场景，涵盖玉米、大豆、小麦、燕麦），设有两个赛道：Track A评估7种场景优化方法（NeRF和3DGS变体）在视角合成、深度恢复和冠层高度估算上的表现；Track B评估4种预训练前馈模型在零样本相机位姿和几何估计上的能力。结果表明，没有单一方法在三个目标上同时最优，且仅有一个前馈模型能恢复可用的度量尺度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建方法在通用基准上表现良好，但其渲染外观不一定能转化为农业上有用的度量几何和农学指标，缺乏专门针对作物田块评估的基准，因此需要构建一个面向精准农业的3D作物监测基准来系统评估现有方法。

Method: 构建了UAV3DCrop基准数据集，包含88,830张5280×3956像素的RGB图像，地面采样距离3.6-5.8 mm，来自91个场景（玉米、大豆、小麦、燕麦）的重复多角度无人机调查。设置两个赛道：Track A评估7种场景优化方法（NeRF和3DGS变体）在留出视角、深度（以摄影测量为参考）和冠层高度恢复上的表现；Track B评估4种预训练前馈模型在零样本相机位姿和几何估计上的表现。

Result: 场景优化方法在不同目标上排名不同：Splatfacto-big在视觉外观上领先，Scaffold-GS在深度上领先，并与Splatfacto在冠层高度上统计持平。前馈模型中，MapAnything在8个指标中的7个上领先，其余模型在不同作物上表现差异较大，且在绝对尺度上严重失败（对齐操作掩盖了这一问题）。重复采集揭示了不同输出类型和模型对采集序列位置和连接点多重性的敏感性。

Conclusion: 当前3D重建方法在农业应用上尚不可互换：没有单一方法同时在视觉外观、几何和冠层高度上最优，且四个前馈模型中仅一个能恢复可用的度量尺度。该基准为未来农业专用3D重建方法的发展提供了评估平台。

Abstract: Accurate 3D crop monitoring underpins data-driven precision agriculture by enabling field-scale analysis of plant structure, growth dynamics, and management response. Modern 3D reconstruction methods perform strongly on generic benchmarks, but rendered appearance may not translate into metrically and agronomically useful geometry in crop fields. We introduce UAV3DCrop, a public benchmark of repeated multi-angle unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) crop surveys. It contains 88,830 RGB images at $5280 \times 3956$ pixels, with a ground sampling distance of 3.6-5.8 mm, from 91 scenes spanning corn, soybean, wheat, and oat. Track A evaluates seven scene-optimized methods -- Neural Radiance Field (NeRF) and 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) variants -- on held-out views, photogrammetry-referenced depth, and canopy-height recovery. Track B tests four pretrained feed-forward models on zero-shot camera-pose and geometry estimation. The scene-optimized methods rank differently across the three targets: Splatfacto-big leads appearance, whereas Scaffold-GS leads depth and is statistically tied with Splatfacto for canopy height. Among feed-forward models, MapAnything leads on seven of the eight metrics, while the remaining models vary more across crops and fail severely on absolute scale in a way that alignment conceals. Repeated acquisitions reveal further sensitivities that differ by output type and by model, associated with position within the acquisition sequence and with tie-point multiplicity. Current 3D reconstruction methods are therefore not yet interchangeable for agronomic use: no single method wins on appearance, geometry, and canopy height at once, and only one of four feed-forward models recovers usable metric scale. The dataset is publicly available at https://link-dev.github.io/UAV3DCrop/

</details>


### [3] [Deep Evidential Regression for Sparse Forest Height Estimation from Multimodal Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06406)
*Laura Bader,Muhammad Ammar Ahmed,Xiao Xiang Zhu,Göran Kauermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用深度证据回归（DER）进行森林高度估计，在TreeUQ基准上基于Sentinel-1/2影像和U-Net架构，通过掩码证据损失处理标签稀疏性，实现了与确定性U-Net相当的预测性能，同时提供了良好校准的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在森林高度估计中虽预测准确，但通常无法量化预测不确定性。这一问题在稀疏监督和地理分布偏移的地理空间场景中尤为突出。因此，需要一种能够同时提供准确预测和可靠不确定性估计的方法。

Method: 采用深度证据回归（DER）框架，基于U-Net架构融合多模态Sentinel-1和Sentinel-2输入，针对树清单数据极端稀疏的特点引入掩码证据损失，实现稠密地理空间预测，在单次前向传播中同时预测树高和不确定性。

Result: 在TreeUQ基准上，DER方法取得了与确定性U-Net相当的预测性能，并且提供了良好校准的不确定性估计，验证了证据学习在森林结构不确定性感知估计中的有效性。

Conclusion: 深度证据回归为森林高度估计提供了一种高效的不确定性感知框架，能够在保持预测精度的同时量化不确定性，有望在碳核算、生物多样性监测等应用中发挥重要作用。

Abstract: Accurate estimation of forest height from satellite imagery is essential for applications such as carbon accounting, biodiversity monitoring, and ecosystem management. While recent deep learning approaches provide accurate predictions, they typically do not quantify predictive uncertainty. This limitation is particularly relevant in geospatial settings characterized by sparse supervision and geographic distribution shift. In this work, we investigate Deep Evidential Regression (DER) for forest height estimation on the TreeUQ benchmark, a large-scale dataset designed for the joint estimation of tree count and average tree height at 10 m resolution, based on Sentinel-1/-2 data as well as tree inventory data over the federal state of Bavaria. To account for the extreme label sparsity of the tree inventory data, we introduce a masked evidential loss for dense geospatial prediction. Using a U-Net architecture with multimodal Sentinel-1 and Sentinel-2 inputs, the proposed approach jointly predicts tree height and associated uncertainty estimates in a single forward pass. Experimental results show that DER achieves predictive performance comparable to a deterministic U-Net while additionally providing well-calibrated uncertainty estimates. These findings demonstrate the potential of evidential learning as an efficient framework for uncertainty-aware forest structure estimation from Earth observation data.

</details>


### [4] [TransSLR: A Lightweight Transformer for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06407)
*Lucia Yen Wanchi,Samuel Johnny,Victor Tolulope Olufemi,Emmanuel Aaron,Moise Busogi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TransSLR，一种轻量级时间Transformer编码器，从头训练于64帧归一化姿态序列，用于解决中非手语（CASL）等低资源手语识别问题。在CASL-W60基准上达到80.39%准确率，相比之前最好结果提升10.46%，且计算开销显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源手语（如CASL）的自动识别长期未解决。现有CASL-W60基准最高仅69.93%准确率，且微调高资源模型的常见策略因数据规模有限和领域鸿沟（词汇与视觉差异）而失效，预训练表示几乎无信息量。

Method: 提出TransSLR，一种轻量级时间Transformer编码器，从零训练于64帧归一化关节点序列，采用平均池化和分类头。通过使用几何关键点表示而非原始RGB，实现与手语者无关的泛化，不依赖视觉外观。编码器仅设计降低了计算开销。

Result: 在CASL-W60基准上，TransSLR达到80.39%准确率，超越先前最好结果69.93%达10.46%。与基于RGB和多模态基线相比均取得最先进性能，且计算效率更高，适合资源受限环境部署。

Conclusion: TransSLR通过轻量级姿态序列Transformer有效解决了低资源手语识别中的域偏移和数据稀缺问题，为CASL及其他低资源手语提供了可行的部署方案，展示了从零训练而非依赖预训练模型的优势。

Abstract: Automated Sign Language Recognition for under-represented languages remains a largely unsolved problem. Central African Sign Language (CASL) exemplifies this gap: the only available bench-mark, CASL-W60, has a best reported accuracy of 69.93%, and we show that the common heuristic of fine-tuning high-resource models fails to close it. This failure stems from two compounding factors: the limited scale of available CASL data and the significant lexical and visual domain gap between CASL and large-scale corpora such as WLASL, which renders pre-trained representations largely uninformative.
  To address this, we propose TransSLR, a lightweight Temporal Transformer Encoder trained from scratch on 64-frame normalized pose sequences, with average pooling and a classification head. By operating on geometric keypoint representations rather than raw RGB, TransSLR achieves signer-independent generalization without relying on visual appearance. On the CASL-W60 benchmark, TransSLR establishes a new state-of-the-art accuracy of 80.39%, surpassing the prior best by +10.46%. Beyond accuracy, our encoder-only design significantly reduces computational overhead, making deployment feasible in resource-constrained environments. We conduct extensive experiments on the CASL-W60 benchmark, comparing against RGB-based and multimodal baselines, and demonstrate that TransSLR achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [5] [Test-Time Adaptation with Online Personalized Energy-Based Cache for Fine-Grained Video Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06467)
*Masoumeh Sharafi,Muhammad Osama Zeeshan,Soufiane Belharbi,Alessandro Lameiras Koerich,Marco Pedersoli,Eric Granger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于能量的缓存个性化方法（EB-CaP），用于视频面部表情识别中的测试时自适应。该方法无需参数更新，利用轻量级能量模型从目标视频中采样原型并构建个性化缓存，结合CLIP相似度和自适应门控机制，在多个数据集上取得了优于现有方法的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频面部表情识别方法在面对不同被试的分布偏移时性能下降；测试时自适应方法通常需要更新参数增加计算量，而基于缓存的方法需要大量目标样本才能形成可靠的类原型，在适应初期和稀有类别上效果不佳。

Method: EB-CaP包含以下核心组件：1）轻量级能量模型从当前无标签视频中采样原型并填充个性化缓存；2）能量函数基于预训练CLIP，通过目标视频嵌入与类别文本嵌入的相似度指导原型采样；3）正负缓存分别存储可靠和不确定的目标嵌入；4）自适应熵门根据置信度分布控制缓存更新；5）多样性门限制冗余样本；6）最终预测结合缓存得分和当前CLIP得分。

Result: 在BioVid、StressID和BAH三个数据集上，EB-CaP在保持低计算和低内存开销的同时，优于现有的测试时自适应方法。

Conclusion: EB-CaP有效解决了视频FER中的主体特定分布偏移问题，无需参数更新即可实现高效的测试时个性化适应，具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Facial expression recognition (FER) in videos is challenging because models must identify subtle, temporally evolving affective states that vary across individuals. Although vision-language models provide transferable visual-semantic representations, models trained on subject-independent data often degrade under subject-specific distribution shifts at inference time. Existing test-time adaptation (TTA) methods commonly update model parameters during inference, increasing computational cost and latency. Cache-based methods avoid parameter updates, but they usually require enough target samples to form reliable class prototypes, which is difficult early in adaptation and for rarely observed classes. We introduce Energy-Based Cache Personalization (EB-CaP), a subject-based online TTA method for video FER that generates class-specific prototypes personalized to each target video. EB-CaP uses a lightweight energy-based model to sample prototypes from the current unlabeled video and populate a personalized cache online, without accumulating large amounts of target data or storing diverse source prototypes. Its energy function relies only on pretrained CLIP: similarities between the target video embedding and class text embeddings guide prototype sampling. In parallel, positive and negative caches store reliable and uncertain target embeddings. An adaptive entropy gate controls cache updates according to the evolving confidence distribution, while a diversity gate limits redundant samples. Final predictions combine cache-derived scores with the current CLIP scores. Experiments on BioVid, StressID, and BAH show that EB-CaP outperforms state-of-the-art TTA methods while maintaining low computational and memory overhead. Code is available at https://github.com/MasoumehSharafi/EB-CaP.

</details>


### [6] [InsertFuse: A Unified Framework for Multi-Category Reference-Guided Image Insertion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06490)
*Guangzhao Li,Qingyan Wei,Huayu Zheng,Yige Zheng,Chaoyang Zhang,Jie Yang,Yunan Ding,Yan Tai,Siqi Luo,Xiaohong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InsertFuse presents a unified framework for multi-category reference-guided image insertion. It decouples category-specific expertise learning from cross-category consolidation: first trains specialized experts for different insertion categories, then uses Insertion On-Policy Distillation (IOPD) to consolidate capabilities into a single student model. To enhance spatial control, it introduces Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning (TAGC) and Region-Balanced Flow Matching. Reference CFG is proposed to strengthen visual reference guidance, and IOPD transfers this enhanced supervision into the unified student. Experiments on AnyInsertion benchmark and a multi-category test set show state-of-the-art performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for reference-guided image insertion often struggle with multi-category scenarios due to cross-category interference when training a single model jointly on diverse insertion tasks. The paper aims to unify multiple insertion categories into one framework while preserving category-specific behavior and avoiding performance degradation caused by direct joint training.

Method: InsertFuse consists of three main components: (1) A two-stage training paradigm: first train specialized experts per insertion category, then apply Insertion On-Policy Distillation (IOPD) to transfer their knowledge into a unified student model while mitigating cross-category interference. (2) Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning (TAGC) for precise spatial control by mapping mask-derived geometric cues to the visual token grid. (3) Region-Balanced Flow Matching that separately normalizes prediction errors inside and outside the insertion region to avoid background-dominated and scale-dependent supervision. Additionally, Reference CFG is introduced to isolate and strengthen visual reference guidance under fixed scene and geometry conditions.

Result: The method achieves state-of-the-art performance on most metrics on the public AnyInsertion benchmark and a multi-category test set, demonstrating strong reference fidelity and generation quality across diverse insertion categories.

Conclusion: InsertFuse effectively unifies multi-category reference-guided image insertion by decoupling expert learning from capability consolidation, and introduces novel techniques for spatial control and balanced supervision. The proposed framework sets a new state-of-the-art, showing potential for practical applications requiring diverse insertion capabilities in a single model.

Abstract: We present InsertFuse, a unified framework for multi-category reference-guided image insertion. Its key idea is to decouple category-specific expertise learning from cross-category capability consolidation. InsertFuse first trains specialized experts for different insertion categories and then introduces Insertion On-Policy Distillation (IOPD) to consolidate their capabilities into a single student. By querying the matched expert at states visited by the student, IOPD preserves category-specific insertion behavior while mitigating the cross-category interference caused by direct joint training. To improve spatial control, we propose Token-Aligned Geometry Conditioning (TAGC), which maps mask-derived geometric cues to the visual token grid, and Region-Balanced Flow Matching, which separately normalizes prediction errors inside and outside the insertion region to prevent background-dominated and scale-dependent supervision. We further introduce Reference CFG to isolate and strengthen the guidance induced by the visual reference under fixed scene and geometry conditions, with IOPD transferring this enhanced supervision into the unified student. Extensive experiments on the public AnyInsertion benchmark and our multi-category test set demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on most metrics, showing strong reference fidelity and generation quality across diverse insertion categories.

</details>


### [7] [Improving Low-Resolution Face Recognition under Limited Data: How Synthetic Data Generation Can Close the Domain Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06580)
*Luis S. Luevano,Ünsal Öztürk,Hatef Otroshi Shahreza,Anjith George,Sébastien Marcel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This paper studies synthetic generation strategies for low-resolution face recognition on edge devices, finding that simple interpolation augmentation outperforms more complex methods and that generative methods must be validated on real LR data against a direct-feed baseline.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Low-resolution face recognition in surveillance settings is challenging due to scarcity of labelled native-LR and paired LR/HR data. While HR data is abundant, it is unclear how much synthesis effort (degrading HR to LR) improves recognition accuracy, motivating a systematic study of synthetic generation strategies for compact edge-device systems.

Method: The study compares multiple synthetic degradation strategies: interpolation-based degradation, knowledge distillation, a Prepended Domain Transformer (PDT), Real ESRGAN-style degradation, and a learned Super Resolution (SR) front-end with identity-aware loss. These are evaluated on synthetic cross-resolution benchmarks (LFW, CFP-FP, AgeDB-30) and the real native-LR dataset TinyFace.

Result: A synthetic-real gap exists: the optimal degradation on synthetic benchmarks differs from that on real LR. More synthesis effort does not help monotonically; the learned SR front-end does not surpass directly feeding the aligned LR image into a strong backbone. Simple interpolation augmentation of a compact backbone is the only synthesis that improves over its own baseline.

Conclusion: Generative methods for low-resolution face recognition must be validated on real LR data and against a direct-feed baseline to ensure practical effectiveness.

Abstract: Face Recognition (FR) systems in surveillance settings often encounter Low Resolution (LR) faces, those whose face region falls below the standard 112 $\times$ 112 input size. While labelled High Resolution (HR) training data is abundant, labelled native-LR data, and above all paired native LR/HR data, is scarce. One workaround is to synthesize LR data from the available HR faces, but how much synthesis effort is repaid in recognition accuracy remains unclear. We present a study of simple synthetic generation strategies for a compact, edge device-oriented face recognition system, spanning interpolation-based degradation, knowledge distillation, a Prepended Domain Transformer (PDT), Real ESRGAN-style degradation, and a learned Super Resolution (SR) front-end with an identity-aware loss. We evaluate these strategies on synthetic cross-resolution face benchmarks (LFW, CFP-FP, AgeDB-30) and on TinyFace, a real-world native LR dataset, and expose a synthetic-real gap: the degradation setting that is optimal on synthetic benchmarks is not the one that is optimal on real LR. We find that more synthesis effort does not help monotonically: the learned SR front-end does not surpass a direct feed of the aligned LR image into a strong backbone, while simple interpolation augmentation of a compact backbone is the only synthesis that improves over its own baseline. We conclude that generative methods for LR face recognition must be validated on real LR and against a direct-feed baseline, and release our pipeline at https://idiap.ch/paper/synth-lrfr

</details>


### [8] [Toward surface-based registration of a virtual preoperative cutting guide onto the mandible for reconstruction surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06599)
*Yue Yang,Jie Ying Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于增强现实（AR）的无标记配准方法，利用HoloLens 2在经口下颌骨重建手术中替代实体切割导板，通过牙齿加权的全局配准和非对称点对平面ICP实现虚拟导板与暴露下颌骨的精确对齐，中位目标配准误差（TRE）为4.05–7.10 mm，中位延迟0.805 s，验证了AR替代物理打印导板的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统患者特异切割导板虽能基于CT三维计划指导手术，但存在成本高、制作周期长、打印后无法调整、无菌问题导致术中中断等局限；而现有AR方法通常需要外部标记物或手动选择标志点，在经口入路中口腔内空间狭小、标记物放置困难。因此，本文旨在探索一种无标记、基于表面几何配准的AR方案，以替代物理导板，简化流程并提高手术灵活性。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 相机标定后，HoloLens 2飞行时间相机获取术中部分点云；(2) 用户提供粗略头部对齐以裁剪感兴趣区域；(3) 牙齿加权的全局配准阶段计算对应关系并求解截断最小二乘刚性对齐；(4) 非对称点对平面ICP阶段将完整CT下颌骨配准到部分深度点云目标；(5) 导板到下颌骨的变换将虚拟导板置于HoloLens世界坐标系，并通过姿态更新和插值跟踪目标运动。采用盲法体模协议，在全暴露、中等暴露和仅牙齿暴露三种条件下测量30个目标配准误差（TRE）点，并进行运动到显示延迟测试。

Result: 中位TRE在全暴露、中等暴露和仅牙齿暴露条件下分别为4.05 mm、6.10 mm和7.10 mm；中位运动到显示延迟为0.805秒。这些数值支持AR替代物理打印导板的可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了安装标记物和手动选择标志点的步骤，提供了一条通往经口切割引导的可测试路径，证明基于AR的无标记配准方法在精度和延迟上具备替代传统实体导板的潜力，有望降低手术成本、缩短准备时间并提高术中适应性。

Abstract: Mandibular reconstruction restores facial continuity and oral function after segmental resection. Patient-specific cutting guides transfer a computed tomography (CT)-based plan to the operating room with three-dimensional information, but printed guides add cost and lead time, cannot adapt after fabrication, and may interrupt surgery if sterility is lost. We investigate a markerless augmented reality (AR) alternative that registers a virtual cutting guide to the exposed mandible from surface geometry. The method extends surface-based registration for the transoral setting, where teeth form the most distinctive visible surface. After camera calibration, a HoloLens 2 time-of-flight camera captures a partial intraoperative point cloud. The user supplies a rough head-based alignment only to crop the region of interest. A teeth-weighted global stage computes correspondences and solves a truncated least-squares rigid alignment. An asymmetric point-to-plane iterative closest point (ICP) stage refines the complete CT mandible against the partial depth cloud target. The guide-to-mandible transform places the guide in the HoloLens world frame, while pose updates and interpolation follow target motion. We define a blinded phantom protocol with 30 target registration error (TRE) points under full, intermediate, and teeth-only exposure, plus a motion-to-display latency test. Our median TRE is 4.05, 6.10, and 7.10 mm respectively, and median latency is 0.805 s. These values support the feasibility of using AR to replace physical prints. The workflow removes mounted fiducials and manual landmark selection and provides a testable path toward transoral cutting guidance.

</details>


### [9] [SLED: Scalable Location Encoding via Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06612)
*Kevin Lane,Zhongying Wang,Esther Rolf,Morteza Karimzadeh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SLED is a distillation-based location encoder that uses geospatial location as a binding modality to pretrain lightweight, modular location encoders with any modality of geospatial data, eliminating the need for large batch sizes and spatiotemporal coregistration, achieving competitive performance across 19 benchmark tasks at a fraction of the compute cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing state-of-the-art location encoders rely on CLIP-style frameworks that require huge batch sizes (16K–32K), suffer from false negative samples, and scale poorly with additional modalities, making them computationally expensive and inflexible. A more efficient, scalable, and modular approach is needed.

Method: SLED employs distillation where geospatial location serves as a binding modality, enabling pretraining of location encoders with any modality (e.g., Sentinel-1, Sentinel-2, Landsat) without requiring spatiotemporal coregistration. The framework is lightweight, modular, and works effectively with batch sizes as small as 128, drastically reducing runtime and compute costs.

Result: Unimodal and multimodal SLED models keep pace with or outperform existing approaches on 19 diverse human-centric benchmark tasks. Pretraining requires only a fraction of the runtime and compute costs compared to CLIP-style models.

Conclusion: SLED provides a scalable, efficient, and modular alternative to CLIP-style location encoders, leveraging distillation to overcome key limitations and enabling flexible multimodal geospatial pretraining.

Abstract: The plethora of readily available geospatial data offers exciting opportunities to learn high quality representations of the planet, but the sheer size of the Earth Observations (EO), differing modalities, and different sensor types pose significant challenges in doing so. Location encoders have emerged as an efficient way of compressing EOs into location-specific embeddings. However, current state-of-the-art location encoders rely on computationally expensive CLIP-style frameworks that require large batch sizes in the 16K--32K range, suffer from false negative samples, and scale poorly with additional modalities. We introduce the Scalable Location Encoder via Distillation (SLED), a distillation-based location encoder that uses geospatial location as a binding modality to pretrain location encoders with any modality of geospatial data. The resulting location encoder framework is lightweight, modular, and can flexibly incorporate multiple modes, while eliminating the need for spatiotemporal coregistration of samples. SLED is performant with batch sizes as small as 128, enabling pretraining at a fraction of the runtime and compute costs of current state-of-the-art models. We demonstrate our approach by pretraining unimodal and multimodal SLED models on Sentinel-1, Sentinel-2, and Landsat imagery. We show that both unimodal and multimodal SLED models keep pace with or outperform existing approaches on a diverse set of 19 human-centric benchmark tasks and explore the benefits of using additional modes in pretraining.

</details>


### [10] [Do 3D Medical Foundation Models See Through MRI Artifacts? A Controlled Study of Representation Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06613)
*Julia Anna Mielcarz,Daniel Klaaby,Mostafa Mehdipour Ghazi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了五种自监督3D医学基础模型对MRI伪影的鲁棒性，发现不同模型对伪影的敏感程度差异显著，其中3DINO最稳定，而大规模或特定领域预训练并不能保证对伪影的不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督3D医学基础模型被广泛用作通用特征提取器，但它们对MRI伪影的敏感性尚不清楚，因此需要系统评估其表征鲁棒性。

Method: 使用BraTS-Africa数据集的四个MRI序列，对五种预训练3D编码器（涵盖不同架构、目标、预训练域和数据集规模）施加七种频率域和图像域伪影（五种预定义损坏级别），通过线性中心核对齐（CKA）、RankMe和UMAP评估表征鲁棒性，并辅以独立的分割一致性分析。

Result: 鲁棒性强烈依赖于模型和伪影类型：3DINO表征最稳定，BrainIAC对多种伪影高度敏感，NeuroVFM、BrainFM和Neuro-SimCLR表现居中且具有特定伪影特征；CKA显著下降而RankMe相对稳定，表明伪影扭曲表征几何结构但未导致维度塌缩；分割一致性在伪影下退化（尤其重影和Rician噪声），但与表征级鲁棒性仅部分对齐。

Conclusion: 更大规模或领域特定的预训练并不能保证对伪影的不变性，在异构MRI环境中部署3D基础模型前需要进行明确的鲁棒性评估。

Abstract: Self-supervised 3D medical foundation models are increasingly used as general-purpose feature extractors, yet their sensitivity to MRI artifacts remains poorly understood. We present a controlled evaluation of representation robustness across five pretrained 3D encoders spanning different architectures, objectives, pretraining domains, and dataset scales. Using BraTS-Africa cases with four MRI sequences, we generate seven frequency- and image-domain artifacts at five predefined corruption settings. Robustness is assessed using linear centered kernel alignment (CKA), RankMe, and UMAP, complemented by an independent segmentation-consistency analysis. We find that robustness is strongly model- and artifact-dependent. 3DINO exhibits the most consistently stable representations, while BrainIAC is highly sensitive to several corruptions; NeuroVFM, BrainFM, and Neuro-SimCLR show intermediate but distinct artifact-specific profiles. Across many conditions, CKA decreases substantially while RankMe remains comparatively stable, indicating that artifacts often distort representation geometry without causing dimensional collapse. Segmentation consistency also degrades under corruption, particularly for ghosting and Rician noise, but aligns only partially with representation-level robustness. These findings show that larger-scale or domain-specific pretraining alone does not guarantee artifact invariance and motivate explicit robustness evaluation before deploying 3D foundation models in heterogeneous MRI settings.

</details>


### [11] [When Semantics Saturate or Emerge: Adaptation-Conditional Semantic Utility in Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06673)
*Wei Liu,Xing Deng,Haijian Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在源自由跨域少样本学习（SF-CDFSL）中，基于零样本准确率选择的语言描述在目标域视觉适应后其排序并不保持。论文识别出两种模式：语义饱和（初始增益在适应后大幅缩减）和语义涌现（仅适应后细节描述才展现优势），表明零样本提示质量不能完全代理适应锚点质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SF-CDFSL方法常依据冻结视觉-语言模型上的零样本准确率来挑选语言描述，但该排序在目标域视觉适应（如LoRA）后是否仍然有效尚不清楚。本文旨在探究这一问题，验证零样本提示质量是否能可靠预测适应后的表现。

Method: 采用严格配对协议，在EuroSAT、CropDisease、ISIC和ChestX四个数据集上，比较通用类名模板与固定详细类描述在视觉低秩适应（LoRA）前后的准确率差异。定义δ_zero（适应前详细描述减基准准确率）和δ_lora（适应后对应差值），分析训练轨迹和样本级分解，并执行打乱语义控制、第二CLIP主干和多种随机种子等验证。

Result: 观察到两种典型情况：语义饱和（δ_zero>0但0<δ_lora≪δ_zero，如EuroSAT和CropDisease上初始增益8.13–21.54个百分点收缩至0.69–2.96个百分点）和语义涌现（δ_zero≤0但δ_lora>0，如ISIC和ChestX上细节描述仅在视觉表示更新后更有用）。饱和主要源于基准LoRA恢复了细节语义已解决的错误，涌现则与预测翻转和新形成的细节独有正确决策相关。

Conclusion: 零样本提示质量无法完全代理适应锚点质量；评估语言描述时应同时考虑适应前和适应后的表现。这一发现挑战了现有SF-CDFSL中仅凭零样本准确率选择描述的做法，并提示需要更全面的语言评价方法。

Abstract: Language descriptions in source-free cross-domain few-shot learning (SF-CDFSL) are often selected according to zero-shot accuracy obtained with a frozen vision--language model. This paper asks whether that ranking remains valid after target-domain visual adaptation. Under a strictly paired protocol, we compare a generic class-name template with fixed detailed class descriptions before and after visual Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) on EuroSAT, CropDisease, ISIC, and ChestX. Let $\deltazero$ and $\deltalora$ denote the Detailed-minus-Base accuracy before and after adaptation, respectively. Two recurring regimes emerge. In \emph{semantic saturation}, $\deltazero>0$ but $0<\deltalora\ll\deltazero$: on EuroSAT and CropDisease, initial gains of 8.13--21.54 percentage points contract to 0.69--2.96 points after LoRA. In \emph{semantic emergence}, $\deltazero\leq0$ but $\deltalora>0$: on ISIC and ChestX, detailed descriptions become more useful only after the visual representation is updated. Training trajectories and sample-level decomposition show that saturation is driven mainly by Base-LoRA recovering errors already solved by detailed semantics, whereas emergence is associated with prediction turnover and newly formed Detailed-only correct decisions. Fixed-point-free shuffled-semantic controls, a second CLIP backbone, and multiple random seeds support the broad pattern while identifying ChestX 1-shot as a weak boundary case. These findings establish that zero-shot prompt quality is an incomplete proxy for adaptation-anchor quality and motivate evaluating language on both sides of the adaptation boundary.

</details>


### [12] [Corrupting Attention: Evasion-Based Adversarial Attacks on Encoder Attention in Detection Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06674)
*Ridma Jayasundara,Shaheer Mohamed,Tharindu Fernando,Harshala Gammulle,Basura Fernando,Sanka Rasnayake,A V Subramanyam,Sridha Sridharan,Clinton Fookes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次提出直接优化编码器注意力目标的不受限ℓ∞扰动攻击，通过破坏检测变换器（DETR）的注意力结构而非仅攻击检测输出，在COCO上将DETR-R50的mAP从42.1降至0.97（相比现有最强攻击提升约4倍），并证明该攻击对多种注意力形式和目标函数均有效，在DINO-Swin-L上也实现56.8→1.44的mAP下降，达到密集与可变形注意力场景下的新最优水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗攻击大多针对目标检测的输出而非驱动检测变换器模型独特性的注意力机制，且已有攻击通过可见补丁引入攻击者所有的sink token，存在局限性。作者希望探索直接破坏编码器注意力结构的攻击方法，揭示这一核心组件的脆弱性。

Method: 提出一种直接优化编码器注意力目标的不受限ℓ∞扰动攻击，区别于引入sink token的方式，该攻击驱动模型自身的注意力朝向一个被破坏的目标，而非通过可见补丁引入外部token。攻击通过扰乱编码器注意力（模型空间推理的核心），使破坏沿检测流水线传播。

Result: 在COCO数据集上，DETR-R50的mAP从42.1降至0.97，相比现有最强攻击在相同扰动预算和迭代次数下mAP降低约4倍。在DINO-Swin-L上mAP从56.8降至1.44（最强现有攻击为7.3）。四种不同的损坏目标（分散、重排序、置换、峰值抑制）均将检测降至3 mAP以下，表明脆弱性源于注意力结构本身被破坏而非特定目标函数。

Conclusion: 注意力机制是检测变换器的核心脆弱点，直接攻击编码器注意力比攻击检测输出更有效；该攻击方法具有通用性，在不同注意力形式（密集注意力和可变形注意力）上均有效；注意力结构的破坏是导致检测性能崩溃的根本原因。

Abstract: Adversarial vulnerabilities remain a major concern for the safe deployment of neural networks, particularly in object detection, a core task embedded in many safety-critical systems. Detection transformers have emerged as leading object detectors, yet their adversarial robustness remains comparatively underexplored. Most existing attacks target the detection output rather than the attention mechanism that makes these models distinctive. In this paper, we introduce the first attack that directly optimizes an encoder-attention objective under an imperceptible, bounded $\ell_\infty$ perturbation. Rather than introducing an attacker-owned sink token through a visible patch, it drives the model's own attention toward a corrupted target. We argue that encoder attention concentrates the model's spatial reasoning, so corrupting it propagates through the detection pipeline more disruptively than perturbing the detection output alone. Our attack reduces DETR-R50 mAP on COCO from 42.1 to 0.97, a $\sim 4\times$ reduction in resulting mAP over the strongest existing attack under an identical perturbation budget and iteration count. We further show that this vulnerability is not specific to a particular corruption objective: across four qualitatively distinct targets, dispersion, re-ranking, permutation, and peak-suppression, detection consistently drops below 3 mAP, suggesting that the weakness arises from disrupting the attention structure itself rather than from any single target. Finally, we demonstrate that the attack generalizes across attention formulations, reducing DINO-Swin-L from 56.8 to 1.44 mAP against 7.3 for the strongest prior attack, establishing state-of-the-art on both dense and deformable attention.

</details>


### [13] [CoDAT: Collaborative Dual-Attention Transformer with Low-Cost Temporal Modeling for Efficient Edge Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06691)
*Novendra Setyawan,Chi-Chia Sun,Mao-Hsiu Hsu,Wen-Kai Kuo,Jing-Ming Guo,Jun-Wei Hsieh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoDAT is a lightweight Collaborative Dual-Attention Transformer that replaces conventional multi-head attention with a dual-branch module (SCA for local aggregation, SSHA for global context) plus a parameter-free TShift for temporal communication, achieving competitive real-time action recognition on IoT edge devices with significant speedups and reduced computational cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-time human action recognition on IoT edge devices requires efficient spatio-temporal modeling under strict latency, memory, and power constraints. Existing 3D CNNs, video transformers, and shift-based ViTs deliver high accuracy but are too computationally expensive for edge IoT deployment.

Method: CoDAT replaces conventional multi-head attention with a lightweight dual-branch module: Spatial Convolutional Attention (SCA) for local spatial aggregation and Strided Single-Head Attention (SSHA) for global context. SSHA compresses spatial resolution and channel dimensions of Q, K, V via stride-based sparse projection, then fuses global and local features at reduced cost. A parameter-free TShift module embedded in each block enables temporal communication across frames.

Result: On Jetson AGX Orin and Raspberry Pi 5, CoDAT achieves energy-accuracy balance. On ImageNet-1K, CoDAT-M runs 2x faster than EfficientViT384 and FastViT-S12 at comparable accuracy; CoDAT-L matches ViT-S with 3x fewer parameters and 2x higher throughput. On Kinetics-400 and MA-52, CoDAT achieves competitive Top-1 accuracy while running up to 2.9x faster than VSwin-T, 2x faster than ViT-Temporal-Shift variants, and 5x faster than UniFormer-B. On UCF-101, CoDAT-S384 matches TokShift and LAPS while being 6x faster and requiring up to 13x fewer FLOPs.

Conclusion: CoDAT establishes an efficiency-accuracy balance suitable for real-time action recognition in edge IoT perception systems, demonstrating that carefully designed lightweight attention mechanisms can replace heavy multi-head attention for resource-constrained deployment.

Abstract: Real-time human action recognition on Internet-of-Things (IoT) edge devices requires models that capture rich spatio-temporal cues within strict latency, memory, and power envelopes. Current 3D CNNs, video transformers, and shift-based ViT deliver high accuracy but come at computational costs that preclude edge IoT deployment. This paper proposes CoDAT, a Collaborative Dual-Attention Transformer that replaces conventional multi-head attention with a lightweight dual-branch module: Spatial Convolutional Attention (SCA) for local aggregation and Strided Single-Head Attention (SSHA) for global context. SSHA jointly compresses the spatial resolution and channel dimensions of the query, key, and value tensors via stride-based sparse projection, then fuses the resulting global and local features at a markedly reduced cost. To enable temporal communication across frames, a parameter-free TShift module is embedded in each block. Extensive experiments on Jetson AGX Orin and Raspberry Pi 5 demonstrate that CoDAT achieves an energy-accuracy balance in both image and action recognition. On ImageNet-1K, CoDAT-M runs 2x faster than EfficientViT384 and FastViT-S12 at comparable accuracy, and CoDAT-L matches ViT-S with 3x fewer parameters at 2x higher throughput. On Kinetics-400 and MA-52, CoDAT achieves competitive Top-1 accuracy against state-of-the-art CNN, transformer, and hybrid baselines while running up to 2.9x faster than VSwin-T, 2x faster than ViT-Temporal-Shift variants, and 5x faster than UniFormer-B. On UCF-101, CoDAT-S384 matches TokShift and LAPS while being 6x faster and requiring up to 13x fewer FLOPs, establishing an efficiency-accuracy balance for real-time action recognition in edge IoT perception systems. Code is available at https://github.com/novendrastywn/CoDAT .

</details>


### [14] [Suppress and Diversify: Refining Robust Pathways for Corruption Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06712)
*Jiangang Yang,Wenhui Shi,Xiaoran Xu,Wenyue Chong,Luqing Luo,Jing Xing,Jian Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Suppress and Diversify (S&D)的非侵入式精炼方法，通过动态选择鲁棒通路并利用对称保持变换进行多样化，以增强模型对自然图像损坏的鲁棒性。该方法与架构无关、无需参数且测试时零开销，在多个基准测试上一致提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注隐式表示学习来提升模型鲁棒性，但缺乏对内部鲁棒性的显式表征研究。作者希望系统性地探索计算路径以显式刻画内部鲁棒性，从而更好地理解并提升模型对自然图像损坏的抵抗力。

Method: S&D方法包含两个核心步骤：(1) 动态选择鲁棒通路——识别并保留网络中表现出鲁棒特征的计算路径；(2) 通过对称保持变换进行多样化——对选中的鲁棒通路施加对称性保持的变换以增加多样性。该方法是非侵入式的（不需要修改原网络架构）、无需参数、且在测试阶段零额外开销。

Result: 在八个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，S&D在多种视觉任务、不同骨干网络以及复杂真实场景中持续提升了模型性能，验证了其广泛的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 本文首次系统地探索了显式表征内部鲁棒性的计算途径，发现了鲁棒特征在网络层间逐渐衰减的现象以及其与模型性能的函数依赖关系。基于这些洞察提出的S&D方法为实现非侵入式的鲁棒性增强提供了有效且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Model robustness against natural image corruptions is essential for safety-critical applications. While existing methods primarily focus on implicit representation learning, we provide the first systematic exploration of computational pathways to explicitly characterize internal robustness. We identify a progressive decay of robust features across network layers and establish a functional dependency between the prevalence of these features and model performance. To exploit these insights, we propose Suppress and Diversify (S\&D), a non-intrusive refinement approach that enhances robustness by dynamically selecting robust pathways and diversifying them through symmetry-preserving transformations. S\&D is architecture-agnostic, parameter-free, and incurs zero test-time overhead. Extensive evaluations across eight benchmarks demonstrate that S\&D consistently improves performance across multiple vision tasks, diverse backbones, and complex real-world scenarios, highlighting its broad efficacy and scalability.

</details>


### [15] [WaveFreqAnchor: Wave-Structural Anchoring and Frequency Correction Diffusion for Training-Free Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06717)
*Zelin Du,Wenjie Li,Zhengxue Wang,Juncheng Li,Cailing Wang,Guangwei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 WaveFreqAnchor，一种无需训练的面部修复框架，通过波结构锚定（ASWG）和多尺度小波-傅里叶注入（MWFI）在反向扩散过程中约束面部结构并修正频率不一致性，同时引入子带高频增强（SHE）处理真实场景中的复杂退化，从而在保持身份一致性的同时恢复清晰逼真的面部细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的面部修复方法在反向扩散过程中提供的约束不足，导致在严重退化下出现身份相关的结构漂移和保真度下降，亟需一种能有效保持面部结构一致性的修复方法。

Method: 提出三部分组成的训练无关框架：1) Anchor-Space Wave-Structural Guidance (ASWG) 通过各向异性波响应一致性约束面部结构；2) Multi-scale Wavelet-Fourier Injection (MWFI) 通过替换预测低频子带的相位来对齐观测数据，修正反向扩散中累积的不一致性；3) Subband High-Frequency Enhancement (SHE) 对预测的高频子带进行有界、空间掩蔽的细化，恢复真实场景下复杂退化中的精细面部细节。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在面部修复质量上持续优于现有方法，实现了高质量、高保真度的面部修复效果。

Conclusion: 所提出的 ASWG、MWFI 和 SHE 三种设计协同作用，能够有效保留面部身份特征，同时恢复锐利且真实的面部细节，为训练无关的扩散修复提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion-based face restoration that adjusts the sampling trajectory of pre-trained diffusion models has achieved remarkable progress. However, existing approaches provide insufficient constraints during reverse diffusion, causing identity-related structural drift and degraded fidelity under severe degradations. To address this, we propose WaveFreqAnchor, a training-free framework based on Wave-Structural Anchoring and Frequency Correction Diffusion. Specifically, Anchor-Space Wave-Structural Guidance (ASWG) constrains facial structures through anisotropic wave-response consistency, while Multi-scale Wavelet-Fourier Injection (MWFI) aligns the predicted low-frequency subband with the observation by replacing its phase, correcting inconsistencies accumulated during reverse diffusion. For real-world scenes, we further introduce Subband High-Frequency Enhancement (SHE), which performs bounded, spatially masked refinement on the predicted high-frequency subbands to recover fine facial details under unknown compound degradations. Together, these designs effectively preserve facial identity while restoring sharp and realistic facial details. Extensive experiments show that our method consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving high-quality and high-fidelity face restoration.

</details>


### [16] [Beyond Starry Night: Shortcut-Aware Control-State Planning for Artist-Grounded Text to Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06751)
*Kuan Xing,Ye Wang,Changyi Gan,Yuheng Li,Thao Nguyen,Yi Chang,Yilin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Atelier 框架，通过显式控制状态规划解决艺术家风格图像生成中的"捷径替代"问题（模型用刻板印象替代用户场景），并在新基准 ArtIntentBench 上验证其优于提示工程、检索增强等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成模型对艺术家名称的响应存在"捷径"行为——即倾向于重复使用该艺术家的典型 motif、通用调色板或过度代表性的时期签名，而非忠实保留用户预期的场景构图。这揭示了艺术家风格生成不仅受限于底层图像合成能力，更受限于缺乏显式的、基于证据的艺术控制信号的上游推理。

Method: Atelier 是一个"捷径感知"的控制状态规划框架，核心步骤包括：(1) 将不明确的艺术意图翻译为显式控制状态，包含场景锚点、保留/变换决策、风格体制假设、角色绑定的艺术家证据和捷径避免约束；(2) 利用艺术家级别知识和局部面片参考来ground该状态；(3) 编译后端感知的生成计划；(4) 通过全局和局部真实性反馈迭代优化候选结果。

Result: 在梵高和齐白石两个艺术家上，覆盖作品重渲染、时期/风格控制生成、历史未见主题、捷径审计和人类偏好评估五个任务。相比提示工程、检索增强和通用智能体基线，Atelier 在艺术家级别风格保真度、源结构保持上更优，并显著减少了捷径替代现象。

Conclusion: 艺术家风格图像生成的瓶颈不仅在于图像合成技术本身，更在于上游推理环节——即如何将模糊的艺术意图转化为显式的、基于证据的艺术控制信号。Atelier 通过显式控制状态规划证明了这一方向的有效性。

Abstract: Artist-grounded image generation requires more than appending an artist name to a prompt. Image models often respond to artist names through canonical shortcuts, such as recurring motifs, generic palettes, or overrepresented period signatures, rather than preserving the user's intended scene. We introduce Atelier, a shortcut-aware control-state planning framework for artist-grounded image generation. Atelier translates underspecified artistic intent into an explicit control state that separates scene anchors, preserve/transform decisions, style-regime hypotheses, role-bound artist evidence, and shortcut-avoidance constraints. It grounds this state using artist-level knowledge and local patch references, compiles backend-aware generation plans, and iteratively refines candidates through global and local authenticity feedback. We further introduce ArtIntentBench, a benchmark covering Van Gogh and Qi Baishi across artwork re-rendering, period/style-controlled generation, historically unseen subjects, shortcut auditing, and human preference evaluation. Across open-weight and closed-source generators, Atelier improves artist-level style fidelity, preserves source structure more faithfully, and substantially reduces shortcut substitution compared with prompt-engineered, retrieval-augmented, and general-purpose agent baselines. These results suggest that artist-grounded generation is bottlenecked not only by image synthesis, but by the upstream inference of explicit, evidence-grounded artistic controls.

</details>


### [17] [Explore or Converge? Stage-Guided Per-Step Optimization for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06768)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出阶段引导的每步优化方法（SGPO），通过联合利用信噪比和语义变化识别扩散模型的生成阶段（早、中、晚期），并为每个阶段分配针对性的优化目标，解决了现有强化学习方法因奖励回填导致的时序目标不匹配和奖励黑客问题，显著提升了生成质量和收敛速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的最大似然训练难以对齐特定偏好，而强化学习用于偏好对齐虽前景广阔但受限于奖励稀疏。现有RL方法将最终奖励反向传播至所有去噪步骤，忽略了去噪过程的阶段语义差异和可控性变化，导致时序目标不匹配、奖励捷径与奖励黑客；同时每步接收相同奖励使得动作难以区分，弱化了优化过程。

Method: 提出SGPO方法，联合利用信噪比与语义变化自动识别扩散模型去噪的三个阶段，并为各阶段自适应分配特定目标：早期混沌状态远离最终奖励，优先退出混沌；中期潜在结构趋于稳定，优化最终奖励并探索多样性以避早熟；后期核心结构基本固定，偏好优化主要放大局部细节，故侧重稳定收敛以防止过拟合和质量下降。

Result: 在16项对比实验中，SGPO在生成质量上平均提升26.7%，收敛速度提升36.7%，验证了方法的有效性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: SGPO通过阶段感知的逐步优化，有效解决了扩散模型偏好对齐中的奖励稀疏与目标不匹配问题，在提升生成质量和加速收敛方面均取得显著优势，为扩散模型的偏好对齐提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have strong generative capabilities. However, their maximum likelihood training objective only focuses on reconstructing the data distribution, making it difficult to align with specific preferences. Reinforcement learning (RL) for preference alignment in diffusion models is promising but limited by reward sparsity. Since a single reward cannot support optimization, existing RL methods usually backpropagate the final reward to all previous steps. However, denoising is stage-wise, with distinct semantics and controllability. Repeating the final reward across all steps creates a temporal objective mismatch, encouraging reward shortcuts that lead to reward hacking. At the same time, due to reward backfilling, each time step receives the same reward, making it impossible to distinguish between actions, thereby weakening the optimization process.
  To resolve this issue, we propose Stage-Guided Per-Step Optimization (SGPO) for diffusion models, which jointly leverages signal-to-noise ratio and semantic changes to identify generation stages and adaptively assign stage-specific objectives. Early denoising is chaotic and far from the final reward, resulting in weak reward-behavior correlation. This stage should prioritize exiting the chaotic state. In the mid stage, the latent transitions to a stable structure, where the final reward better corresponds to generative behavior. Therefore, this stage optimizes the final reward while exploring diversity to avoid early convergence to a single mode.
  In the late stage, the latent's core structure is largely fixed, and preference optimization mainly amplifies local details, risking overfitting. Therefore, stable convergence is preferred to avoid quality degradation. Results from 16 comparative experiments validate SGPO. Our method achieves 26.7% average gains in generative quality and 36.7% higher convergence speed.

</details>


### [18] [GraphVerse: A Comprehensive Visual Graph Reasoning Benchmark for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06769)
*Yuanfu Sun,Yuanhang Ren,Kang Li,Chuanhao Ji,Jiaxi Li,Jiajin Liu,Ninghao Liu,Qiaoyu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GraphVerse 是一个统一的多模态基准，用于评估大语言模型在视觉图推理（VGR）中的感知、视觉推理和基于文本的图推理能力，涵盖单图像和配对图像设置。它通过图中心图像编辑（GIE）策略生成主动测试视觉推理的样本，并引入过程敏感指标 VGR-Score 来评估推理质量。实验揭示了当前 MLLM 在 VGR 中的关键局限，验证了 GIE 策略的有效性以及 GraphVerse 向更广泛多模态推理能力的可迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉图推理（VGR）基准将任务简化为视觉感知后接文本推理，局限于单图像设置，仅使用答案准确性指标，且缺乏现实世界的图中心场景。因此需要更全面的基准来评估 MLLM 在结构化视觉信息上的推理能力。

Method: 提出 GraphVerse 基准，包含：(1) 多维度评估——联合评估感知、视觉推理和基于文本的图推理；(2) 单图像和配对图像双设置；(3) 图中心图像编辑（GIE）策略，修改图图像的同时保留语义，作为视觉推理的主动测试；(4) VGR-Score 过程敏感指标，超越仅关注最终答案准确性的评估方式。

Result: 实验揭示了当前 MLLM 在 VGR 中的多个关键局限性，验证了 GIE 策略的有效性，并证明了 GraphVerse 向更广泛多模态推理能力的可迁移性。

Conclusion: GraphVerse 为 MLLM 的视觉图推理能力提供了更全面、更具挑战性的评估框架，推动了该领域基准的发展。

Abstract: Recent Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable progress across diverse vision-language tasks, creating an urgent need for more challenging benchmarks. Yet existing evaluations still provide limited insight into whether these models can truly reason over structured visual information. Visual Graph Reasoning (VGR) offers a compelling testbed for this challenge, requiring models to integrate perception, structural understanding, and multi-step reasoning over graph-based visual inputs. However, prior VGR benchmarks often reduce the task to visual perception followed by text-based reasoning, restrict evaluation to single-image settings, rely on answer-only metrics, and underrepresent realistic graph-centric scenarios. To bridge the gap, we introduce GraphVerse, a unified benchmark that jointly evaluates perception, visual reasoning, and text-based graph reasoning in MLLMs under both single-image and paired-image settings. At its core is a suite of Graph-centric Image Editing (GIE) strategies that modify graph images while preserving their semantics, turning them into active tests of visual reasoning. We further propose VGR-Score, a process-sensitive metric that evaluates reasoning quality beyond final-answer accuracy. Extensive experiments reveal several key limitations of current MLLMs in VGR, while also validating the effectiveness of GIE strategies and the transferability of GraphVerse to broader multimodal reasoning capabilities. The code is available at https://github.com/sunyuanfu/GraphVerse.

</details>


### [19] [AnyTrack: Unifying Visual Object Tracking with Any Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06773)
*Hao Li,Yunzhi Zhuge,Wenning Hao,Pingping Zhang,Xiaoxiong Zhang,Dong Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AnyTrack提出了一种统一的多模态目标跟踪框架，通过模态感知交互模块（MIM）和上下文理解模块（CUM）支持任意模态组合（包括灰度图像、语言描述、音频等），在完整和缺失模态设置下均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态跟踪器通常针对固定的模态组合设计，需要为不同输入训练独立模型，导致对缺失或不完美模态的适应性差且泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出AnyTrack统一框架：1) 模态感知交互模块（MIM）实现跨模态动态交互，弥合模态差异并聚合时序线索以保持时空一致性；2) 上下文理解模块（CUM）通过全局-局部提示建立视觉特征与目标位置间的空间对应关系，利用目标感知上下文增强前景-背景判别；3) 扩展现有基准，纳入灰度图、语言描述和音频以支持多模态训练与评估。

Result: 在完整和缺失模态设置下的广泛实验表明，AnyTrack达到了最先进的性能，验证了其有效性和灵活性。

Conclusion: AnyTrack通过统一框架有效解决了多模态跟踪中模态固定组合的局限性，支持任意模态输入，具有良好的适应性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual object tracking aims to continuously locate specific targets within sequential frames, evolving from single-modal methods to multi-modal ones. However, existing multi-modal trackers are typically designed for fixed modality combinations, requiring separate models for different inputs. This leads to a poor adaptability to missing or imperfect modalities, and limited generalization. To address these issues, we propose a novel unified framework called AnyTrack for object tracking with any modalities. Specifically, we design a Modality-aware Interaction Module (MIM) to facilitate dynamic interaction across diverse modalities. This module bridges modality discrepancies and aggregates temporal cues to maintain spatio-temporal consistency during cross-modal interaction. Furthermore, we introduce a Context Understanding Module (CUM) to establish spatial correspondence between visual features and target locations via global-local prompts. This module employs target-aware context modeling to enhance foreground-background discrimination for precise localization. Finally, to support the training and evaluation under diverse modalities, we extend existing multi-modal object tracking benchmarks by incorporating grayscale images, language descriptions, and audio clips. Extensive experiments with both complete and missing modality settings demonstrate that our AnyTrack achieves state-of-the-art performance, validating its effectiveness and flexibility. The source code is available at https://github.com/IdolLab/AnyTrack.

</details>


### [20] [UniCycleFlow: Bidirectional Unpaired Image Translation with a Shared Rectified Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06784)
*Xianhao Zhou,Jianghao Wu,Shaoting Zhang,Guotai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniCycleFlow，一种基于整流流（rectified flow）的框架，将双向无配对图像翻译建模为单个时间条件速度场的正向和反向积分，通过对抗匹配、自流匹配、离散循环闭合和路径速度正则化实现无需配对数据的双向一致变换，在10个翻译方向上以单步Euler采样取得最佳平均FID 55.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双向无配对图像翻译方法通常使用两个方向独立的生成器或单向模型，即使通过循环一致性约束也只保证往返端点重建，而不要求两个方向服从共同的局部变换规则，缺乏对双向翻译内在一致性的建模。

Method: 将双向翻译表示为单个时间条件速度场的正向和反向积分，使两个方向共享同一连续动力学。针对无配对数据无法构造整流流轨迹的问题，学习确定性的源条件端点并通过对抗匹配使其边缘分布与目标域一致；路径通过停止梯度的自流匹配提供中间速度监督、离散循环闭合保证前向-反向一致性、表示路径速度正则化控制局部特征变化。

Result: 在10个翻译方向中，UniCycleFlow使用单步Euler评估在7个任务上取得最低FID，平均FID达55.1，优于现有方法。

Conclusion: UniCycleFlow通过统一的整流流框架有效解决了双向无配对图像翻译中的一致性问题，为无需配对数据的双向图像翻译提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Bidirectional unpaired image translation must preserve source-specific structure while learning coherent transformations in both directions without paired supervision. Existing methods typically employ two direction-specific generators or train separate one-way models. Even when linked by cycle consistency, such models constrain only the round-trip endpoint reconstruction, without requiring the two directions to obey a common local transformation rule. We propose UniCycleFlow, a rectified-flow framework that represents bidirectional translation as forward and reverse integration of a single time-conditioned velocity field. This formulation organizes both directions within the same continuous dynamics, rather than coupling otherwise separate endpoint mappings. A key challenge is that unpaired data provide no meaningful source--target coupling from which rectified-flow trajectories can be constructed. UniCycleFlow addresses this challenge by learning deterministic source-conditioned endpoints whose marginal distributions are adversarially matched to the opposite domains. The resulting paths are regularized by stop-gradient self-flow matching for intermediate velocity supervision, discrete cycle closure for forward--reverse consistency, and representation path-velocity regularization for controlling localized feature changes along the trajectory. Across ten translation directions, UniCycleFlow achieves the lowest FID on 7 of 10 tasks using a single Euler evaluation and obtains the best average FID of 55.1.

</details>


### [21] [PAST: Prompt-Adaptive Sampling Termination for Efficient Diffusion Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06794)
*Renye Yan,Jikang Cheng,You Wu,Wei Peng,Zongwei Wang,Ling Liang,Yimao Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAST方法通过内在奖励范式、自适应训练终止和双自适应协调机制，解决了扩散模型在文本到图像任务中RL微调效率低和奖励稀疏的问题，计算效率提升高达66.7%，偏好优化质量提升高达29.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在文本到图像任务中直接优化下游目标时存在局限性；现有的强化学习方法受限于低效微调和稀疏奖励，难以高效指导模型探索最优路径。

Method: 提出PAST方法：1)设计内在奖励范式补偿稀疏的外在奖励，并引导模型高效偏离噪声模式；2)动态监测去噪完成度和语义对齐度，自适应终止训练以合理分配回合长度；3)基于预测残差噪声水平建立双自适应协调机制，平衡内外奖励以及探索与收敛。

Result: 相比现有RL微调方法，PAST将计算效率提升高达66.7%，偏好优化质量提升高达29.5%。

Conclusion: PAST通过感知去噪进度和提示难度的自适应调控机制，有效提升了RL微调扩散模型的效率和生成质量，为文本到图像任务提供了更高效的优化方法。

Abstract: While diffusion models have made significant progress in text-to-image tasks, they still exhibit limitations when directly optimizing downstream objectives. Although Reinforcement Learning (RL) enables targeted optimization, existing methods are generally constrained by low-efficiency fine-tuning and sparse rewards. To address these challenges, we propose PAST, which provides differentiated rewards while adaptively regulating training episode length by jointly perceiving denoising progress and prompt difficulty. Specifically, we design an intrinsic reward paradigm to compensate for sparse extrinsic rewards and guide the model to explore paths that diverge more efficiently from noise patterns. We further provide theoretical justification for intrinsic rewards. Then, PAST dynamically monitors denoising completion and semantic alignment between image structures and prompt semantics. When both metrics satisfy generation requirements, the system adaptively terminates training. This enables appropriate allocation of episode lengths based on prompt difficulty and the current generation process. Finally, based on the predicted residual noise level, we establish a dual adaptive coordination mechanism. Specifically, it not only balances the extrinsic and intrinsic rewards but also balances the exploration and convergence. Experimental results demonstrate that PAST enhances computational efficiency of existing RL fine-tuning methods by up to 66.7%, while improving preference optimization quality by up to 29.5% through its dual adaptive regulation mechanism.

</details>


### [22] [AdvTiles: Physical Adversarial Camouflage Clothing against Person Detectors via Learnable Tiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06801)
*Jinlei Wang,Jiahuan Long,Mingkai Sun,Yafei Guo,Yuanhao Huang,Ming Wang,Junqi Wu,Jiacheng Hou,Hongbo Chen,Xingxing Wei,Tingsong Jiang,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AdvTiles，一种基于可学习图块的物理对抗伪装框架，通过ST Gumbel-Softmax实现图块图案与空间布局的联合优化，并结合3D高斯泼溅渲染增强物理鲁棒性，在多个检测器上平均攻击成功率86.2%，优于现有方法，并制成可穿戴衣物在真实场景中验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理对抗攻击方法难以同时兼顾视觉自然性和攻击有效性；整体优化伪装纹理的方式缺乏对局部对抗模式及其空间排列的精细控制，限制了攻击性能与自然外观的平衡。

Method: 提出AdvTiles框架，使用可学习图块作为基本单元，通过Straight-through Gumbel-Softmax估计器实现图块的差异化选择，联合优化图块图案和空间布局；进一步利用可微3D高斯泼溅渲染模拟视角、尺度、光照和背景变化，增强物理环境鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个检测器上平均攻击成功率达86.2%，超越现有最先进方法；实际制作的对抗衣物在真实场景（不同距离、角度、背景）中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: AdvTiles通过图块级精细控制实现了自然外观与强攻击性的统一，为物理对抗伪装提供了新范式，并展示了从数字优化到真实部署的可行性。

Abstract: Physical adversarial attacks against person detectors have evolved from localized patches to full-body textures. However, achieving both visual naturalness and strong attack effectiveness remains challenging. Existing natural-looking methods typically optimize camouflage textures as a whole, limiting the flexibility to refine local adversarial patterns and their spatial arrangement. To address this issue, we propose AdvTiles, a physical adversarial camouflage framework built from learnable tiles, enabling strong attack performance while preserving a natural camouflage appearance. Specifically, we use a Straight-through (ST) Gumbel-Softmax estimator for differentiable tile selection, enabling joint optimization of tile patterns and spatial layouts. This design provides fine-grained control over adversarial texture generation. To improve robustness in diverse physical conditions, we further optimize the camouflage through differentiable 3D Gaussian Splatting rendering with variations in viewpoints, scales, illuminations and backgrounds. Extensive experiments across multiple detectors demonstrate that AdvTiles achieves an average ASR of 86.2%, outperforming existing state-of-the-art attack methods. We further fabricate the optimized camouflage into wearable adversarial clothing, validating its effectiveness in real-world scenarios across diverse distances, angles and backgrounds.

</details>


### [23] [Bend the Basics: Degradation-Aware Deformable Tokenization for All-in-One Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06832)
*Zihao He,Yunfeng Wu,Xinchao Wang,Songhua Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Flexible Image Transformer (FIT)，通过在整个流水线（从 patch 采样到像素重建）中显式建模退化感知，并使用轻量级退化编码器预测全局退化向量和空间退化图来指导自适应形变，在五个标准基准上达到 SOTA 性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一 Transformer 依赖固定的 patch 划分，任务/退化条件仅在 token 化后注入主干块，而嵌入和重建阶段对局部退化变化不敏感。

Method: FIT 采用轻量级退化编码器从局部退化严重度预测全局退化向量 g 和空间退化图 M，通过自适应形变联合调节 patch 嵌入与解嵌入；并引入任务 token dropout 策略以增强对不同退化类型的鲁棒性。

Result: 在 BSD68、Rain100L、SOTS、GoPro、LOLv1 五个基准上，五退化设置平均 PSNR 30.72 dB，三退化设置 32.83 dB，超过近期统一恢复方法 0.5~1.1 dB。

Conclusion: 学习到的偏移量为可视化退化感知的空间自适应提供了直接途径。

Abstract: All-in-one image restoration seeks a single model that can recover images degraded by diverse and spatially non-uniform corruptions. However, many unified Transformers rely on fixed patch partitioning: task/degradation condition is injected only into the backbone blocks after tokenization, leaving the embedding and reconstruction stages insensitive to local degradation variations. In contrast to previous approaches, we present Flexible Image Transformer (FIT) that explicitly models degradation awareness across the entire pipeline, from patch sampling to pixel reconstruction. Specifically, FIT employs a lightweight Degradation Encoder to predict a global degradation vector $\mathbf{g}$ and a spatial degradation map $\mathbf{M}$ from local degradation severity, which jointly condition the patch embedding and unembedding through adaptive deformation. Moreover, to improve robustness across degradation types, we introduce a task-token dropout strategy that regularizes task conditioning during training. On five standard benchmarks (BSD68, Rain100L, SOTS, GoPro, and LOLv1), FIT achieves state-of-the-art performance with 30.72 dB average PSNR on the five-degradation setting and 32.83 dB on the three-degradation setting, outperforming recent unified restoration methods by +0.5$\sim$1.1 dB. Moreover, the learned offsets provide a direct handle for visualizing degradation-aware spatial adaptation.

</details>


### [24] [GOPI: Generation-Oriented 3D Pose Inference for Furniture Insertion from Single-View RGB-D Indoor Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06836)
*Ruifeng Zhai,Renjie Liu,Guangrun Wang,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种两阶段框架，先通过数据驱动的迭代推理（GOPI）估计单视图场景中新家具的3D位姿，再通过几何引导的条件生成策略将3D位姿投影到图像平面作为像素对齐约束，从而实现物理合理的家具插入与图像合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在单视图图像中插入新家具时，由于缺乏深度和尺度信息，物理尺度与3D放置位置无法唯一确定，导致现有方法难以生成几何上合理的插入结果。作者将任务重新定义为3D位姿推理与几何引导图像生成的组合，强调可靠的合成依赖于可解释的3D几何先验。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：(1) 3D位姿推理阶段——GOPI（面向生成的3D位姿推理），通过数据驱动的迭代推断解决单视图家具插入的不确定性问题，产生几何上合理的目标放置；(2) 图像生成阶段——几何引导的条件生成策略，将推断出的3D位姿投影到图像平面作为像素级约束，保证合成图像与底层3D几何的一致性。

Result: 实验从3D位姿估计和图像合成两个角度验证：在3D放置上，GOPI比直接回归和普通基线方法产生几何可行性更强、与参考布局一致性更好的位姿；在图像合成上，该方法在不同家具尺度下均能保持与投影3D几何的对齐，显示出稳定的投影-生成一致性。

Conclusion: 通过将单视图家具插入分解为显式3D位姿推理与几何引导生成两个子任务，有效解决了物理尺度不确定性问题，证明了利用3D几何先验指导2D图像合成是可行的技术路线。

Abstract: We study the problem of inserting new furniture into indoor scene images. Under masked single-view 2D image-plane conditioning, however, the physical scale of the inserted furniture relative to the scene cannot be uniquely determined, making physically grounded furniture placement underdetermined from image evidence alone. We therefore reformulate the task as a combination of 3D pose inference and geometry-guided image generation, where estimating a geometrically plausible 3D placement is essential for reliable synthesis.
  To this end, we propose a two-stage framework. For 3D placement, we introduce GOPI, a generation-oriented 3D pose inference framework that addresses the underdetermined nature of single-view furniture insertion through data-driven iterative inference, producing geometrically plausible object placements. For image generation, we develop a geometry-guided conditioning strategy that projects the inferred 3D pose into the image plane as a pixel-aligned constraint, enforcing consistency between the synthesized image and the underlying 3D geometry.
  Experimental results validate the proposed framework from both 3D pose estimation and image synthesis perspectives. For 3D placement, GOPI produces poses with stronger geometric feasibility and better consistency with reference layouts than direct regression and vanilla baselines. For image synthesis, our method preserves alignment with the projected 3D geometry across different furniture scales, showing stable projection-generation alignment across the tested furniture scales.

</details>


### [25] [ECAD: Expanding Class-Agnostic Detection Beyond Thing-Centric Objectness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06841)
*Liang Wan,Zixin Ren,Yupeng Zhang,Yuhan Wang,Fangzhuo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出扩展类别无关检测(ECAD)新任务，旨在发现超越传统物体中心视角的视觉元素（如天空、道路等），并构建BTCO-Bench基准，提出ECADet检测器，通过几何感知专家回归和原型引导查询调制，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测范式受限于"物体中心"的物体性概念，主要训练检测器定位离散、可计数的物体实例，导致许多语义上有意义的视觉元素（如天空、道路、草地、水面、运动场等）被归为背景，但这些元素对场景理解和空间推理至关重要。

Method: 1) 提出ECAD（扩展类别无关检测）新任务；2) 构建BTCO-Bench基准，包含超越物体中心的类别无关框标注，覆盖真实世界和跨域场景；3) 提出ECADet，一种轻量级基于DETR的检测器，使用冻结的DINOv3编码器，引入几何感知专家回归（GAER）和原型引导查询调制（PGQM）以改进定位和物体性估计。

Result: 在BTCO-Bench上，ECADet持续优于代表性类别无关检测器和基于提议的检测器，展示了扩展物体性发现的有效性。

Conclusion: ECADet在扩展物体性发现任务上表现优异，代码和基准将开源。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental task in visual perception, providing structured region representations for recognition, grounding, reasoning, and interaction. However, existing detection paradigms largely inherit a thing-centric notion of objectness, where detectors are mainly trained to localize discrete and countable object instances. Consequently, many semantically meaningful visual elements, such as sky, road, grassland, water, and sports courts, are often absorbed into the background despite their importance for scene understanding and spatial reasoning. In this paper, we formulate Expanded Class-Agnostic Detection (ECAD), a new setting that aims to discover category-agnostic visual candidates beyond conventional thing-centric objects. To support this setting, we construct BTCO-Bench, a Beyond Thing-Centric Objectness benchmark with category-agnostic box annotations covering both real-world and cross-domain scenarios. We further propose ECADet, a lightweight DETR-based detector built upon a frozen DINOv3 encoder, and introduce Geometry-Aware Expert Regression (GAER) and Prototype-Guided Query Modulation (PGQM) to improve localization and objectness estimation for diverse visual elements, respectively. Extensive experiments show that ECADet consistently outperforms representative class-agnostic and proposal-based detectors on BTCO-Bench, demonstrating the effectiveness of expanded objectness discovery. Code and benchmark will be released.

</details>


### [26] [RegionDet: A Benchmark for Region Detection Beyond Object Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06850)
*Liang Wan,Yuhan Wang,Yupeng Zhang,Zhen Xu,Han Wang,Fangjie Fu,Sirui Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出区域检测（Region Detection）任务，将目标检测从离散物体实例扩展到由视觉状态、场景上下文、物体关系和人类活动定义的区域目标（如施工区、受损道路、排队、群聊等），并构建了包含8个类别的RegionDet基准。实验表明，闭集检测器可在监督下部分学习区域模式，而零样本/开放词汇检测器因强烈的物体中心偏见表现严重不足，揭示了当前视觉-语言检测器的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有目标检测基准主要围绕物体实例构建，难以系统评估现实场景中的区域目标（如施工区、排队区域等），这些目标由视觉状态、场景上下文、物体关系和人类活动定义，而非离散的物体边界。

Method: 定义了区域检测任务，构建RegionDet基准，包含施工、穿越、损坏、排队、交谈、商贩、等待、行走8个区域类别，采用COCO风格的边界框标注和评估协议。系统评估了代表性闭集检测器和零样本/开放词汇检测器。

Result: 闭集检测器可在监督下部分学习区域级模式；零样本/开放词汇检测器表现严重不足，揭示了当前视觉-语言检测器存在强烈的物体中心偏见。区域检测的关键挑战包括弱边界线索、强上下文依赖性和关系级区域理解不足。

Conclusion: 区域检测任务扩展了传统目标检测的范畴，现有开放词汇检测器难以胜任，需要发展超越物体中心范式、具备上下文和关系理解能力的检测模型。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental task in computer vision and has achieved remarkable progress on standard benchmarks by localizing discrete and well-bounded object instances. However, many visual targets in real-world scenarios are not individual objects, but regions defined by visual states, scene context, object relations, and human activities, such as construction areas, damaged road regions, queues, group conversations, and vendor regions. Existing detection benchmarks are mainly built around object instances, providing limited support for systematically evaluating such region targets. To address this gap, we introduce Region Detection, a task that extends conventional object detection beyond object instances, and construct RegionDet, a benchmark for region target localization. RegionDet contains eight region categories, including Construction, Crossing, Damage, Queuing, Talking, Vendor, Waiting, and Walking, with COCO-style bounding-box annotations and evaluation protocols. We systematically evaluate representative closed-set and zero-shot/open-vocabulary detectors on RegionDet. Results show that closed-set detectors can partially learn region-level patterns under supervision, while zero-shot/open-vocabulary detectors struggle severely, revealing the strong object-centric bias of current vision-language detectors. Further analyses highlight key challenges in Region Detection, including weak boundary cues, strong context dependency, and insufficient relation-level region understanding. The RegionDet will be released.

</details>


### [27] [Multi-Agent Forensic Reasoning for Generalizable Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06865)
*Xuechao Zou,Shun Zhang,Kai Li,Yi Zhou,Xinyu Sun,Yuhui Chen,Zhe Wu,Congyan Lang,Junliang Xing*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了FaceVid-Forensics-100K大型深度伪造视频数据集（含10万视频、33种合成方法）和基于多智能体（纹理、光照、运动、物理四个专家代理+裁判代理）的取证推理框架，在跨域测试中超越GPT/Gemini等闭源模型，达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造视频基准覆盖面有限，缺乏细粒度文本标注；传统检测器和多模态大模型（MLLMs）难以捕捉细微伪造痕迹，对新兴AI生成方法的泛化能力不足。

Method: 构建FaceVid-Forensics-100K数据集（10万视频、33种合成方法，含Seedance 2.0等最新生成器），通过多模型聚合与冲突消解流水线自动生成细粒度文本标注和一致的取证解释。提出多智能体取证推理框架：四个领域专家智能体分别从纹理、光照、运动和物理四个视角独立分析伪造线索，再由裁判智能体整合报告生成最终预测与解释。

Result: 在跨域测试集上，仅使用小型开源MLLMs的该框架在所有指标上均超越包括GPT和Gemini在内的所有闭源模型，排名第一。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过多视角专业智能体协作和高质量数据集支撑，即使使用小型开源模型也能在深度伪造检测上达到超越闭源大模型的性能，为AI安全提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The malicious use of generative artificial intelligence to create highly realistic deepfake videos raises serious ethical concerns and poses substantial challenges to AI safety. However, existing deepfake video benchmarks provide limited coverage of recent synthesis methods and generally lack reliable fine-grained textual annotations. Meanwhile, conventional detectors and multimodal large language models (MLLMs), whether operating as a single model or relying on a single analytical perspective, often fail to capture subtle forgery artifacts, limiting their generalization to emerging AI-generated methods. To address these limitations, we introduce FaceVid-Forensics-100K, a large-scale deepfake video dataset comprising 100,000 videos and spanning 33 synthesis methods across face swapping, face reenactment, and entire-face synthesis, including recent generators such as Seedance 2.0. The dataset provides fine-grained textual annotations of visual observations and verdict-consistent forensic explanations, automatically synthesized through a multi-model aggregation and conflict-resolution pipeline powered by advanced MLLMs. Building on this benchmark, we propose a multi-agent forensic reasoning framework that employs four specialized domain-expert agents to independently analyze forgery cues from four perspectives: texture, lighting, motion, and physics. A judge agent then reconciles their reports to produce a final prediction together with an explanation. Extensive evaluations on out-of-domain test sets show that, despite being composed entirely of small open-source MLLMs, our framework outperforms all methods including closed-source GPT and Gemini models and ranks first across all reported metrics on this benchmark. The project page is available at https://xavierjiezou.github.io/ARGUS/.

</details>


### [28] [DAEP: Difficulty-Aware Evidence Planning for Medical Video Corpus Temporal Answer Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06869)
*Tianjian He,Yujie Liu,Zhiping Huang,Changbo Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DAEP is a difficulty-aware system for temporal answer grounding in video corpus, which retrieves and localizes answers by dynamically adjusting modality weights and aggregation strategies based on input difficulty labels, achieving first place in the NLPCC 2026 Shared Task.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the Difficulty-Aware Temporal Answer Grounding in Video Corpus (DA-TAGVC) task, where questions vary in complexity and require adaptive evidence integration from subtitles, visual content, and procedural context.

Method: DAEP ranks videos using subtitle, visual, and procedural-context evidence; expands high-scoring anchors into temporal spans; and reranks spans. Its core design converts the task-provided simple/complex label into an inference-time evidence plan that controls modality weights, Top-K aggregation, boundary threshold, expansion length, and reranking strength.

Result: BIGC ranked first among ten systems with an Average score of 0.2728 in the official evaluation. Validation ablations show that visual evidence, procedural context, and difficulty-aware planning improve ranking quality, with the largest gain on complex questions.

Conclusion: Difficulty-aware planning is effective for temporal grounding, especially for complex questions; visual evidence and procedural context contribute positively to ranking quality.

Abstract: We describe DAEP, team BIGC's submission to NLPCC 2026 Shared Task 1 Track 3: Difficulty-Aware Temporal Answer Grounding in Video Corpus (DA-TAGVC). The task requires retrieving the target video from 50 candidates and localizing the answer-supporting span. DAEP ranks videos with subtitle, visual, and procedural-context evidence, expands high-scoring anchors into temporal spans, and reranks spans for final output. Its main design is to convert the task-provided simple/complex input label into an inference-time evidence plan controlling modality weights, Top-K aggregation, boundary threshold, expansion length, and reranking strength. In the official evaluation, BIGC ranks first among ten systems with an Average score of 0.2728. Validation ablations show that visual evidence, procedural context, and difficulty-aware planning improve ranking quality, with the largest gain on complex questions.

</details>


### [29] [FedVAR: Prototype-Aligned Federated Framework for Video Anomaly Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06876)
*Ghani Haider,Majid Kundroo,Boyun Eom,Dong Hwan Park,Chen Chen,Taehong Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FedVAR 是一个面向视频异常识别（VAR）的弱监督联邦学习框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的原型对齐机制解决分布式客户端之间的语义错位问题，实现一致的“正常”事件表示，并在多种非独立同分布场景下超越现有联邦基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业物联网（IIoT）和网络物理系统（CPS）中，联邦学习（FL）为视频异常识别（VAR）提供了去中心化智能范式，但分布式边缘客户端间的数据异构性导致语义错位——各客户端对“正常”和“异常”事件学习到不同的特征表示。现有联邦方法仅关注二元异常检测，无法应对细粒度异常识别中的语义对齐挑战。

Method: 提出 FedVAR 框架，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）的丰富表征，通过原型对齐机制为所有客户端创建共享语义锚点，对视觉和文本特征空间进行重新中心化与对齐。该方法在去中心化网络中强制执行一致的“正常性”表示，直接缓解语义错位，并以极低的通信开销实现异常方向向量的鲁棒提示学习。

Result: 在多种非独立同分布（non-IID）数据划分方案、未见领域和新型异常类别设置下，FedVAR 在具有挑战性的基准测试中持续优于最先进的联邦基线方法。

Conclusion: FedVAR 为基于视频的网络物理系统提供了一种鲁棒的分布式智能框架，有效解决了联邦视频异常识别中的语义错位问题，验证了视觉-语言模型与原型对齐在弱监督联邦学习中的有效性。

Abstract: In the era of Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Cyber-Physical Systems (CPS), Federated Learning (FL) offers a promising decentralized intelligence paradigm for Video Anomaly Recognition (VAR). This task is vital for maintaining high-fidelity Digital Twins and ensuring safety in mission-critical environments. However, the inherent data heterogeneity across distributed edge clients leads to a fundamental challenge known as semantic misalignment, where clients learn divergent feature representations of "normal" and "abnormal" events. The problem becomes particularly pronounced in VAR, where the presence of diverse and fine-grained anomaly categories leads each client to develop distinct semantic interpretations of abnormality. Existing federated methods primarily focus on binary anomaly detection and fail to address this misalignment, preventing effective fine-grained recognition. In this paper, we introduce FedVAR, a weakly-supervised FL framework explicitly designed for VAR. Leveraging the rich representations of Vision-Language Models (VLMs), FedVAR employs a prototype-based alignment mechanism that creates a shared semantic anchor for all clients to re-center and align their visual and textual feature spaces. This process enforces a consistent representation of "normality" across the decentralized network, directly mitigating semantic misalignment and enabling robust prompt-learning of anomaly direction vectors with minimal communication overhead. We conduct extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks under various non-IID data partitioning schemes, unseen domains, and novel anomaly classes. The results demonstrate that FedVAR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art federated baselines, establishing a robust framework for distributed intelligence in video-based CPS.

</details>


### [30] [ControlRef: Efficient Layout-Guided Multi-Instance Generation via Anchored 4D-RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06878)
*Yunkai Yang,Yudong Zhang,Xinying Chen,Haoyuan Liang,Yizhuo Niu,Jinshuai Cheng,Kunquan Zhang,Liziyue Fang,Weitao Wan,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ControlRef框架，通过Unified Instance-Layout Control (UILC)注意力掩码和Anchored 4D-RoPE位置编码，解决了多模态扩散Transformer中布局引导的多实例生成问题，在稀疏布局下推理延迟降低80%以上，密集场景内存开销减少50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全分辨率画布填充和Shifted-RoPE的多参考图像处理机制存在严重缺陷：稀疏布局下计算开销巨大（冗余填充），且Shifted-RoPE破坏低频RoPE特征，导致空间-频率妥协（spatial-frequency compromise），模糊了绝对空间对应关系。需要一种高效且精确的替代方案。

Method: 提出ControlRef框架，包含两大核心技术：(1) Unified Instance-Layout Control (UILC)注意力掩码，严格解耦实例间语义交互并强制精确区域绑定；(2) Anchored 4D-RoPE，一种新的位置编码机制，通过将参考图像预对齐到对应边界框分辨率、将布局和参考token锚定到绝对几何中心、并沿z轴堆叠参考图像，原生保留空间先验，避免损失性移位。

Result: 实现了最先进的视觉保真度和定位精度；在稀疏布局中推理延迟降低80%以上，密集场景内存开销减少50%。

Conclusion: ControlRef通过创新的注意力掩码和4D-RoPE位置编码，高效解决了多实例生成中的空间-频率妥协问题，显著提升了计算效率和生成质量。

Abstract: Layout-guided multi-instance generation is essential for controllable image synthesis in Multi-Modal Diffusion Transformers (MM-DiTs). However, integrating this capability into unified architectures remains challenging. Prior frameworks rely on redundant full-resolution canvas padding and Shifted-RoPE to manage multiple reference images. This mechanism drastically inflates computational overhead for sparse layouts and disrupts critical low-frequency RoPE features, creating a severe spatial-frequency compromise that blurs absolute spatial correspondence. To overcome these limitations, we propose ControlRef, a highly efficient and precise multi-instance synthesis framework. ControlRef utilizes a Unified Instance-Layout Control (UILC) attention mask to strictly decouple inter-instance semantic interactions and enforce precise regional binding. To further promote region-level spatial alignment, we introduce Anchored 4D-RoPE, a novel positional encoding mechanism that directly anchors tokens to their absolute geometric centers. By pre-aligning reference images to their corresponding bounding box resolutions, physically anchoring both layout and reference tokens to their absolute geometric centers, and stacking the references along the z-axis, Anchored 4D-RoPE natively preserves spatial priors and mitigates the spatial-frequency compromise without lossy shifting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ControlRef achieves state-of-the-art visual fidelity and localization accuracy, while concurrently slashing inference latency by over 80% in sparse layouts and reducing memory overhead by 50% in dense scenarios.

</details>


### [31] [HazeSpikeMamba: Coupling Spiking-Inspired and State-Space Features for Self-Supervised Real-World Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06886)
*Haoran Liu,Huibin Li,Mingzhe Liu,Peng Li,Guibin Zan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了 HazeSpikeMamba，一种紧凑的去雾框架，结合了脉冲耦合局部路径（TPCNNSpike）和注意力状态空间全局路径（改进自 MambaIRv2），并通过冻结的退化网络进行无标签目标域自适应，实现数据集级别的直推式评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的去雾网络常在合成雾霾-清晰对上训练，但真实照片上的性能显著下降。原因在于：1）基于大气散射模型合成的雾霾无法完全捕捉真实雾霾的多样性；2）配对的真实雾霾-清晰图像非常稀缺。因此需要一种无需配对真实数据即可适应目标域的方法。

Method: 1）整体架构：多尺度 U-Net，包含脉冲启发局部路径和注意力状态空间全局路径。2）局部路径：提出 TPCNNSpike 脉冲发射方案，受脉冲耦合神经网络（PCNN）邻域耦合启发，所有神经元并行更新，利用高斯加权邻域的前一发放状态。3）全局路径：改编自 MambaIRv2 的注意力状态空间模块，保留语义提示和序列重排序，移除窗口自注意力分支，利用状态空间处理以线性复杂度建模长距离依赖。4）目标域自适应：冻结的退化网络（在 NH-HAZE 上预训练）从去雾预测中重新合成雾霾，重建误差仅更新 HazeSpikeMamba 的最后恢复层，无需清晰标签。自适应仅在完整无标签目标集上执行一次，属于直推式（transductive）而非零样本或逐图像优化。
