We study report-grounded abnormality segmentation, where a model must determine target eligibility, cardinality, and finding-to-mask correspondence directly from an unfiltered report before delineating the corresponding regions. We propose \textbf{EliSeg}, an atcor--verify--revise framework that integrates target construction with mask generation. A grammar-constrained Actor proposes target slots and masks, an independent text-only Verifier reconstructs the eligible finding inventory, and Revision selectively re-executes the shared Actor when their target structures disagree. EliSeg requires no predefined target identity, finding prompt, point, or bounding box. Experiments on MIMIC-CXR-ILS show that EliSeg consistently outperforms direct segmentation methods and extract-then-segment cascades across findings, while effectively suppressing masks for ineligible report mentions. Ablation studies confirm the complementary roles of verification and revision, and evaluation on CheXlocalize demonstrates effective transfer of the EliSeg to an external dataset.Code is available at https://github.com/Maybach-dream/EliSeg.

</details>


### [64] [Same Attention, Different Truths: Put Logit-Lens over Visual Attention to Detect and Mitigate LVLM Object Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07302)
*Zichuan Wang,Songlin Yang,Bo Peng,Zhenchen Tang,Yang Li,Beibei Dong,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型视觉-语言模型（LVLM）中对象幻觉的根本原因并非视觉注意力不足，而是注意力所关注的内容及原因。通过Logit Lens解码高注意力区域的视觉特征，发现真实对象区域可正确解码为目标token，而幻觉对象区域则不能。据此识别出两种幻觉机制：视觉不确定性（由语义相似或易混淆区域触发）和上下文先验（由强共现先验触发）。提出无需训练的检测-缓解框架，包括Logit-Lens一致性检查检测幻觉、高注意力区域掩码（HARM）缓解视觉不确定性幻觉、视觉证据增强解码（VEED）缓解上下文先验幻觉，在多个幻觉基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将LVLM的对象幻觉归因于视觉注意力不足，但本文发现真实和幻觉对象在模型中层到高层都获得同等强度的视觉注意力，表明关键问题不在于注意力数量而在于注意力的质量（关注了什么以及为何关注）。因此，作者旨在深入探究幻觉产生的具体机制，并设计无需额外训练的缓解方法。

Method: 1）使用Logit Lens解码高注意力区域的视觉特征，对比真实对象与幻觉对象的解码差异；2）识别两种幻觉机制：视觉不确定性（语义相似/易混淆区域）和上下文先验（强共现先验）；3）提出检测-缓解框架：Logit-Lens一致性检查检测幻觉，高注意力区域掩码（HARM）处理视觉不确定性幻觉，视觉证据增强解码（VEED）处理上下文先验幻觉。

Result: 在多个对象幻觉基准测试上取得最先进（SOTA）结果，验证了所提框架的有效性。具体来说，HARM可消除由视觉不确定性引起的幻觉，VEED可在注意力漂移情况下仍缓解由上下文先验引起的幻觉。

Conclusion: LVLM的对象幻觉主要源于两种机制：视觉不确定性和上下文先验。所提出的无需训练的检测-缓解框架能够有效检测并针对性缓解这两种幻觉，达到SOTA性能。代码将开源。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often suffer from object hallucination, generating objects that are absent from the image. Prior work largely attributes this to insufficient visual attention. However, we find that both real and hallucinated objects receive equally strong visual attention in the model's mid-to-late layers, suggesting that the key issue may not be how much the model attends, but what it attends to and why. To this end, we decode the visual features of high-attention regions using Logit Lens, and observe that regions corresponding to real objects can be correctly decoded to the target object tokens, whereas those for hallucinated objects cannot. Building on this, we identify two hallucination mechanisms: (i) visual uncertainty, triggered by semantically similar or confusable regions; masking these regions eliminates the hallucination. (ii) contextual prior, triggered by strong co-occurrence priors; even when the initially attended region is masked, the hallucination persists and attention drifts to other regions. Based on these findings, we propose a simple yet effective training-free Detect-Mitigate framework comprising a Logit-Lens Consistency Check to detect hallucination and targeted remedies: High-Attention Regions Masking (HARM) for visual uncertainty hallucination, and Visual Evidence Enhanced Decoding (VEED) for contextual prior hallucination. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on multiple hallucination benchmarks. Code will be available.

</details>


### [65] [H2AL: Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning for Registration-based Few-shot Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07340)
*Jia Wang,Jiaming Cai,Zunying Hu,Zhanjie Wu,Jinyuan Liu,Hua Cheng,Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于双曲空间的层次感知聚合学习框架（H2AL），用于配准式小样本医学图像分割。通过双曲层次感知注入模块和梯度聚合的端到端联合优化，提升了配准与分割双任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配准式小样本医学图像分割方法在欧氏空间进行像素级优化和推理，将解剖结构视为平坦且不相交的，忽略了固有的层次结构，导致伪标签质量下降、模糊区域判别能力弱，限制了分割性能。

Method: 提出H2AL框架：1）双曲层次感知注入（H2I）模块，利用双曲空间的层次建模能力，通过变换引导的监督双曲对比学习学习层次感知表示，并通过门控注入块将层次先验注入欧氏空间；2）端到端联合优化算法，通过梯度聚合，将配准和分割解码器的梯度（嵌入语义和层次线索）聚合以更新共享编码器，促进跨任务协作学习。

Result: 在两个解剖区域、五种实验设置上的大量实验表明，该方法在配准和分割任务中均有效且高效。

Conclusion: H2AL通过双曲空间的层次感知和梯度聚合的联合优化，有效解决了现有方法忽略层次结构的问题，提升了配准式小样本医学图像分割的性能。

Abstract: Registration-based Few-shot medical image segmentation (RFMIS) aims to generate pseudo-labels for unlabeled images by warping a labeled image through registration. However, existing methods primarily perform pixel-level optimization and inference in Euclidean space, treating anatomical structures as flat and disjoint. This neglect of inherent hierarchies degrades pseudo-label quality and weakens the discrimination of ambiguous regions, limiting the segmentation performance. To overcome this challenge, we propose a Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Aggregative Learning framework for RFMIS, termed H2AL, that enhances both deformation plausibility and anatomical discrimination for dual-task learning. Specifically, we introduce a Hyperbolic Hierarchy-aware Infusion (H2I) module, which leverages the hierarchical modeling capability of hyperbolic space to learn precise hierarchy-aware representations via transformation-guided supervised hyperbolic contrastive learning, and injects such hierarchical priors into Euclidean space through a gated infusion block while preserving semantic richness. Furthermore, we propose an end-to-end joint optimization algorithm by gradient aggregation, where the gradients from the registration and segmentation decoders, embedding semantic and hierarchical cues, are aggregated to update the shared encoder to promote collaborative learning across tasks. Extensive experiments on two anatomical regions, with five experimental settings, demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of our method in both registration and segmentation. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/JiamingCai469/H2AL.

</details>


### [66] [SABRE: Scalable and Automated Benchmarking of VLMs under Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07435)
*Zixuan Lan,Luzhe Sun,Matthew R. Walter,Jiawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SABRE，一个可扩展的自动化流水线，用于构建视觉-语言模型（VLM）的压力测试。它从测试模板（Markdown 任务设计与数据模式）生成结构化规格、图像和问答对，并通过自动过滤和人工审核确保质量。实例化 SABRE-Prior 评估 VLM 是否依赖世界先验而非视觉证据，在 6 个模型上平均准确率仅 22.6%。SABRE-Counting 和 SABRE-Spatial 展示了其通用性。SABRE 定位为可复用的压力测试框架，而非单一固定基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM 发展迅速，但基准测试开发滞后，难以识别模型弱点。手动构建压力测试成本高昂，需要满足受控条件、保证可回答性并挑战当前模型。

Method: SABRE 流水线将测试模板（Markdown 任务设计+数据模式）转化为结构化规格，生成或编辑图像，并创建问答对。自动过滤移除被过滤 VLM 解决的候选样本，人工审核验证有效性、修正标注并修复局部图像。具体实例 SABRE-Prior 涵盖上下文、纹理、属性和语言诱发四类测试。

Result: 在 6 个 VLM 上，宏观平均准确率 17.8%-31.3%（均值 22.6%）。真实图像属性控制对过滤 VLM 同样困难。SABRE-Counting 和 SABRE-Spatial 试点表明该工作流支持其他压力测试设置。

Conclusion: SABRE 是一个可复用的框架，用于构建和更新 VLM 压力测试，而非单一固定基准。它能够系统性地暴露 VLM 依赖世界先验而非视觉证据的倾向。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are improving rapidly, but benchmark development lags behind, making weaknesses hard to identify. Building stress tests is costly: samples must satisfy controlled conditions, remain answerable, and challenge current models. We present SABRE, a scalable, automated pipeline that converts a Test Primer (a Markdown Task Design with Data Schema) into structured specifications, generated or edited images, and question-answer pairs. Automated filtering removes candidates solved by a Filtering VLM, while human review verifies candidate validity and supports annotation correction and localized image repair. We instantiate SABRE-Prior to test whether VLMs follow visual evidence instead of relying on world priors -- learned expectations about familiar objects and scenes. Its 600 images and 1,000 questions span Context (unexpected entities in familiar scenes), Texture (counterfactual materials), Attribute (noncanonical component counts), and Language Elicitation (answers suggested by language but unsupported by the image). Across six VLMs, macro-average accuracy ranges from 17.8% to 31.3% (22.6% mean). A real-image Attribute control is comparably difficult for the Filtering VLM. SABRE-Counting and SABRE-Spatial pilots show that the workflow supports other stress-test settings. These results establish SABRE as a reusable framework for constructing and refreshing VLM stress tests rather than a single fixed benchmark.

</details>


### [67] [SkySeaLand: A Wide-Format Satellite Transportation Benchmark with an Ultra-Lightweight Detection Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07382)
*Md. Zahid Hasan Riad,Md Sultanul Islam Ovi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SkySeaLand是一个包含1,307张高分辨率卫星图像和19,101个标注框的公开数据集，覆盖飞机、船只、汽车和船舶四类目标，用于陆地与海洋混合场景的目标检测。论文评估了12种检测器，并提出了一个轻量级基线模型SkyDet（1.22M参数，4.90MB，72.8 FPS），其mAP50为60.5，mAP50-95为24.32，旨在为低计算开销的遥感检测提供参考基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有卫星目标检测面临小目标和大场景挑战，标准方形缩放会丢失细节。缺乏针对陆地-海洋混合场景的紧凑、高分辨率基准数据集。

Method: 构建SkySeaLand数据集（1,307张高分辨率图像，19,101个边界框，支持COCO和YOLO格式），评估YOLO、RT-DETR、DETR、Faster R-CNN等12种检测器，并提出一个1.22M参数的anchor-free基线模型SkyDet。

Result: YOLO和RT-DETR变体在mAP50上达到84.4–88.2，但更大参数量的模型未见一致提升。SkyDet在4.90MB模型大小下达到60.5 mAP50和24.32 mAP50-95，在Tesla T4上延迟13.74 ms（72.8 FPS）。

Conclusion: SkySeaLand为陆地-海洋交通检测提供了紧凑基准，SkyDet作为低内存占用的参考基线，而非追求最高精度。

Abstract: Satellite object detection is challenged by small targets and wide-format scenes that lose detail under standard square-input resizing. We introduce SkySeaLand, a public dataset of 1,307 high-resolution satellite images and 19,101 verified bounding boxes across airplane, boat, car, and ship classes in terrestrial and maritime scenes. Native COCO and YOLO annotations are provided. The collection is dominated by large source images and wide scene geometry: 84.5 percent exceed 3,836 pixels on the longest side and 73.1 percent are near a 3:1 aspect ratio. We evaluate twelve detectors from the YOLO, RT-DETR, DETR, and Faster R-CNN families using a common split and COCO metrics. The tested YOLO and RT-DETR variants obtain 84.4--88.2 mAP50, with no consistent accuracy gain from larger parameter counts under the reported model-specific recipes. We also report SkyDet, a 1.22 M parameter anchor-free baseline that obtains 60.5 mAP50 and 24.32 mAP50-95 in a 4.90 MB footprint, with 13.74 ms latency (72.8 FPS) on a Tesla T4. SkySeaLand provides a compact benchmark for mixed land--maritime transportation detection, while SkyDet establishes a documented low-footprint reference rather than a state-of-the-art accuracy claim.

</details>


### [68] [GeoDistill-Refine: Silhouette-First Geometry Distillation for Annotation-Free Spacecraft Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07405)
*Yonglong Zhang,Zongwu Xie,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoDistill-Refine is a two-stage knowledge distillation framework that transfers SAM 3 pseudo-masks to a compact TinyUNet (0.263M params) for spacecraft segmentation. It fuses six prompted SAM outputs via unweighted majority vote, trains the student on foreground silhouette then refines with signed-distance-field, skeleton, and area losses, and uses a sample-level gate to suppress unreliable pseudo-geometry. On SpaceSense-Bench it improves Image IoU by 0.0456 and Boundary F1 by 0.1380 over a plain pseudo-label student; the deployed model runs at ~1.1 ms per image on an RTX 4090.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Foundation segmentation models like SAM can provide supervision for spacecraft imagery without manual masks, but their predictions vary with textual prompts and contain geometric errors that are amplified during distillation. The goal is to transfer offline SAM 3 pseudo-masks to a compact segmentation network while mitigating prompt sensitivity and geometric inaccuracies.

Method: Two-stage framework: (1) Teacher: six fixed prompts fed to SAM 3, outputs fused by unweighted 50% majority vote to stabilize predictions. (2) Student (TinyUNet): first trained on foreground silhouette, then refined with auxiliary objectives—signed-distance-field, skeleton, and area losses derived from the pseudo-mask. A sample-level gate computed from prompt agreement, valid-prompt ratio, and pseudo-mask area plausibility reduces the influence of unreliable pseudo-geometry.

Result: On SpaceSense-Bench HJM lockbox set: Image IoU improved by 0.0456 and Boundary F1 by 0.1380 over a plain pseudo-label student. On SPEED+ Lightbox, Sunlamp domains, and TANGO: competitive regional overlap with gains in boundary quality or foreground precision. TinyUNet has 0.263M parameters and runs ~1.1 ms per image on RTX 4090.

Conclusion: GeoDistill-Refine effectively distills SAM 3 knowledge into a lightweight student for spacecraft segmentation, overcoming prompt variability and geometric errors via prompt fusion, multi-objective refinement, and sample-level gating. The approach achieves strong boundary and foreground performance while maintaining high inference efficiency suitable for deployment.

Abstract: Foundation segmentation models can provide supervision for spacecraft imagery without manual training masks, but their predictions vary with textual prompts and may contain geometric errors that are amplified during distillation. This paper presents GeoDistill-Refine, a two-stage framework that transfers offline SAM 3 pseudo-masks to a compact segmentation network. Six fixed prompts are fused by an unweighted 50% vote to stabilize the teacher output. The student first learns the foreground silhouette and is then refined with signed-distance-field, skeleton, and area objectives derived from the pseudo-mask. A sample-level gate, computed from prompt agreement, the valid-prompt ratio, and pseudo-mask area plausibility, reduces the influence of unreliable pseudo-geometry. On the SpaceSense-Bench HJM lockbox set, GeoDistill-Refine improves Image IoU and Boundary F1 by 0.0456 and 0.1380, respectively, over a plain pseudo-label student. External evaluations on the SPEED+ Lightbox and Sunlamp domains and on TANGO show competitive regional overlap together with gains in boundary quality or foreground precision. The deployed TinyUNet contains 0.263 M parameters and requires approximately 1.1 ms per image on an RTX 4090; SAM 3 pseudo-mask construction and the auxiliary geometry branches are used only during training.

</details>


### [69] [Addressable Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07408)
*Xindi Wu,Sven Elflein,James Lucas,Olga Russakovsky,Laura Leal-Taixé,Despoina Paschalidou,Jonathan Lorraine,Aljoša Ošep*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出WorldTrace，一种无需训练的记忆框架，通过为压缩缓存分配分布内的虚拟位置来解决交互式视频世界模型中视觉持久性难题。其中WorldTrace-Field提升时间一致性15.5%，WorldTrace-Landmark提升情景回忆19.5%，无需重新训练即可扩展视觉持久生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式视频世界模型依赖KV缓存作为视觉记忆，但一旦生成轨迹超出训练范围，时间RoPE偏移超出训练分布，模型无法可靠地通过注意力检索存储的视觉信息；此外，在RoPE旋转空间中直接压缩缓存会因混合不兼容的相位而破坏记忆。

Method: 提出WorldTrace，一种无需训练的记忆框架，通过为每个压缩摘要槽分配一个独特的、在分布内的虚拟位置，使压缩缓存保持可寻址。包含两种方法：WorldTrace-Field压缩历史以保持时间连贯性；WorldTrace-Landmark在检测到的场景转换处存储逐字场景轨迹以支持情景回忆。同时引入LoopBench基准测试，评估压缩缓存是否能在长绕行后重建先前访问的场景。

Result: 在LoopBench上，WorldTrace-Field将时间一致性提升15.5%，WorldTrace-Landmark将情景回忆提升19.5%，无需重新训练即可扩展视觉持久生成。

Conclusion: WorldTrace通过解决KV缓存的可寻址性问题，有效扩展了视频世界模型的视觉记忆跨度，证明了无需重新训练即可实现长程视觉持久化的可行性。

Abstract: We study visual persistence in interactive video world models. These models rely on a Key-Value (KV) cache as a growing visual memory to carry forward previously generated frames. However, we find that models can no longer reliably address stored content once rollouts extend beyond the training horizon, because temporal Rotary Positional Embeddings (RoPE) offsets then fall outside the range seen during training and the model struggles to retrieve the relevant visual information through attention. Moreover, naively compressing the cache in the RoPE-rotated space corrupts memory by averaging together incompatible positional phases. To address this, we propose WorldTrace, a training-free memory framework for long-horizon visual persistence. WorldTrace keeps compressed memory addressable by assigning each summary slot a distinct, in-distribution virtual position. Within this addressable cache, we study two memory compression approaches: WorldTrace-Field compresses history for temporal coherence, while WorldTrace-Landmark stores verbatim scene traces at detected transitions for episodic recall. We further introduce LoopBench, a benchmark evaluating whether a compressed cache can reconstruct a previously visited scene after a long detour. WorldTrace-Field improves temporal consistency by +15.5%, and WorldTrace-Landmark improves episodic recall by +19.5% on LoopBench, extending visually persistent generation without retraining.

</details>


### [70] [UniJEPA: A Unified Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture for Task-Agnostic Visual World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07409)
*An Lanji,Dawei Liu,Jin Li,Haoran Xu,Mei Chen,Yu Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniJEPA，一个统一的联合嵌入预测架构，通过单一端到端目标（下一嵌入预测损失+高斯正则化器）同时学习图像级光度变换预测和视频级时序动态预测，实现无需EMA、stop-gradient或预训练编码器的抗坍塌编码器-预测器训练，并在图像、视频和控制基准上达到或超越任务特定JEPA，同时实现比生成式世界模型快数十倍的计划速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法将图像级（光度变换预测）和视频级（时序状态预测）世界建模视为不同的任务，使用分离的编码器、预测器和抗坍塌正则化器，阻碍了一个统一模型同时处理图像级和视频级的世界建模。

Method: 提出UniJEPA，一个统一的联合嵌入预测架构，使用单一端到端目标函数结合下一嵌入预测损失和高斯正则化器，在共享潜在空间中联合学习光度预测（图像级变换不变结构）和时序预测（视频级下一状态等变动力学），无需EMA、stop-gradient或预训练编码器即可实现可证明的抗坍塌编码器-预测器训练。

Result: 在图像、视频和控制基准测试上，UniJEPA匹配或超越任务特定JEPA方法，仅需单一损失超参数，并且计划速度比生成式世界模型快数十倍，同时保持相当的精度。

Conclusion: UniJEPA证明了通过统一的目标函数可以在单个共享潜在空间中同时学习图像级和视频级的世界模型，实现可控抽象（不变性与等变性），并通过行动条件后训练实现零样本规划，为自监督世界模型学习提供了一个更简洁、高效的框架。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have emerged as a principled framework for self-supervised learning of world models in compact latent spaces, yet existing methods are fragmented: some predict masked parts of a single image in latent space (I-JEPA), others learn to predict global photometric transformations (Image World Models), while video-scale JEPAs predict future temporal states and are post-trained for action-conditioned planning (V-JEPA~2, DINO-World, DINO-WM). These objectives are treated as distinct recipes with separate encoders, predictors, and anti-collapse regularizers, hindering a single model from unifying image-level and video-level world modeling. We present UniJEPA, a unified JEPA that jointly learns photometric prediction (image-level transformations) and temporal prediction (video-level next-state dynamics) in one shared latent space. A single end-to-end objective, composed of a next-embedding prediction loss and a Gaussian regularizer, yields a provably anti-collapse encoder-predictor pair trainable from raw pixels without EMA, stop-gradient, or pre-trained encoders. We show that the same latent space supports controllable abstraction: photometric prediction learns invariant structure while temporal prediction learns equivariant dynamics. After action-conditioned post-training on offline trajectories, UniJEPA enables zero-shot planning by treating goal features as prediction targets. On image, video, and control benchmarks, UniJEPA matches or surpasses task-specific JEPAs while requiring a single loss hyperparameter, and plans up to tens of times faster than generative world models at comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [71] [I Seek You in Videos: Identity-Conditioned Queries for Person-Centric Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07417)
*Shibo Gao,Chongxiao Wang,Chenglong Huang,Jie Ma,Haolin Shi,Fei Ding,Jing Li,Qiang Lyu,Yangyang Liu,Yang Liu,Jun Liu,Linlin Huang,Peipei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出身份条件化查询（ICQ）任务，要求模型联合处理视频与人物参考图像，实现身份关联、行为理解与时序推理。为此构建了ISYV系统，包含评测基准ISYV-Bench（1377个复杂视频及问答对，六难度层级）、大规模训练集ISYV-75K（7.5万高质量样本，经自动标注、多阶段验证与人工审查）以及ISYV框架（面向ICQ的模型与训练策略）。实验表明主流多模态大模型在基准上表现困难，尤其在跨域身份匹配与长时跟踪方面；ISYV模型超越强基线，部分接近闭源模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频推理任务通常简化为视频-文本设定，限制了身份匹配与人物中心推理能力。现实场景中视频推理常涉及多模态、多源输入（如视频+人物参考图像），因此需要新的任务定义与解决方案来弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出ICQ任务，要求模型同时关联输入视频与人物参考图像。构建ISYV系统，包含：(1) ISYV-Bench：1377个真实复杂视频与问答对，按六难度级别组织（从身份识别到因果推理）；(2) ISYV-75K：7.5万训练样本，通过自动标注、多阶段验证与人工审查构建；(3) ISYV-Framework：面向ICQ的模型与训练策略，无需额外镜头级标注即可学习利用信息性视频镜头。

Result: 主流闭源与开源多模态大模型在ISYV-Bench上表现不佳，尤其在跨域身份匹配与长时跟踪任务中。ISYV模型显著优于强基线，部分指标接近闭源模型性能。

Conclusion: ISYV为人物中心视频推理提供了统一的任务定义、可扩展的数据集/基准以及建模见解，推动了该领域的发展。

Abstract: Real-world video reasoning often involves multimodal, multi-source inputs, whereas existing video reasoning tasks typically assume a simplified video-text setting, limiting identity matching and person-centric reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce the Identity-conditioned Queries (ICQ) task, in which models are required to jointly associate and interpret an input video and a reference image of a person, and leverage this conditioning to address identity grounding, behavior understanding, and temporal reasoning, among other challenges. Building on ICQ, we present ISYV (I Seek You in Videos), a systematic solution comprising three components: (1) ISYV-Bench, a challenging evaluation benchmark with 1,377 real-world complex videos and 1,377 question-answer pairs, organized into six difficulty levels spanning capabilities from identity recognition to causal reasoning; (2) ISYV-75K, a large-scale training set of 75K high-quality samples constructed via automated annotation, multi-stage verification, and manual review; and (3) ISYV-Framework, containing an ICQ-oriented model and training strategy for learning to exploit informative video shots without additional shot-level annotations. Extensive experiments show that both mainstream closed-source and open-source MLLMs struggle on ISYV-Bench, especially in cross-domain identity matching and long-horizon tracking. ISYV-Model outperforms strong baselines and in some aspects approaches closed-source performance. Overall, ISYV provides a unified task definition, scalable datasets/benchmarks, and modeling insights for person-centric video reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [Conformal Coverage Guarantees for Any Video Temporal Grounder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07434)
*Aseel Mohamed,Rasul Khanbayov,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: COVER is a post-hoc, model-agnostic wrapper that converts any video temporal grounder into one that outputs a calibrated temporal region containing the true event moment with at least 1-α probability, using conformal prediction on temporal nonconformity scores. It addresses the issue of ambiguous event boundaries in continuous video where ground truth is a distribution over intervals.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Event boundaries in continuous video are inherently ambiguous: different annotators mark different moments for the same query-video pair, often overlapping by less than half. Existing grounders return a single interval without reliability statements, making wrong intervals indistinguishable from correct ones at deployment.

Method: COVER wraps any trained localizer or black-box video-language model post-hoc. It calibrates a quantile of a temporal nonconformity score on held-out labels, then widens the base prediction by that amount to produce a certified region. Two score families are proposed: a two-sided boundary-widening score for interval-output grounders, and a super-level-set score for grounders emitting a relevance signal. The method provides finite-sample, distribution-free guarantees under exchangeability.

Result: Across three benchmarks and five grounders, realized coverage closely tracks the target coverage level. Calibration reveals what point metrics (e.g., mIoU) hide. The method also provides theoretical bounds on how large the certified region becomes, when coverage survives conditioning on event length, and how performance degrades when moments from one video break exchangeability.

Conclusion: COVER effectively converts any video temporal grounder into one with statistically rigorous coverage guarantees, addressing annotation ambiguity. It is model-agnostic, requires no retraining or white-box access, and works with both interval-output and relevance-signal grounders. The approach improves reliability in deployment scenarios.

Abstract: Event boundaries in continuous video are ambiguous: re-annotate the same query-video pair and independent annotators mark moments that overlap by less than half on a large fraction of samples. The ground truth for video temporal grounding is therefore a distribution over intervals, yet every grounder returns a single interval with no statement of reliability, so at deployment a wrong interval is indistinguishable from a right one. COVER changes the output object: a post-hoc, model-agnostic wrapper that turns any grounder, a trained localizer or a black-box video--language model, into one that emits a temporal region containing the true moment with probability at least $1-α$, by calibrating the quantile of a temporal nonconformity score on held-out labels and widening the base prediction by that amount. The guarantee is finite-sample and distribution-free under exchangeability, and requires neither retraining nor white-box access. We give two score families, a two-sided boundary-widening score for grounders that emit an interval and a super-level-set score for grounders that emit a relevance signal, and develop theory specific to grounding that bounds how large the certified region becomes, when coverage survives conditioning on event length, and how it degrades when moments from one video break exchangeability. Across three benchmarks and five grounders, realized coverage tracks the target, and calibration exposes what point metrics hide.

</details>


### [73] [MirrorWorld: Taming Video Diffusion Models for Mirror Reflection Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07463)
*Youjun Zhao,Alex Warren,Gary K. L. Tam,Rynson W. H. Lau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MirrorWorld 提出了一种针对视频扩散模型中镜面反射生成的感知一致性修复框架，通过语义关系蒸馏（SRD）和几何变换对齐（GTA）分别建模"反射什么"和"如何排列"，在重建质量上优于现有图像反射生成和视频修复方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型难以生成与场景保持一致的镜面反射，因为镜中内容必须与周围环境在语义和空间上协调，而现有模型未专门建模这种场景-镜子关系。

Method: 提出 MirrorWorld 框架，包含两个互补组件：（1）语义关系蒸馏（SRD），从冻结的视觉基础模型中迁移关系信息，增强可见场景内容与镜面区域之间的语义关联；（2）几何变换对齐（GTA），学习一个变换来指导反射内容的空间排列。两者分别负责"反射什么"和"如何排列"。

Result: 在构建的基于四个现有视频镜子数据集的统一反射重建基准上，MirrorWorld 在重建质量上优于代表性的基于图像的反射生成方法和强视频修复基线。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦"语义内容"和"空间排列"两个子问题，MirrorWorld 能够更有效地建模场景-镜子关系，从而提升视频中镜面反射的生成质量。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models (VDMs) have enabled high-fidelity video synthesis. However, generating mirror reflections remains challenging because the content within a mirror must remain consistent with the surrounding scene. Existing VDMs are not specifically designed to model scene-to-mirror relationships, which can lead to reflections with incorrect content or inconsistent spatial arrangements. We observe that mirror reflection generation involves two complementary challenges: determining what scene content should be reflected and how the reflected content should be spatially arranged within the mirror region. Motivated by this observation, we propose MirrorWorld, a reflection-aware video inpainting framework that models scene-to-mirror relationships during generation. Specifically, we introduce Semantic Relation Distillation (SRD), which transfers relational information from a frozen visual foundation model to encourage semantic associations between visible scene content and mirror regions. We further propose Geometric Transformation Alignment (GTA), which learns a transformation that guides the spatial arrangement of reflected content. The two components play complementary roles, with SRD modeling what should be reflected and GTA modeling how it should be arranged. To facilitate research on this problem, we construct a benchmark for video mirror reflection generation by repurposing four existing video mirror datasets into a unified reflection reconstruction task. Experimental results show that MirrorWorld achieves improved reflection reconstruction quality over representative image-based reflection generation methods and strong video inpainting baselines.

</details>


### [74] [SimWAM: A Simple World Action Model for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07468)
*Zongchuang Zhao,Xin Zhou,Tianyang Xu,Zhengyang Sun,Kaixuan Zhou,Honglin Li,Dingkang Liang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimWAM 提出了一种简化的世界-动作模型（WAM），在训练阶段利用视频生成作为训练信号，但推理时完全丢弃视频分支，仅保留轻量级动作专家直接预测轨迹，从而在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 WAM 方法将视频动态先验迁移到动作预测中，但推理时仍需耗费大量计算进行未来帧生成，导致延迟过高，不利于实际部署。

Method: 联合训练预训练的视频专家和轻量级动作专家，使用联合流匹配（joint flow matching）和隔离注意力掩码（isolated attention mask）确保动作预测不依赖未来帧；训练后丢弃视频分支，仅保留动作专家作为独立规划器；额外使用强化学习优化组合驾驶奖励。

Result: 在 NAVSIM 上达到 91.5 PDMS，超越现有最先进的 WAM 规划器，同时延迟显著更低；零样本迁移到 nuScenes 数据集表现优异。

Conclusion: SimWAM 是一个简单而坚实的基线，能够直接受益于视频生成领域的进步，为高效自动驾驶提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) improve end-to-end autonomous driving by transferring video dynamics priors to action prediction, but existing methods require costly future generation at inference. We present SimWAM, a simple yet effective WAM that uses video generation purely as a training signal. It co-trains a pretrained video expert and a lightweight action expert with joint flow matching. An isolated attention mask keeps action prediction independent of future frames, allowing the video branch to be discarded after training and leaving a self-contained planner that directly predicts trajectories. Since the two experts share no parameters and interact only through a unified attention interface, the video backbone could be replaced and the action expert scaled independently without modifying the learning objective or inference pipeline. We further apply reinforcement learning to optimize a compositional driving reward beyond trajectory imitation. Our SimWAM achieves $91.5$ PDMS on NAVSIM, surpasses state-of-the-art WAM-based planners with substantially lower latency, and transfers zero-shot to nuScenes. These results position SimWAM as a simple yet solid baseline that could readily benefit from advances in video generation for efficient autonomous driving. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/H-EmbodVis/SimWAM/

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [TEXAS: Task-Expert-Aware Supervision for Downstream Mixture-of-Experts LLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06396)
*Guanzhi Deng,Haibo Wang,Kuan Wu,Xiangru Jian,Shing Yin Wong,Sichun Luo,Zhuoran Wang,Linqi Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TEXAS 是一种针对混合专家（MoE）语言模型的任务专家感知监督方法，通过正确性条件化的专家激活模式识别与任务相关的专家，并在微调时对失败实例中激活这些专家的答案令牌进行加权，从而在不限制专家子集或强制路由分布的情况下提升性能。在三个 MoE 模型和六个基准测试中，TEXAS 在 18 个设置中的 17 个上达到最佳或并列最佳，平均提升 1.3–1.5 个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法识别任务专家时依赖于聚合路由统计量，反映的是使用频率而非与成功完成任务的关联性；此外，任务专家激活未被充分利用作为监督信号来分配训练注意力。

Method: TEXAS 首先比较基础模型在成功解决和未能解决的实例上的专家激活，保留在成功实例上激活更强的专家；然后在微调过程中，当失败实例的答案令牌激活这些专家时，对其赋予更高的权重。该方法利用已有的路由行为，不限制专家子集也不强制目标路由分布。

Result: 在三个 MoE 模型和六个基准测试上，TEXAS 在 18 个设置中的 17 个上取得最佳或并列最佳性能，平均超过最强基线 1.3–1.5 个点。消融实验和进一步分析验证了所发现专家和监督策略的有效性。

Conclusion: TEXAS 通过正确性条件化的专家发现和令牌级监督分配，有效提升了 MoE 模型的下游适应性能，且无需改变模型结构或路由机制。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models route each token through a small subset of experts, making routing patterns useful for identifying task-relevant experts during downstream adaptation. Yet current approaches have two limitations: task experts are typically identified from aggregate routing statistics that reflect usage rather than association with successful task completion, and task-expert activations remain underexplored as signals for supervision allocation. We introduce Task-Expert-Aware Supervision (TEXAS), which combines correctness-conditioned task expert discovery with token-level supervision allocation. TEXAS compares expert activations on instances that the base model solves successfully and those it fails to solve, and retains experts more strongly activated on successful instances. During fine-tuning, it upweights answer tokens in failed instances when they activate these experts. TEXAS therefore leverages existing routing behavior without restricting adaptation to a fixed expert subset or imposing an explicit target routing distribution. Across three MoE models and six benchmarks, TEXAS achieves the best or tied-best performance in 17 of 18 settings and improves over the strongest baseline by 1.3--1.5 points on average. Ablations and further analyses validate both the discovered experts and the resulting supervision strategy.

</details>


### [76] [Separating Decision-Rule Misalignment from Readout-Coverage Limitations in Speech Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06409)
*Linkai Peng,Baorian Nuchged*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成对齐的诊断阶梯，用于区分语音语言模型在副语言任务（如情感识别）中从音频到答案计算的不同阶段的失败。通过比较生成答案、选项logits、logits的仿射读出和隐藏状态的线性读出，分离了端点、决策规则和读出覆盖的差距。实验表明，状态解码平均比生成高27.8个准确率点，决策规则和读出覆盖差距在所有条件下均为正。一种无标签的logits修正方法持续提高了生成准确率。情感信息在原生读出之外可泛化到未见说话人，但替换选定的读出外部方向对生成答案影响甚微。这些结果区分了信息可用性与行为使用，并定位了性能损失所在的决策规则和状态到答案的读出环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音语言模型在副语言任务（如情感识别）上通常通过提示答案的准确率来评估，但准确率混淆了音频到答案计算不同阶段的失败。作者希望区分信息可用性与实际行为使用，并定位性能损失的具体环节（决策规则、读出覆盖等）。

Method: 提出一种生成对齐的诊断阶梯（generation-aligned diagnostic ladder），比较四种表征：生成答案、选项logits、logits的仿射读出、隐藏状态的线性读出。通过逐级差异分离端点差距、决策规则差距和读出覆盖差距。此外，提出一种无标签的logits修正方法，并进行了跨说话人泛化、声学描述子控制等分析。

Result: 状态解码准确率平均比生成答案高27.8个百分点；决策规则和读出覆盖差距在所有十种条件下均为正；无标签logits修正持续提高生成准确率；情感信息在原生读出之外可泛化到未见说话人并独立于声学描述子，但替换读出外部方向对生成答案影响很小。

Conclusion: 该诊断阶梯能有效区分信息可用性与行为使用，表明语音语言模型在情感识别任务中，主要性能损失来自决策规则和读出覆盖环节，而非信息缺失；部分决策规则差距可通过logits修正弥补，但模型在行为上未充分利用读出外部的可用情感信息。

Abstract: Speech language models are increasingly evaluated on paralinguistic tasks by the accuracy of prompted answers, but answer accuracy combines failures at different stages of the audio-to-answer computation. We introduce a generation-aligned diagnostic ladder that compares the emitted answer, the option logits, an affine readout of those logits, and a linear readout of the hidden state at the same answer token. Successive differences separate endpoint, decision-rule, and readout-coverage gaps. Across five systems and two emotion corpora, state decoding exceeds generation by 27.8 accuracy points on average, and both the decision-rule and readout-coverage gaps are positive in all ten conditions. A label-free logit correction improves generated accuracy in every condition, showing that part of the decision-rule gap is actionable. In rank-matched comparisons, emotion information outside the native readout generalizes to held-out speakers and survives controls for measured acoustic descriptors, but replacing the selected readout-external directions usually has little effect on emitted answers. These results distinguish information availability from behavioral use and localize performance losses across the decision rule and the state-to-answer readout.

</details>


### [77] [NTDH: Complex Reasoning for Comprehensive Affective Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06425)
*Tianlei Zhu,Zhiwei Liu,Yuyan Wang,Xiao-Yang Liu,Sophia Ananiadou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出NTDH方法，将情感分析重构为复杂推理问题，通过自然化、容忍度门控、领域策略和方向性提示四模块合成高质量推理轨迹，结合SFT+GRPO训练，仅用1.6万样本（约为对比系统的1/14）即达到SOTA性能（EI-reg皮尔逊相关性0.862）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感分析面临两大挑战：任务异质性（连续/有序/多标签输出）和语境依赖性（需调和矛盾线索）。现有方法直接学习从输入到标签的映射，未显式建模调和过程，且合成推理轨迹时存在与情感目标不对齐、丢弃失败案例等问题。

Method: 将情感分析重构为复杂推理问题，统一异构标签空间的输出接口，并生成可优化验证奖励的轨迹。提出NTDH框架：(1) Naturalisation：将训练答案设为金标，保证正确性；(2) Tolerance-aware gate：根据任务评分阈限检查答案；(3) Domain-aware strategies：融入情感科学领域知识；(4) Directional Hints：仅报告错误类型和方向而不暴露目标。基于Qwen3-8B，先SFT后GRPO（验证容忍度与构造门一致）。

Result: 使用16,302条训练记录（约为对比指令微调系统的1/14），最终策略在五个官方测试指标上超越SFT检查点，EI-reg任务取得最强结果，皮尔逊相关性达0.862。

Conclusion: 将情感分析视为复杂推理问题并合成高质量推理轨迹是有效的，NTDH的四个组件各自贡献数据质量提升，该方法在数据效率上具有显著优势。

Abstract: Comprehensive affective analysis is challenging for two reasons: it spans heterogeneous prediction tasks with continuous, ordinal, and multi-label outputs, and affective meaning is context-dependent, requiring conflicting cues to be reconciled rather than mapped directly to labels. Existing methods learn this mapping directly and do not model the reconciliation explicitly. We recast the task as a complex-reasoning problem, which yields one output interface across heterogeneous label spaces and a trajectory over which a verifiable reward can be optimised; to our knowledge, this is the first such treatment covering both sentiment and emotion. The obstacle is on the data side: affective reasoning traces must be synthesised, and generic synthesis is misaligned with the targets, tolerances, and phenomena of affect, and discards or leaks its failure cases. We propose NTDH, which addresses these four failures. Naturalisation sets the training answer to the gold label, so it is correct by construction. A Tolerance-aware gate checks each answer against the task's own scoring margin. Domain-aware strategies refine the reasoning using ideas from affective science. Directional Hints report only the type and direction of an error, without exposing the target. We train Qwen3-8B with SFT and then GRPO under the same tolerance used for verification (up to a more permissive construction gate on the multi-label subtask), and a component ablation quantifies the data-quality effect of each part. Using 16,302 training records, about 14x fewer than comparable instruction-tuned systems, the final policy improves over its SFT checkpoint on five of six official-test metrics and achieves the strongest EI-reg result among the compared systems, at a Pearson correlation of 0.862.

</details>


### [78] [Recovering Lesion Parameters from Aphasic Picture Naming Error Profiles in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06429)
*Yong Yang,Roger Newman-Norlund,Xiang Guan,Saeed Ahmadi,Regan Willis,Nadra Salman,Kalil Warren,Sophie Arheix-Parras,Srihari Nelakuditi,Leonardo Bonilha,Christopher Rorden,Rutvik H. Desai,Julius Fridriksson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆向映射方法：给定大语言模型（LLM）在图片命名任务中的错误剖面，能否恢复产生该行为的损伤参数（层索引、修改百分比、噪声标准差）。通过训练多任务神经网络，发现修改百分比和噪声标准差可恢复，层索引仅能在邻域内恢复；反事实验证中81.4%的案例能复现目标行为，而低层索引恢复率与高反事实保真度之间的分离表明Transformer层存在功能冗余。将模型应用于278名脑卒中患者的错误剖面，恢复参数具有综合征区分性，证实了跨分布泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释性方法仅描述LLM的内部状态，但没有直接检验该状态是否因果充分地产生观察行为。作者希望通过逆向问题——从输出错误剖面反推产生该行为的损伤参数——来揭示Transformer计算的性质，并验证因果充分性。

Method: 对LLaVA-Vicuna 13B模型进行参数化损伤（层索引、修改百分比、噪声标准差），共4840种配置，获得图片命名错误剖面（七类临床分类）。训练一个多任务神经网络，以错误剖面为输入，预测损伤参数。通过10个独立逆向模型评估恢复能力，并进行反事实验证（用恢复参数扰动新模型实例，检查是否复现目标行为）和跨分布测试（应用于脑卒中患者数据）。

Result: 修改百分比和噪声标准差可精确恢复，层索引仅能在一定邻域内恢复；反事实验证中81.4%的案例成功复现目标行为。低层索引恢复与高反事实保真度的分离表明Transformer层存在功能冗余。应用于278名卒中患者的错误剖面时，恢复参数具有综合征区分性，特别是扰动强度维度。

Conclusion: 逆向映射结合反事实验证为LLM可解释性提供了通用框架，能够揭示标准方法无法捕获的层间功能冗余，并具有跨分布泛化能力（如应用于临床神经心理学数据）。

Abstract: Interpretability methods for large language models (LLMs) describe internal state but do not directly test whether that state is causally sufficient to produce the observed behavior. In earlier work, we lesioned LLMs to produce error profiles in picture naming, a central task for assessing aphasia, and found that specific lesions produced errors resembling those of individual stroke survivors. Here we ask the inverse question: given an error profile, can the lesion parameters that produced it be recovered, and what does this inverse problem reveal about transformer computation? Lesions in LLaVA-Vicuna 13B were parameterized by layer index, modification percentage, and noise sigma across 4,840 configurations, and error profiles were characterized by a seven-category clinical taxonomy (correct, semantic, unrelated, formal, mixed, neologism, no-response). We trained a multi-task neural network to map error profiles back to perturbation parameters. The problem admitted a partial solution: across 10 independently trained inverse models, modification percentage and noise sigma were recoverable, whereas layer index was recoverable only within a neighborhood. In counterfactual validation, a fresh model instance perturbed with the recovered parameters reproduced the target behavior in 81.4% of cases. This dissociation between low layer recovery and high counterfactual fidelity is consistent with functional redundancy across transformer layers, a property not captured by standard interpretability methods. As an out-of-distribution test, we applied the trained model to picture-naming error profiles from 278 stroke survivors; recovered parameters were syndrome-discriminative, most strongly for perturbation intensity, indicating generalization beyond the training distribution. Counterfactual validation provides a general framework for LLM interpretability claims beyond inverse mapping.

</details>


### [79] [Do AI Personas Grow? Analyzing and Benchmarking Personality Evolution in LLM Agents After Life Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06485)
*Ming Wang,Peidong Wang,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Fiona Fui-Hoon Nah,Ee-Peng Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出BFI-Adapt基准，系统评估11种重大生活事件对基于大语言模型的性格智能体（PC-Agents）的人格改变，发现其仅能模拟人类人格动态的平均值而非形状，性格特质偏移幅度通常低于人类效应量，且人格层面离散度压缩3-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PC-Agents在情感支持、社会模拟和角色扮演中越来越重要，需要开发能够在长期交互中保持连贯性的终身智能体，其关键组成部分是人格演变：智能体应在经历不同情境下的生活事件时发生合理的、基于心理学的人格变化。先前研究虽显示LLM人格会在情境扰动下发生偏移，但对这些偏移如何因特质、事件、人物设定和模型而异仍知之甚少。

Method: 以Big Five人格特质为心理测量锚点，研究11种重大生活事件引发的人格变化，引入BFI-Adapt基准用于评分事件诱发人格改变的方向保真度，并在14个模型上排名。通过验证套件（无事件重测噪声、独立改写提示稳定性、与基于场景的行为选择收敛性、无关对话干扰）确认测量轨迹的稳健性。

Result: PC-Agents在四个诊断轴上表现出可测量的特质偏移，有/无人类变化方向记录的事件-特质对偏移率相似；即使偏移方向符合预期，幅度通常低于人类效应量范围；性别和文化区域提示几乎没有调节作用；人格层面离散度相对于人类样本压缩3-4倍。验证表明测量偏移超过无事件重测噪声，在独立改写提示下保持稳定，与场景行为选择存在有限且模型依赖的收敛，并在无关对话后持续存在。

Conclusion: 当前PC-Agents模拟了人类人格动态的均值，但未能模拟其形状（分布形态）。BFI-Adapt基准为系统比较事件诱发人格变化提供了可复用的评估工具。

Abstract: Personality-conditioned LLM agents (PC-Agents) are increasingly used in emotional support, social simulation, and role-playing, motivating the development of lifelong agents that remain coherent over extended interactions. A key component of such coherence is personality evolution: agents should undergo plausible, psychology-grounded changes as they experience life events in different contexts. Although prior work shows that LLM personalities can shift under contextual perturbations, how these shifts vary across traits, events, personas, and models remains poorly understood. We study event-induced personality change after 11 major life events, using the Big Five traits as a psychometric anchor and interpreting the resulting trajectories against longitudinal evidence from human personality psychology. Across four diagnostic axes, PC-Agents exhibit measurable trait shifts at similar rates for event-trait pairs with and without documented human change directions. Even when shifts follow the expected direction, their magnitudes usually fall below human effect-size ranges. Gender and cultural-region prompts show little moderating effect, while persona-level dispersion is compressed three- to four-fold relative to human samples. To enable systematic comparison, we introduce BFI-Adapt, a reusable benchmark for scoring the directional fidelity of event-induced personality change, and use it to rank 14 models. A validation suite shows that the measured shifts exceed no-event retest noise, remain stable under independently paraphrased prompts, exhibit limited and model-dependent convergence with scenario-based behavioral choices, and persist across intervening unrelated dialogue. Together, these checks establish the measured trajectories as robust event-conditioned response patterns. Our results suggest that current PC-Agents simulate the mean of human personality dynamics, but not its shape.

</details>


### [80] [ConstructCIE: A Dataset for Extracting Causal Information from Construction Accident Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06495)
*Hung Nguyen,Jaehoon Lee,Namgyun Kim,Kuan-Hao Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 ConstructCIE，一个用于从 OSHA 建筑事故报告中提取因果信息的人工标注数据集，并评估了序列标注器和指令微调的大语言模型在端到端层级提取任务上的表现。结果表明模型能较好地预测事故类型和恢复因果大意，但在精确的跨度级提取上仍有限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑事故叙述包含丰富的因果信息，但证据通常是隐式、长跨度且分布式的，现有方法难以准确提取这些因果关系，因此需要构建专门的数据集和评估基准。

Method: 构建了 ConstructCIE 数据集，采用事故类型、因果因素、子因果因素和支持证据跨度的层级标注模式。评估了监督序列标注器和指令微调的大语言模型在端到端层级因果信息提取任务上的表现。

Result: 大多数模型在事故类型预测上表现良好，能恢复粗粒度的因果意义，但在精确的跨度级提取上仍有限。JHE 在精确匹配和软匹配上更强，IHE 有时在关键词 F1 上更高。错误分布在提取策略上有所不同，但证据选择和跨度边界错误仍然普遍。

Conclusion: 要实现可靠的建筑事故因果信息提取，需要更强的领域基础（domain grounding）和更准确的证据提取能力。

Abstract: Construction accident narratives contain rich causal information, but the evidence is often implicit, long-span, and distributed. We introduce ConstructCIE, a manually annotated dataset for Causal Information Extraction from OSHA construction accident reports. The dataset uses a hierarchical schema for accident types, causal factors, sub-causal factors, and supporting evidence spans. We evaluate supervised sequence taggers and instruction-tuned LLMs in an end-to-end hierarchical extraction setting. Results show that most evaluated models achieve strong accident-type prediction and recover broad causal meaning but remain limited in precise span-level extraction. JHE generally achieves stronger exact and soft matching, while IHE sometimes achieves higher keyword F1. Error distributions vary by extraction strategy, but evidence-selection and span-boundary errors remain common. These findings show that reliable Causal Information Extraction for construction accidents requires stronger domain grounding and more accurate evidence extraction.

</details>


### [81] [Measuring the Cross-Lingual Comprehension Gap: How the language of the evidence shapes what language models understand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06506)
*Rafael da Silva,Jeff Eicher*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出跨语言理解差距（CLCG）概念，利用专业人工翻译的平行语料库ParallelQA-18，在18种语言上评估5个模型。发现模型在非英语语言上的回答质量平均下降约17%（CLCG=0.078），且低资源语言差距更大。英语中心评估会高估低资源语言用户的实际体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多语言基准测试在比较不同语言时未能隔离语言变量本身，通常假设模型在英语中展现的能力在其他语言中同样可用。作者旨在量化同一内容从英语转换为目标语言时模型回答质量的下降程度，揭示英语中心评估的偏差。

Method: 使用ParallelQA-18（专业人工翻译的平行语料库），涵盖18种语言（英语为参考，葡萄牙语为高资源基线，16个目标语言涵盖Joshi资源类别0-4）。采用项目内设计（仅改变语段语言），对150篇文章进行分层抽样。主要估计量是比较英语与目标语言在更高复杂度开放性问题上的Token-F1微平均，使用文章聚类自助法（bootstrap）区间估计。

Result: 主要合并CLCG为0.078（95% CI 0.072-0.084），相对英语分数下降约17%；等语言宏观汇总为0.077。扣除葡萄牙语后宏观差距为0.016（95% CI 0.013-0.020）。语言层面的CLCG与Joshi资源类别负相关（rho = -0.594, p = 0.015）。人工评估中，高资源语言的回答在61.6%的决定性判断中被偏好（估计偏好概率0.655）。

Conclusion: 英语中展现的能力不应假定能同等迁移到其他语言；英语中心的评估可能高估低资源语言用户的实际模型质量。需要在多语言评估中更细致地考虑语言差异。

Abstract: Language models are often evaluated as though capabilities demonstrated in English remain equally available when the same content is presented in other languages. Traditional multilingual benchmarks rarely isolate language while holding content, question, reference answer, model, and evaluation unit constant. We define the Cross-Lingual Comprehension Gap (CLCG) as the reduction in response quality when the same content and question are presented in a target language rather than in English.
  Using ParallelQA-18, a professionally human-translated parallel corpus, we evaluate five models from five laboratories on a stratified sample of 150 articles across 18 languages (English reference; Portuguese high-resource baseline; 16 targets spanning Joshi et al. 2020 classes 0-4). A within-item design varies only passage language. The primary estimator contrasts English versus pooled target-language Token-F1 micro-means on higher-complexity open-ended questions, with article-cluster bootstrap intervals.
  The primary pooled CLCG is 0.078 (95% CI 0.072-0.084), about a 17% reduction relative to the English score; the equal-language macro summary is 0.077. Net of Portuguese, the macro gap is 0.016 (95% CI 0.013-0.020). Language-level CLCG is negatively associated with Joshi resource class (rho = -0.594, p = 0.015, n = 16). In blinded paired human evaluations, higher-resource responses are preferred in 61.6% of decisive judgments (estimated preference probability 0.655, 95% CI 0.558-0.741).
  Capabilities shown in English should not be assumed to transfer equally to other languages; English-centered evaluations may overestimate quality for users of low-resource languages.

</details>


### [82] [GRASP: Reinforcing Language Model Anonymizers with Group Relative Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06526)
*Sajjad Ghiasvand,Nader Sehatbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP方法，通过组相对策略优化（GRPO）在线训练一个本地的小型语言模型（Llama-3.1-8B），使其同时扮演匿名化器、攻击者和效用评判者，在保护隐私（隐藏年龄、位置、职业等敏感属性）的同时保持文本语义。该方法无需将私有文本发送给第三方，完全在本地设备运行，成本仅为GPT-4o教师的约1%，在隐私-效用权衡上优于基于DPO蒸馏的基线，并与前沿模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude）相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型能够从普通文本中推断出敏感的个人属性（如年龄、位置、职业），这使得日常写作成为隐私风险。现有的对抗性匿名化方法虽然能通过改写文本来防御，但需要强大的模型在推理时运行，从而需要将私有文本发送给第三方，这恰恰是匿名化应该防止的暴露。最近的蒸馏工作（SFT+DPO）虽然将行为转移到本地小模型，但DPO仅模仿教师的离线选择，从未直接优化我们关心的隐私-效用目标。

Method: 提出GRASP（Group-Relative Anonymization via Self-refinement Policy-optimization），使用组相对策略优化（GRPO）在线强化本地匿名化模型。单个小模型同时扮演匿名化器、攻击者和效用评判者，基于自生成奖励进行训练——该奖励在隐藏属性的同时保持语义，并设计了防止奖励黑客攻击的机制。模型基于Llama-3.1-8B训练。

Result: GRASP在隐私-效用权衡上持续优于基于DPO蒸馏的基线，经三个独立的LLM评判者验证。与由前沿模型（Gemini 2.5 Flash和Claude）驱动的对抗性匿名化相比，GRASP实现了相当或更好的总体权衡，同时去除了显著更多的隐私信息，并且完全在本地设备运行，成本约为GPT-4o教师的1%。

Conclusion: GRASP通过在线策略优化（GRPO）克服了DPO蒸馏的局限性，使小型本地模型能够在保护隐私和保持语义之间实现更好的平衡。该方法不仅比基于前沿模型的对抗性匿名化更经济（1%成本）且更私密（本地运行），还能去除更多隐私信息，为实际部署中的隐私保护提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models can infer sensitive personal attributes, such as age, location, and occupation, from ordinary text, turning everyday writing into a privacy risk. Adversarial anonymization defends against this by rewriting a text with a capable language model that also plays the attacker, but it needs a powerful model at inference time and thus sends private text to a third party, the very exposure anonymization should prevent. Recent work distills this behavior into a small on-device model using supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization (DPO), but DPO only imitates the teacher's offline choices and never directly optimizes the privacy--utility objective we care about. We introduce \textbf{GRASP} (\textbf{G}roup-\textbf{R}elative \textbf{A}nonymization via \textbf{S}elf-refinement \textbf{P}olicy-optimization), which reinforces the local anonymizer online with Group Relative Policy Optimization. A single small model acts as anonymizer, adversary, and utility judge, trained against a self-generated reward that hides attributes while preserving meaning, with a design that guards against reward hacking. Trained on Llama-3.1-8B, \ours{} improves the privacy--utility trade-off over the DPO-distilled baseline, consistently across three independent LLM judges. Against adversarial anonymization driven by frontier models such as Gemini~2.5~Flash and Claude, it achieves a comparable or better overall trade-off while removing substantially more private information, and it runs entirely on-device at roughly $1\%$ of the GPT-4o teacher's cost.

</details>


### [83] [Lost in Interpolation: Why Predictive Feedback Fails in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06529)
*Lavanya Nigam,Ishaan Bansal,Aryan Sood,Vidit Aggarwal,Gaurav Kumar Nayak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper identifies that Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) treat embedding space as Euclidean via linear interpolation (LERP), but the embedding geometry is actually hyperspherical. They propose Spherical Soft-Masking (S-SM) using Fréchet mean and spherical linear interpolation (SLERP), which improves convergence, MAUVE scores, and generative perplexity without changing entropy or convergence speed.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing MDLMs use linear interpolation (LERP) in raw embedding space, implicitly assuming Euclidean geometry. However, analysis shows that mask and predicted-token embeddings maintain a near-constant angle (~73°) and norms are flat across frequency rank, indicating a hyperspherical geometry. LERP is inappropriate for such geometry, leading to training degradation.

Method: Spherical Soft-Masking (S-SM) replaces LERP with spherical operations: it aggregates top-k predictions using Fréchet mean on the hypersphere, blends this mean with the mask direction using spherical linear interpolation (SLERP), and then restores the native mask norm. This is a drop-in replacement for existing soft-masking.

Result: S-SM achieves MAUVE gains up to 2x over vanilla MDLM baseline and 27.5-56.1% over TopK/LERP at various sampling budgets, with lower generative perplexity (16.9-19.6% over baseline), while output entropy and convergence remain essentially unchanged. SLERP feedback avoids training degradation that LERP feedback induces.

Conclusion: The hyperspherical geometry of MDLM embeddings requires spherical interpolation rather than linear Euclidean interpolation. S-SM provides consistent improvements across inference budgets and avoids degradation, making it a better soft-masking strategy for MDLMs.

Abstract: Soft-masking accelerates the convergence of Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs). Existing formulations build this blend with linear interpolation (LERP) in the raw embedding space, which implicitly treats that space as Euclidean. We analyze the embedding space of MDLMs and find that the mask and predicted-token embeddings maintain a near-constant angle of (\approx 73^\circ) throughout training, while embedding norms remain essentially flat across vocabulary-frequency rank. These indicate a hyperspherical geometry, for which LERP is the wrong interpolation primitive. We introduce Spherical Soft-Masking (S-SM), a drop-in replacement that aggregates the top-(k) predictions with a Fr'echet mean on the hypersphere and blends this mean with the mask direction using spherical linear interpolation (SLERP), then restores the native mask norm. We evaluate S-SM on continued pre-training of a released 169M-parameter MDLM checkpoint across a wide range of inference-time step budgets, SLERP feedback avoids the training degradation that LERP feedback induces and delivers MAUVE gains of up to 2x over the vanilla MDLM baseline and 27.5-56.1% over TopK/LERP at various sampling budgets, alongside consistently lower generative perplexity (16.9-19.6% over the baseline), while leaving output entropy and convergence essentially unchanged.

</details>


### [84] [Confidence Estimation for Financial Vision-Language Models in Chart and Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06532)
*Reza Khanmohammadi,Simerjot Kaur,Charese H. Smiley,Ivan Brugere,Mohammad M. Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LVLM在金融图表理解中的信任问题，评估7种置信度估计器（3种推理型、4种探针型）在5个开源LVLM和3个金融VQA基准上的表现，发现（1）校准比排序更稀缺，仅探针型可产生阈值分数；（2）可靠性按模型和任务结构化，不存在全局最优；（3）在错误预算下自动化量首先取决于模型能力，其次才是置信度；其中仅基于grounding的探针能区分模型是否真正使用了图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融领域中LVLM误读图表可能导致错误决策，因此需要区分哪些答案可被信任执行、哪些需人工复核，即从追求准确率转向评估置信度的可靠性。

Method: 选取7种置信度估计器（3种推理型baseline和4种内部探针），在5个开源LVLM上，使用3个金融VQA基准（含双语）的4种条件进行评估；所有探针仅在自然图像上训练后零适配应用于金融领域，衡量分布外迁移能力。

Result: （1）推理型baseline排序能力尚可但严重过度自信，校准误差远超可阈值化范围，仅探针型能产生可设阈值的分数；（2）最优估计器随模型和任务变化，无全局领先者；双语对比表明语言鲁棒性可能是组合伪象；（3）在5%严格错误预算下，自动化比例在最简单条件下可观，在最难条件下接近零；仅两个探针提供校准，其中grounding感知探针能在模型未使用图表时降低置信度。

Conclusion: 金融LVLM的信任决策应依赖探针型置信度估计器而非推理型baseline；部署时需根据具体模型和任务选择估计器，且自动化程度受限于模型本身能力；grounding感知探针是识别模型是否真正使用了图表的有效工具。

Abstract: LVLMs are increasingly used to read financial charts, tables, and documents, where a single misread figure can move a decision and the most authoritative-looking answer is sometimes one the model produced without reading the exhibit. The operational question is therefore trust, not accuracy: which answers can be acted on, and which escalated to a reviewer. We evaluate seven confidence estimators, three inference-only and four trained internal probes, across five open-weight LVLMs and four conditions from three financial visual question-answering benchmarks, one bilingual; every probe is trained only on natural images and applied to finance without adaptation, so the results measure out-of-distribution transfer. Three findings hold. First, the scarce property is calibration, not ranking: the inference baselines rank correct above incorrect answers competitively but are badly overconfident, calibration error far above what a threshold can tolerate, and only the trained probes produce a thresholdable score. Second, reliability is structured rather than global, along two axes a practitioner can read directly: the best estimator shifts with both model and task, none leading more than eight of twenty (model, condition) cells, and a controlled bilingual contrast exposes an apparent language robustness as a composition artifact that dissolves once models are read one at a time. Third, cast as deferral under an error budget, how much can be safely automated is set first by the model's competence and only narrowed by its confidence, so deferral clears a real share of the easiest condition and almost none of the hardest, near zero at a strict 5% budget. Two trained probes carry the calibration a deferral policy needs, and among them only the grounding-aware one lowers its confidence on answers a model gives without using the figure, separating detected non-grounding from a fluent guess.

</details>


### [85] [Don't `Well, Actually' Me Unless You Know What You're Talking About: Weak Presupposition Verification Degrades General QA Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06539)
*Shenran Wang,Vered Shwartz,Hila Gonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了虚假预设问答（FPQA）任务，发现现有评测过分关注含有虚假预设的问题（FPQ）而忽略正常问题（TPQ），且基准测试中FPQ比例远高于自然分布，导致评测结果无法反映真实问答性能。实验表明，在FPQ上表现更好的方法往往在TPQ上表现更差，原因是它们使用了脆弱的事实检验模块，容易错误地拒绝正确的预设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前FPQA评测主要针对虚假预设问题（FPQ），忽略了正常问题（TPQ）的性能；同时基准测试中FPQ比例远高于自然分布，导致评测结果不能反映真实场景。此外，那些在FPQ上表现更好的方法往往在TPQ上表现更差，这揭示了现有方法的缺陷，需要被分析和改进。

Method: 通过跨多个模型家族、模型大小和基准测试的广泛实验，对比不同方法在FPQ和TPQ上的表现，并分析其内在原因。

Result: 在FPQ上表现更好的方法在TPQ上表现更差；原因是这些方法中的事实检验模块过于脆弱，不仅拒绝了虚假预设，也错误地拒绝了正确的预设。

Conclusion: 希望这些发现能指导未来FPQA方法的设计，使其能在更现实的场景中具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: False-presupposition QA (FPQA) tests LLMs on their ability to identify false presuppositions in questions and abstain or correct them rather than reinforcing false assumptions. The common approach reduces the task to prompting LLMs to extract presuppositions and fact checking each presupposition. While the performance on dedicated benchmarks keeps improving, evaluation largely focuses on questions with false presuppositions (FPQs) while ignoring the performance on ``normal'' questions (TPQs). Since many benchmarks over-represent FPQs compared to their natural occurrence, the result is that performance on these benchmarks doesn't reflect real-world QA performance. Through extensive experiments across various model families, sizes, and benchmarks, we show that methods that perform better on FPQs tend to perform worse on TPQs. Our analysis reveals this is the result of weak fact checking modules that reject also true presuppositions. We hope our findings will help guide future work toward FPQA methods that generalize well to realistic settings.

</details>


### [86] [TradeVerse: A Longitudinal Benchmark of Political Negotiation in International Trade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06549)
*Debodeep Banerjee,Amitangshu Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TradeVerse 是一个基于 WTO 贸易争端会议记录构建的基准测试，包含 1170 场会议记录，涵盖 5 个商品组和 89 个产品组，设计了三个纵向理解任务（预测 HS 编码、猜测回应国、生成最后一轮声明），旨在评估 LLM 对多轮政治贸易谈判的理解能力，实验表明当前 LLM 在该基准上存在显著挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估 LLM 在孤立文档或单一任务上的表现，而现实政治谈判是纵向数据，多轮互动中每一轮都依赖于之前的所有轮次。因此需要构建一个能评估 LLM 对纵向贸易谈判理解的基准。

Method: 从 WTO 具体贸易关切会议记录中重建了 1170 场会议记录，分为 5 个商品组和 89 个产品组，定义了三个任务：1) 根据纵向会议记录预测讨论产品的 HS 编码；2) 从匿名会议内容猜测回应国；3) 扮演回应国生成最后一轮声明。所有标签直接从会议记录中提取，无需人工标注。

Result: 实验表明当前 LLM 在 TradeVerse 的三个任务上面临显著挑战，说明现有模型在理解多轮政治贸易谈判方面存在不足。

Conclusion: TradeVerse 是首个评估 LLM 理解纵向政治贸易谈判能力的基准，揭示了当前模型在该领域的局限性，为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly being applied to tasks involving institutional and political texts, but existing benchmarks evaluate them on isolated documents or single tasks. In realpolitik, negotiations are longitudinal data, where participating parties can align or argue over multiple iterations and each turn is an outcome of the previous turns, hence, understanding one turn requires tracking everything before it. We introduce TradeVerse, a benchmark built from the World Trade Organisation (WTO) specific trade concerns, where member states challenge one another and exchange arguments over multiple rounds, sometimes for years. We, in TradeVerse, reconstruct minutes of $1170$ meetings, spanning across 5 groups and $89$ product groups and define three tasks: first, the system has to analyze the longitudinal meeting records and predict the harmonized system codes (HS chapters) of the products under discussion in the particular meeting, second, we examine whether the system, upon analyzing the anonymized content of the meeting, can guess the name of the responding country and third, we ask the system to play the role of the responding country and provide the statement for the very last round. All labels are recovered directly from the proceedings, requiring no manual annotation. Our experiments highlight the challenges these tasks pose for current LLMs. To the best of our knowledge, TradeVerseis the first benchmark to investigate potential of LLMs in understanding longitudinal political trade negotiations.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond "AI Language": The case for the idiolectal nature of LLM output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06589)
*Karolina Rudnicka,Thomas Stephan Juzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大型语言模型的输出并非统一的“AI语言”，而是具有类似人类个人方言（idiolect）的模型特异性语言特征。通过分析2024年和2026年两个时间段的模型生成文本语料，利用计算描述符和文体主成分分析，发现模型之间存在代际风格转变，同时每个模型保持独特的语言轮廓。收缩形式频率等特征在同一代模型内差异巨大（每百万词从1,200到30,000+）。结论认为将LLM输出视为个人方言性质为语言变异与变化、AI文本检测、司法语言学及基于用法的语言研究提供了有价值的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型输出常被统称为“AI语言”，但本文认为这种视角忽略了不同模型之间存在的特异性语言特征。作者旨在论证模型拥有类似人类个人方言（idiolect）的独特语言轮廓，并探究模型间及代际间的语言变异。

Method: 使用两个数据集：2024年六个模型的语料库（Improta et al. 2024）和2026年使用相同提示生成的六个当代模型的新语料库。采用计算描述符和文体主成分分析（stylometric PCA）进行量化比较。

Result: 发现2024与2026代模型之间存在显著代际风格转变；同时每个模型个体保持独特的语言轮廓。例如，2026年同代模型中收缩形式频率差异巨大（每百万词从1,200到30,000以上）。

Conclusion: 将LLM输出视为个人方言性质提供了一个有价值的框架，对语言变异与变化研究、AI生成文本检测、司法语言学以及基于用法的语言研究方法具有重要意义。

Abstract: While large language model outputs are frequently analysed as a collective super variety termed "AI language," this chapter argues that this perspective coexists with distinct, model-specific linguistic signatures akin to human idiolects. We analyse two datasets of LLM-generated texts on societal topics: a 2024 corpus of six models (Improta et al. 2024) and a newly generated 2026 corpus using the same prompts featuring six contemporary models. Our findings, utilising computational descriptors and stylometric principal component analysis reveal a generational shift between the style of the 2024 and 2026 cohorts, while demonstrating that each individual model maintains a unique linguistic profile. This multi-layered interplay is illustrated by contraction frequencies, which vary from over 1,200 to over 30,000 per million words within the same cohort of models (2026). Ultimately, we conclude that treating LLM output as idiolectal in nature provides a valuable framework with potential implications for research on variation and change, LLM-generated text detection, forensic linguistics and usage-based approaches to language.

</details>


### [88] [Pre-Inference Routing for Cost-Efficient Document Field Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06607)
*Sreerekha Rajendran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于文档难度预测的模型路由方法，在文档提取前通过廉价信号预测其难度，从而在轻量模型和强模型之间动态选择。实验表明，路由有效需满足两个条件：轻模型失败频率足够高且失败可预测。在满足条件的场景下（如收据、广告表单），路由在保持F1接近（0.02以内）的同时降低了31-77%的成本；在不满足条件时（如清晰电子发票、营养标签）则无效。路由需每数据集重新训练，不可跨数据集迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档提取系统对所有文档使用单一模型，简单但对简单文档成本过高，对困难文档效果不佳。因此希望根据文档难度动态选择模型，在保证质量的同时降低成本。

Method: 提出路由框架：先通过廉价文档特征（如图像质量、布局）预测文档难度，然后选择使用弱模型还是强模型。设计了两个条件测试（失败频率和失败可预测性），并用F1和成本评估。使用可解释特征（如词袋模型）分析路由适用性，并在五个文档类型上实验。

Result: 路由在收据上降低31-33%成本，在退化广告表单上降低77%成本，F1损失均小于0.02。在清晰数字发票和营养标签等简单文档上无效。词袋路由器与工程特征效果相当，表明主要限制在于文档类型而非路由器设计。路由器需每数据集重新训练，不跨数据集迁移。

Conclusion: 路由是否有效取决于文档类型的两个条件：弱模型失败频率和失败可预测性。可通过小规模标注试点预测路由效果。路由器设计简单即可，关键是文档类型本身的性质。路由不可跨数据集迁移。

Abstract: Most document-extraction systems use a single model for all documents. This is simple but can be costly for easy cases and less effective for difficult ones. We examine whether we can predict a document's difficulty before extraction using inexpensive, document-based signals, and use this to choose between a cheaper and a stronger extractor. We find that routing only helps if two conditions hold: the cheaper model fails often enough to make routing worthwhile, and those failures can be predicted from visible features such as image quality and layout. We turn these into a practical test and apply it to five genres. When both conditions are met, the calibrated router reduces cost by 31-33% on receipts and 77% on degraded ad-buy forms while keeping quality within 0.02 F1 of always choosing the large model. Routing does not help if either condition is missing, as with clean digital invoices or nutrition labels that are already easy to read. A small labeled pilot can predict whether routing will work, and in the two cases where we ran it first, the prediction was correct. A simple bag-of-words router works about as well as engineered features, showing that the main limit is the genre, not the router design; we use interpretable features to help explain which genres can be routed. The router must be retrained for each dataset and does not transfer across datasets, even within the same genre. These results hold for two model pairs with cost differences of 5x and 3x.

</details>


### [89] [Factorized Hypothesis Search for Evidence-to-Taxonomy Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06614)
*Linhai Ma,Ethan F. Wei,Xueqing Peng,Yan Wang,Lingfei Qian,Víctor Gutiérrez-Basulto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了“检索就绪差距”问题：现有大规模分类检索假设输入已直接表达目标概念，但在许多场景中（如表格单元格）输入是间接证据。为此，作者提出了因子化假设搜索（FHS），该方法在命名的语义维度上维护多个部分解释，支持结构化查询构建、多假设检索和维度级候选验证。在金融分类标注和CodiEsp临床编码任务上，FHS在非Oracle方法中取得了最佳的Recall@1、MRR和最终准确率。将因子化假设路径替换为自由文本集成会导致头部排名性能的最大下降，而顺序细化在FHS强大的并行第一轮基础上没有带来额外增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的大规模分类检索通常假设输入已经直接表达了目标概念，但在实际场景中（如表格单元格），输入往往是间接证据，其语义依赖于行、列、数据类型和上下文。这种不匹配称为“检索就绪差距”，导致当语义隐含时，目标概念在检索排名中靠后。

Method: 提出因子化假设搜索（Factorized Hypothesis Search, FHS），该方法在命名的语义维度上维护多个部分解释（假设），支持结构化查询渲染、多假设检索以及维度级候选验证。

Result: 在金融分类标注和CodiEsp临床编码任务上，FHS在非Oracle方法中取得了最优的Recall@1、MRR和最终准确率。将因子化假设路径替换为自由文本集成导致头部排名性能下降最大，而顺序细化在FHS的并行第一轮基础上没有额外收益。

Conclusion: 因子化假设方法优于其他替代方案，且并行第一轮检索已足够有效，无需顺序细化。

Abstract: Large-taxonomy retrieval often assumes that the input already expresses the target concept. In many settings, however, the input is indirect evidence, such as a table cell whose meaning depends on its row, column, datatype, and context. We call this mismatch the retrieval readiness gap. Our analysis shows that the current index retrieves the target reliably when its semantics are explicit, while raw evidence often leaves it deep in the ranking. We propose Factorized Hypothesis Search (FHS), which maintains multiple partial interpretations over named semantic dimensions. These hypotheses support structured query rendering, multi-hypothesis retrieval, and dimension-level candidate verification. On both financial taxonomy tagging and CodiEsp clinical coding tasks, FHS achieves the best Recall@1, MRR, and final accuracy among the non-oracle methods. Replacing the factorized hypothesis path with a free-text ensemble causes the largest drop in head-ranking performance, while sequential refinement provides no additional gain over FHS's strong parallel first round.

</details>


### [90] [Discovering Conceptual Metaphors Across Topics and Media Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06652)
*Alexandria Leto,Rohan Das,Juan Vásquez,Abram Handler,Maria Leonor Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督方法，从语料库中提取语言隐喻，并通过结构化聚类形成概念隐喻组，用于揭示说话者或作者的框架。通过分析左右倾向播客的隐喻使用，发现左派倾向于将媒体故事概念化为武器，右派则常将经济视为受垂直变化的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念隐喻引导思维和行动，不同的概念隐喻导致不同的推理。识别概念隐喻有助于揭示对事件的框架，但隐喻无法直接观察，需要从语言隐喻中推断。因此，需要一种自动方法从语料中提取并聚类语言隐喻，以揭示概念隐喻及其框架差异。

Method: 提出无监督方法：从语料库中提取语言隐喻，然后使用结构化聚类方法将提取的语言隐喻分组，形成对应概念隐喻的组。

Result: 应用该方法分析左右倾向播客，发现左派播客常将媒体故事概念化为武器，右派播客常将经济视为受垂直变化的系统，揭示了关键主题和框架差异。

Conclusion: 该无监督方法能有效识别概念隐喻及其框架差异，为理解不同政治倾向的思维方式提供工具。

Abstract: Conceptual metaphors guide our thinking and actions by allowing us to reason about more abstract experiences (e.g., paying taxes) in terms of more concrete or embodied experiences (e.g., carrying a physical load) (Lakoff and Johnson, 2011). It follows that different conceptual metaphors can result in different reasoning: framing paying taxes as an investment in a community rather than a physical load leads to a very different outlook on taxation. Identifying the conceptual metaphors guiding a speaker or writer thus helps to reveal their framing of events. Though these metaphors can't be observed directly, groups of linguistic metaphors, metaphorical expressions as they appear in language, serve as evidence for them. Motivated by this, we present an unsupervised method that extracts linguistic metaphors from a corpus and uses a structured clustering approach to form groups corresponding to conceptual metaphors. Using this method, we point to key topical and framing differences in left- vs. right-leaning podcasts. For example, left-leaning podcasts tend to conceptualize media stories as a weapon, while right-leaning sources commonly discuss the economy as a system subject to vertical changes.

</details>


### [91] [The Horizon Gap: Planning, Memory, Execution, Training, and Evaluation for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06663)
*Mingguang Chen,Licheng Wang,Bo Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文对 2024–2026 年间的 1,547 篇 arXiv 论文进行了系统性调研，聚焦大语言模型在长程任务（long-horizon task）中表现出的“地平线差距”（horizon gap）：模型在需要多步推理的长时间任务中容易丢失目标、过早宣布完成或偏离轨道。论文区分了三种常被混淆的属性——长程任务（所需步骤数）、长上下文（模型 token 容量）和长期记忆（跨步骤/会话的持久性），并按任务生命周期将文献分为六个类别（规划、记忆、执行、训练、评估、基础/安全）以及三种承载形式（上下文内、任务内超上下文、跨任务持久）。主要发现是：随着任务长度增加，仅依靠结果（outcome-only）信号变得不再有效，领域内应对措施（如过程奖励模型、信用分配、轨迹级诊断）本质上都在制造更密集的步骤级信号。论文还将批评性和诊断性文献作为第一类线索贯穿全文，并指出了三个未解决的测量问题：分解模型能力与框架能力、管理训练与评估中过程级信号的共偏置、以及长程可靠性是否存在通用预测理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的前沿语言模型在单步推理上表现出色，但在需要多步、长时间执行的任务（如编程、科研）中频繁失败：丢失对先前决策的跟踪、将未完成的工作声明为完成、逐渐偏离初始目标。这种现象被称为“地平线差距”（horizon gap）。同时，学术界长期混淆“长程任务”（long-horizon）、“长上下文”（long-context）和“长期记忆”（long-term memory）三个概念，导致研究目标和评估标准不清晰。因此，有必要对该领域进行系统梳理，明确概念边界，并识别当前方法的核心模式与瓶颈。

Method: 采用系统性种子采集（systematic seed harvest）策略，结合已披露 26.8% 的泄漏过滤（bleed filter）和针对性补充，收集了 2024–2026 年间的 1,547 篇 arXiv 论文。将文献按长程任务的生命周期分为六个类别：规划（planning）、记忆（memory）、执行（execution）、训练（training）、评估（evaluation）、基础与安全（foundations/safety），并根据承载水平（horizon locality）交叉分类为三种：上下文内（within-context）、任务内但超上下文（within-task-beyond-context）、跨任务持久（cross-task-persistent）。此外，将批评性（critical）和诊断性（diagnostic）文献作为第一类主线纳入分析，而非将它们与主方法割裂。

Result: 在所有类别中均观察到相同的模式：随着任务水平延长，仅依赖最终结果的信号（outcome-only signals）信息量递减，整个领域的主流应对方法——无论是过程奖励模型（process reward models）、信用分配（credit assignment）还是轨迹级诊断（trajectory-level diagnostics）——本质上都在制造更密集的步骤级信号（denser step-level signals）。论文还提出了三个开放测量问题：（1）如何分解模型自身能力与外部框架（harness）的能力；（2）如何管理过程级信号在训练和评估中被同时使用时产生的共偏置（correlated bias）；（3）长程可靠性是否能够形成通用的预测理论。

Conclusion: 长程任务可靠性的提升需要从结果级信号转向更细粒度的步骤级信号，这是当前所有成功方法的共同本质。未来的关键挑战在于建立能够区分模型与框架贡献的测量体系、避免过程级信号的双重使用带来的评估污染，以及探索长程可靠性是否存在统一的理论预测框架。批评性和诊断性研究不应被边缘化，而应与主流方法并置，以形成更完整的领域图景。

Abstract: Frontier language models solve reasoning problems in a single forward pass that would have been research contributions years ago, yet fail at multi-hour tasks: losing track of earlier decisions, declaring half-finished work done, or drifting from goals. We call this the horizon gap and survey 1,547 arXiv papers (2024-2026) collected via systematic seed harvest with a disclosed 26.8% bleed filter, extended by targeted supplementation. We disambiguate three routinely conflated properties: long-horizon (task property: required steps), long-context (model property: token capacity), and long-term memory (system property: persistence across steps/sessions). We organize the corpus into six categories tracking a long-horizon task's lifecycle -- planning, memory, execution, training, evaluation, and foundations/safety -- crossed with an axis capturing where horizons are carried (within-context, within-task-beyond-context, or cross-task-persistent). Across all categories, we find the same pattern: outcome-only signals grow uninformative as horizons lengthen, and the field's response -- whether process reward models, credit assignment, or trajectory-level diagnostics -- manufactures denser step-level signals. We treat critical and diagnostic literature as first-class threads throughout, arguing that segregating critique from method would routinely split single papers across chapters. We close by naming open measurement problems: decomposing model versus harness capability, managing correlated bias in process-level signals used for both training and evaluation, and whether long-horizon reliability admits general predictive theory.

</details>


### [92] [TA-RAG: Tone Awareness as a Design Imperative for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06672)
*Yong-Bin Kang,Anthony McCosker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tone-Aware RAG (TA-RAG) 框架，旨在解决标准 RAG 系统中因检索文档自带专业/学术风格而忽略用户语气要求的"上下文解耦"问题。TA-RAG 将沟通对齐（无污名语言、可读性对齐、接收者敏感适应、共情框架）与事实准确性并列为核心设计目标，并在检索、上下文构建、生成和约束验证阶段加以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 系统存在结构性缺陷：检索到的文档带有自身的沟通风格（专业术语、正式语调等），这些风格在系统处理任何语气指令之前就已经塑造了系统行为，导致系统忽略用户对特定语气的请求。作者将这一现象称为"上下文解耦"（contextual decoupling），即系统优化了事实准确性却与接收者的社会或操作语境脱节。基于公共卫生同伴支持社区的先前研究，他们识别出三种沟通错位（语言、认知、关系），这些错位即使在检索相关且生成事实准确时仍可能持续存在，构成了"沟通转换失败"（failures of communicative transformation），而传统的准确性中心评估指标无法捕捉这些问题。

Method: TA-RAG 是一个概念性架构框架，将沟通对齐（communicative alignment）与事实准确性并列为核心设计目标。该框架在 RAG 管道的四个阶段（检索、上下文构建、生成、约束验证）中操作化四个约束：（1）无污名语言（stigma-free language）、（2）可读性对齐（readability alignment）、（3）接收者敏感适应（recipient-sensitive adaptation）、（4）共情框架（empathetic framing）。作者还提出了一个联合评估事实忠实度和沟通对齐的评估议程。

Result: 提出了 TA-RAG 概念框架，识别了 RAG 系统中由文档风格引起的上下文解耦问题，以及三种沟通错位类型（语言、认知、关系）。该框架操作化了四个沟通对齐约束，并给出了联合评估事实准确性与沟通对齐的评估方向。论文还指出了该领域存在的开放挑战。

Conclusion: 语气感知（tone awareness）不应被视为可选的优化，而应成为运行在社会敏感和高风险场景中的 RAG 系统的当前设计要务（present design imperative）。TA-RAG 框架为将沟通对齐纳入 RAG 系统设计提供了概念基础和操作路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a robust architecture for grounding large language models (LLMs) in trusted knowledge. However, standard RAG systems exhibit a structural limitation: retrieved documents carry their own communication styles-professional jargon, formal tone, or academic writings-that shape the behavior of a RAG system before any tone instructions are processed, often causing the system to ignore user requests for a specific tone. We term this phenomenon contextual decoupling, in which a system optimises for factual accuracy while remaining decoupled from the social or operational context of the recipient. Building on prior research in public health peer-support communities, we identify three communicative misalignment-linguistic, cognitive, and relational-that can persist even when retrieval is relevant and the generated response is factually accurate. We conceptualise these as failures of communicative transformation, which remain largely invisible to accuracy-centred RAG evaluation metrics. To address this gap, we propose Tone-Aware RAG (TA-RAG), a conceptual architectural framework that positions communicative alignment alongside factual accuracy as a core design objective. TA-RAG operationalises four constraints-stigma-free language, readability alignment, recipient-sensitive adaptation, and empathetic framing-across the retrieval, context construction, generation, and constraint validation phases in the proposed RAG pipeline. We further highlight an evaluation agenda for jointly assessing factual fidelity and communicative alignment, and identify open challenges. We argue that tone awareness should be treated not as an optional refinement, but as a present design imperative for RAG systems operating in socially sensitive and high-stakes contexts.

</details>


### [93] [Do Audio Language Models Use Paralinguistic Evidence? Counterfactual Audits for Response Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06718)
*Kevin Miller,Arjun Chandra,Venkatesh Saligrama*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种反事实审计方法，用于检测音频语言模型（ALM）作为语音系统评判者时是否真正使用了副语言线索（如情感、韵律、时机），而非仅依赖词汇内容。实验发现对比成功率高估了原生可靠性，且相似的准确率可能掩盖不同的失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频语言模型（ALM）被越来越多地用作语音到语音系统的评判者，但接收音频的评判者可能并未实际利用副语言证据（如情感、韵律）。需要确保评判者真正关注音频线索而非词汇内容或响应风格。

Method: 引入反事实审计：每个审计项固定转录文本，同时改变情感、韵律或情感转变的时机，迫使有效的评判者跟踪音频线索而非词汇内容。使用原生单上下文判断协议和对比可恢复性控制进行评估，进一步将每个项分解为感知和响应映射技能，产生诊断状态以识别不同的失败来源。

Result: 在Gemini、GPT和开放音频模型上，对比成功率往往高估了原生评判者的可靠性，相似的总体准确率可能隐藏不同的失败模式。

Conclusion: ALM评判者不应仅通过准确率来评估，而需要在部署前进行彻底的行为审计。

Abstract: Audio-language models (ALMs) are increasingly used as judges for speech-to-speech systems, but a judge that receives audio may not actually use paralinguistic evidence. We introduce counterfactual audits for paralinguistic response evaluation. Each audit item holds the transcript fixed while varying affect, prosody, or the timing of an affective shift, forcing a valid judge to track the audio cue rather than lexical content or response style. We evaluate ALM judges using a native one-context judgment protocol and a contrastive recoverability control, then further decompose each item into its constituent perception and response-mapping skills. This yields useful diagnostic states that identify different sources of judge failures. Across Gemini, GPT, and open audio models, we find that contrastive success often overstates native judge reliability, and that similar aggregate accuracies can hide different failure modes. These results suggest that ALM judges should not be evaluated by accuracy alone, instead requiring thorough behavioral audits before deployment.

</details>


### [94] [Progressive Content Refinement with Decaying Reward Joint LinUCB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06750)
*Shion Ishikawa,Pablo Loyola,Young-joo Chung,Yun Ching Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对迭代细化中静态选项导致的过度利用问题（相同提示/臂的奖励随时间递减），提出了一种融合奖励衰减建模的上下文赌博机算法，通过EM算法同时估计臂特定参数与衰减参数，并将提示作为臂嵌入以实现联合学习，在情感反转和GSM8K基准上取得显著性能提升，消融实验证实衰减建模对缓解过度利用至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迭代细化方法（如基于反馈的Self-Refine或传统赌博机方法）依赖静态选项或忽略饱和效应，导致持续使用相同提示或臂时奖励递减（过度利用），亟需一种能显式建模奖励衰减的机制来优化迭代细化过程。

Method: 提出一种新型上下文赌博机算法，显式引入奖励衰减建模。利用期望最大化（EM）算法同时估计臂特定参数和衰减参数；将提示作为臂嵌入，实现臂值的联合学习，区别于传统的分离式线性上置信界（LinUCB）框架。

Result: 在情感反转（Sentiment Reversal）和GSM8K基准测试上，所提方法显著优于强基线方法；消融实验进一步表明，赌博机框架中集成奖励衰减建模对缓解过度利用和优化迭代细化过程至关重要。

Conclusion: 奖励衰减建模与上下文赌博机算法的结合能有效解决迭代细化中的过度利用问题，提升LLM性能，为动态提示优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: Iterative refinement has significantly enhanced Large Language Model (LLM) performance; however, existing methods ranging from feedback-based Self-Refine to traditional bandit approaches often rely on static options or overlook the saturation effect. This neglect leads to over-exploitation, where the continuous use of identical prompts or arms results in diminishing rewards over time.
  To address this challenge, we propose a novel contextual bandit algorithm that explicitly incorporates reward decay modeling. Utilizing an Expectation-Maximization (EM) algorithm, our method simultaneously estimates both arm-specific and decay parameters.
