While the optimal policies and value functions can differ significantly depending on the trade-offs, MORL algorithms commonly represent them with simple feedforward networks conditioned on the trade-off. This raises the question of whether the performance of the algorithms could be improved with more expressive function approximators. In this paper, we integrate recent advances in neural network design: (i) observation and feature normalization, (ii) weight normalization, and (iii) modeling of distributional returns with an entropy-regularized MORL algorithm. The empirical results across standard continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that these changes substantially improve the quality of the produced solution sets without requiring major changes to the underlying algorithm.

</details>


### [193] [Conformal Fusion Under Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07183)
*Alireza Moayedikia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCCF is a multimodal fusion architecture that simultaneously addresses missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty by combining modality dropout training, per-modality evidential heads, Dempster-Shafer fusion with vacuous evidence for absent modalities, and Mondrian conformal calibration, achieving formal coverage guarantees for any subset of available modalities without post-hoc recalibration.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multimodal fusion models typically assume all modalities are available at inference, but real-world scenarios often have missing modalities due to sensor failures, cost constraints, etc. Existing work treats missing modalities as an accuracy problem, ignoring whether confidence estimates remain calibrated when an entire input stream is removed. There is a need to jointly address missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty.

Method: MCCF uses a multimodal bottleneck fusion backbone trained with modality dropout, per-modality evidential heads that output modality-decomposed Dirichlet distributions, a Dempster-Shafer combination rule to fuse per-modality evidence (absent modalities contribute vacuous evidence that is structurally ignored), and a Mondrian conformal calibration module keyed on the modality-presence mask to provide finite-sample group-conditional coverage for every non-empty modality subset.

Result: Across a synthetic problem and three real multimodal benchmarks, MCCF holds its target coverage on every modality-presence subset, substantially narrows the coverage gap between full and partial modalities relative to a marginal split-conformal baseline, and imposes no measurable accuracy cost relative to temperature-scaled and evidential baselines.

Conclusion: MCCF is the first method to provide formal coverage guarantees under arbitrary modality availability through architectural integration rather than post-hoc recalibration, and its evidential decomposition yields per-modality vacuity scores that localize uncertainty to the absent modality responsible.

Abstract: Multimodal fusion architectures typically assume all modalities are available at inference, yet sensor failures, acquisition variability, and cost constraints routinely produce incomplete observations. Existing work treats modality absence as a prediction-accuracy problem, leaving a more basic question unanswered: whether a model's confidence estimates remain calibrated when an entire input stream is removed. We argue that missing-modality robustness and calibrated uncertainty are a single coupled property, and introduce Modality-Conditioned Conformal Fusion (MCCF), an architecture that addresses both at once. MCCF combines a multimodal bottleneck fusion backbone trained with modality dropout, per-modality evidential heads producing modality-decomposed Dirichlet distributions, and a Dempster-Shafer combination rule that fuses the per-modality evidence into a joint predictive distribution; an absent modality contributes vacuous evidence that is structurally ignored, so the fused uncertainty automatically reflects the reduced information without test-time imputation. A Mondrian conformal calibration module keyed on the modality-presence mask then provides finite-sample group-conditional coverage for every non-empty modality subset. MCCF is, to our knowledge, the first method with formal coverage guarantees under arbitrary modality availability through architectural integration rather than post-hoc recalibration, and the evidential decomposition yields per-modality vacuity scores that localise uncertainty to the absent modality responsible. Across a synthetic problem and three real multimodal benchmarks, MCCF holds its target coverage on every modality-presence subset, substantially narrows the coverage gap between full and partial modalities relative to a marginal split-conformal baseline, and imposes no measurable accuracy cost relative to temperature-scaled and evidential baselines.

</details>


### [194] [MAUPITI: On-Device Prototype-Based Learning on a Smart Infrared Sensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07192)
*Beatrice Alessandra Motetti,Tanguy Dugas du Villard,Matteo Risso,Alessio Burrello,Francesco Daghero,Enrico Macii,Massimo Poncino,Marco Castellano,Alfio Basile,Daniele Jahier Pagliari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于16×16热MOSFET（TMOS）阵列和RISC-V微控制器的智能多像素红外传感器，通过离线训练CNN编码器并在设备上以流式更新类原型的方式，实现了低功耗（约1.5mW）、小内存（<32kB）下的姿态与手势识别与持续适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嵌入式系统中，低分辨率红外阵列传感器可用于隐私保护的人体感知，但需在严格的内存和功耗约束下实现设备上的学习与持续适应，传统反向传播和重放缓冲区方法内存开销过大。

Method: 采用基于原型的最近类均值（NCM）分类器，CNN编码器离线训练并量化，类原型在设备上以流式方式存储和更新；RISC-V微控制器扩展了低精度SIMD指令以加速推理和更新。

Result: 在两个数据集上，该方法与传统分类器精度相当，分类和原型更新的延迟开销极低（<0.29%），有效实现了感知框架的在线适应。

Conclusion: 该方法在极低内存和功耗约束下实现了设备上的持续学习，为隐私保护的嵌入式人体感知提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Low-resolution infrared (IR) array sensors represent an interesting solution for privacy-preserving human sensing in embedded systems. In this letter, we describe a smart multi-pixel IR sensor integrating a 16$\times$16 thermal MOSFET (TMOS) array and a RISC-V microcontroller extended with low-precision SIMD instructions, capable of on-device learning and continual adaptation for pose and gesture recognition tasks under tight memory and power constraints ($<$32kB on-chip memory, $\approx$1.5mW). To avoid the memory overheads of backpropagation and replay buffers, we adopt a prototype-based Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier in which a simple Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) encoder is trained and quantized offline, while class prototypes are stored and updated on the device in streaming mode. With experiments on two datasets, we show that this approach yields accuracy on par with a conventional classifier, with negligible latency overheads in both the classification and the prototype update ($<$0.29% considering both phases), effectively enabling online adaptation of the perception framework.

</details>


### [195] [An AI4AI Framework for Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07193)
*Zhen Liu,Wenli Huang,Wei Song,Yuhan Liu,Zhiqin Yang,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoPrune 是一个无需训练、利用大语言模型自动设计视觉标记裁剪策略的框架。它通过引入领域特定语言 TPDSL（包含 131 个可复用原子）并将搜索状态表示为对强基线的残差修改，从而有效缩小搜索空间。实验表明，即便裁剪掉 94.4% 的视觉标记，仍能保持 99% 以上的性能，并实现 9.9 倍 FLOPs 降低与 6.4 倍预填充延迟缩短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉标记裁剪方法依赖固定的人工启发式规则和试错，难以适应多样化的裁剪目标、预算和模型架构。作者旨在回答“大语言模型能否自动设计有效的视觉标记裁剪算法”，从而构建 AI4AI 的自动化框架。

Method: 提出 AutoPrune，一种基于 LLM 驱动的训练无关视觉标记裁剪策略设计框架。其核心是 Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL)，包含 131 个可复用原子，覆盖预算控制、标记评分、选择约束和标记重组。TPDSL 的关键特性是将每个搜索状态表示为对强基线的残差修改，从而缩小搜索空间并将 LLM 的关注引导至对性能最关键的策略组件。

Result: 在 14 个多模态基准和 3 个 MLLM 骨干网络上验证了有效性、效率和可迁移性。即使裁剪掉 94.4% 的视觉标记，AutoPrune 仍能保留超过 99% 的全标记性能，同时 FLOPs 降低 9.9 倍、预填充延迟缩短 6.4 倍。

Conclusion: 证明了利用大语言模型自动设计视觉标记裁剪策略是可行的，通过残差化的领域特定语言表示能够有效桥接 LLM 的通用知识与专用任务的结构约束，从而超越手工启发式方法的局限性。

Abstract: Visual-token pruning can substantially reduce the inference cost of multimodal large language models (MLLMs), yet existing methods largely rely on fixed, handcrafted heuristics and costly expert trial and error. As pruning objectives, budgets, and model architectures diversify, manually navigating the expanding design space becomes increasingly difficult. This paper aims to build an AI4AI framework for visual-token pruning by addressing a natural question: Can large language models automatically design effective visual-token reduction algorithms? Although LLMs possess broad algorithmic knowledge and strong reasoning capabilities, translating such general knowledge into effective solutions for a specialized task remains nontrivial. We argue that the key lies in designing an appropriate search-state representation that connects the internal knowledge of LLMs with the structural requirements and constraints of visual-token pruning. Based on this insight, we propose AutoPrune, a training-free framework for LLM-driven visual-token pruning policy design. At its core, AutoPrune introduces a Token Pruning Domain-Specific Language (TPDSL) comprising 131 reusable atoms for budget control, token scoring, selection constraints, and token reassembly. A key property of TPDSL is that it represents each search state as a residual modification of a strong base policy. This residual formulation narrows the search space and directs the LLM's attention toward the policy components that are most consequential for performance. Experiments on 14 multimodal benchmarks and three MLLM backbones demonstrate the effectiveness, efficiency, and transferability of AutoPrune. Even when removing 94.4% of visual tokens, AutoPrune preserves more than 99% of full-token performance while reducing FLOPs by 9.9x and prefill latency by 6.4x.

</details>


### [196] [Stochastic Autoregressive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07224)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种二元随机自回归学习的PAC模型，受LLM启发，将确定性自回归学习推广到随机设置。研究了三种监督形式（基础一步样本、思维链样本、端到端样本）下的样本复杂度，并证明了随机自回归学习在样本复杂度上与确定性理论有本质区别，给出了不同监督形式之间的上下界关系，并通过对logistic函数的研究进行了补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受大型语言模型（LLM）迭代采样下一个标记分布的生成方式的启发，本文旨在建立随机自回归学习的理论框架，并将其与已有的确定性自回归学习理论进行对比。

Method: 本文引入了一个二元随机自回归学习的PAC模型，其中每个提示符对应一个伯努利下一个标记分布。定义了三种监督形式：基础一步样本（仅观测单步采样）、思维链样本（观测完整M步轨迹）和端到端样本（仅观测最终标记）。对于给定的生成器类别，研究在平方损失误差ε下，学习基础模型中的一步概率以及CoT和e2e模型中的最终标记概率所需的最小样本数。通过理论分析得到了样本复杂度之间的上界和下界。

Result: 随机自回归学习与确定性理论有本质区别：在相同尺度ε下，m_CoT/m_base和m_e2e/m_CoT可以同时任意大于M/ε（确定性结果的类比量）。但经过尺度调整后，对于任何类别，CoT学习在尺度ε下的样本复杂度不超过基础学习在尺度ε/M^2下的样本复杂度；而e2e学习在尺度ε下的样本复杂度（忽略对数因子）不超过(M/ε)·m_CoT(Θ(ε))。这些依赖关系和尺度基本上是紧的。此外，对维度d的逻辑函数进行了具体分析。

Conclusion: 随机自回归学习的样本复杂度与确定性情况不同，不同监督形式之间的样本复杂度关系并非简单的全序，而是需要通过尺度变换来建立联系。本文的界是紧的，为理解LLM等随机自回归模型的样本效率提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Motivated by LLMs, which generate outputs by iteratively sampling from next-token distributions, we introduce a PAC-learning model for binary stochastic autoregressive learning. This generalizes the deterministic autoregressive learning framework of Joshi et al., COLT 2025. In our model, one fixed generator assigns a Bernoulli next-token distribution to every prompt string. Starting from an input prompt, a token is sampled and appended to the prompt; the same generator is then applied again to this expanded prompt; this procedure is repeated for $M$ steps. Three forms of supervision are considered: base one-step samples, chain-of-thought (CoT) samples that reveal full random trajectories of length $M$, and end-to-end (e2e) samples that reveal only the final token of length $M$ trajectories. For a generator class, we study the minimum number of samples $m_{base}(\varepsilon),m_{CoT}(\varepsilon), m_{e2e}(\varepsilon)$, resp., required to learn the one-step probabilities in the base model, and the final-token probability in the CoT and e2e models, under squared loss error~$\varepsilon$.
  We show that stochastic autoregressive learning fundamentally differs from the deterministic theory. At scale $\varepsilon$, there is no universal comparison between the three learning tasks: both $m_{CoT}/m_{base}$ and $m_{e2e}/m_{CoT}$ can be made simultaneously arbitrarily larger than $M/\varepsilon$, the natural analogue for the existing deterministic results. Nevertheless, after altering scales, for every class, CoT learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded by base learning at scale $\varepsilon/M^2$, whereas e2e learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded, up to logarithmic factors, by $(M/\varepsilon) m_{CoT}(Θ(\varepsilon))$. These dependencies and scales are essentially tight. We complement these bounds by studying dimension $d$ logistic functions in our model.

</details>


### [197] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在部分可观察的线性二次型问题中，即使最优策略在可表示范围内且价值函数表达能力足够，标准演员-评论家算法仍会收敛到次优策略（比最优可表示策略差35%），原因是评论家将无法归因于隐藏状态的观测变异误判为价值估计的曲率，导致演员偏离最优。通过调整前瞻步长可消除此偏差，而增加记忆无济于事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示部分可观察强化学习中性能下降的根本原因，区分策略表达能力的限制与学习偏差的影响。

Method: 利用线性二次型问题中可闭式求解的演员-评论家模型，严格分析学习动态，推导策略、代价及消除偏差的设计选择（前瞻步长）的闭式表达式，并通过深度强化学习实验验证。

Result: 在默认设置下，最优可表示策略仅比全观察理想控制器差10.4%，但学习收敛到的策略比该最优可表示策略差35%。偏差源于评论家将未观察到的状态变异误读为价值函数曲率，演员随之偏离最优。增加观测记忆无效，调整前瞻步长可消除问题。

Conclusion: 部分可观察强化学习的瓶颈可能不在策略表达能力，而在于评论家的学习偏差；通过调整算法前瞻步长（而非增加记忆）可有效缓解。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [198] [TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07274)
*Yuhan Xie,Jingrong Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向目标的特征解耦（TOFD）统一框架，用于抵御分裂联邦学习（SFL）中的多种投毒攻击，通过目标推断、样本纯化和解耦优化三个阶段实现主动检测和鲁棒优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分裂联邦学习（SFL）的拆分架构引入了独特的攻击面，容易遭受多种投毒攻击，而现有防御方法未能充分利用拆分范式的特点，难以在早期检测和遏制恶意行为。

Method: TOFD框架包含三个阶段：(1)目标推断：通过类别特定的边界扰动（MP）细化类别级安全区域，识别潜在攻击目标；(2)样本净化：利用跨类别最小-最大归一化MP校准的阈值自适应过滤被投毒的打碎数据；(3)解耦优化：借助对抗性引导模型捕获攻击诱导模式并在优化过程中解耦其影响，抑制残余对抗效应。

Result: 在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，TOFD在不同攻击场景下 consistently 优于最先进的防御方法，实现了优越的鲁棒性，且计算开销低，适合实际部署。

Conclusion: TOFD为SFL提供了一种统一的、可理论保证收敛的防御框架，能够主动检测并鲁棒优化以抵抗多种投毒攻击，兼具高效性和实用性。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage. To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [199] [A foundation-model approach to pediatric headache classification from rs-fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07287)
*Guilherme S. Imai Aldeia,Clara Moon,Julie Shulman,Navil Sethna,Allison Smith,Alyssa Lebel,William G. La Cava,Scott Holmes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 使用静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）和基础模型NeuroSTORM，结合机器学习对儿童头痛进行分类和亚型识别。NeuroSTORM在区分头痛与非头痛（AUROC=0.82, AUPRC=0.93）以及多分类任务中表现优于传统的功能连接矩阵方法，为基于fMRI的儿童头痛预测提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头痛是儿童最常见的神经系统疾病，严重影响生活质量，但目前缺乏有效的客观分类工具。传统机器学习方法通常依赖手工构建的功能连接特征，性能有限。作者希望探索是否可以利用rs-fMRI和基础模型（NeuroSTORM）更好地支持儿童头痛分类，以提升诊断准确性和亚型区分能力。

Method: 使用NeuroSTORM（一种最近提出的基础模型）对rs-fMRI数据进行编码，然后通过微调（fine-tuning）来区分健康对照与头痛儿童，并进一步分类头痛亚型。同时，与使用功能连接矩阵作为预测变量的标准神经科学方法进行对比。数据来自110名个体的189次rs-fMRI扫描，涵盖两次访视，头痛患病率为74%。评估指标包括AUROC和AUPRC。

Result: NeuroSTORM在区分头痛与非头痛任务中取得AUROC为0.82（95% CI, 0.82-0.82）、AUPRC为0.93（95% CI, 0.93-0.94），显著优于基于功能连接矩阵的模型（AUROC=0.67, AUPRC=0.85）。在三分类任务（健康对照、慢性偏头痛、非慢性头痛）中，NeuroSTORM的宏平均AUROC为0.69（95% CI, 0.68-0.69）。该方法能够较好地区分慢性偏头痛，但难以将其他头痛亚型与慢性偏头痛区分开。

Conclusion: 在数据有限的条件下，NeuroSTORM能够从rs-fMRI中捕获潜在的、可迁移至头痛相关任务的表征，无需依赖传统功能连接特征。该研究为基于fMRI的儿童头痛预测提供了概念验证，并展示了未来在亚型识别和个体化治疗策略中的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Headache is the most common neurological disorder in children and substantially affects quality of life. We investigated whether resting-state functional MRI (rs-fMRI) can support pediatric headache classification using machine learning. We encoded rs-fMRI data using NeuroSTORM, a recent foundation model, and fine-tuned it to distinguish healthy controls from children with headache and subsequently classify headache subtypes. We compared NeuroSTORM with a standard neuroscience approach using functional-connectivity (FC) matrices derived from brain activity as predictors. Using 189 rs-fMRI scans from 110 individuals collected across two visits (prevalence of any headache: 74%), NeuroSTORM achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.82 (95% CI, 0.82-0.82) and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.93 (95% CI, 0.93-0.94) for discriminating headache from non-headache. In contrast, models trained on FC matrices showed lower performance (AUROC, 0.67 [95% CI, 0.67-0.67]; AUPRC, 0.85 [95% CI, 0.85-0.85]). In multiclass classification of healthy controls, chronic migraine, and non-chronic headaches (e.g., post-viral headache, new daily persistent headache, post-traumatic headache), NeuroSTORM achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.69 (95% CI, 0.68-0.69). Results suggest that the approach can distinguish chronic migraine but has difficulty differentiating other headache subtypes from chronic migraine. Overall, under limited-data conditions, NeuroSTORM appears to capture latent rs-fMRI representations that transfer to headache-related tasks without relying on FC features. These findings provide proof of concept for fMRI-based prediction of pediatric headache and highlight potential future utility for subtype identification and individualized treatment strategies.

</details>


### [200] [FUSE: Feature-Wise Unified Specialization with Cross-Column Exchange for Mixed-Type Tabular Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07294)
*Suman Cha,Seongchan Lee,Dohyun Ko,Hyunjoong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FUSE is a framework for mixed-type tabular data generation that explicitly separates feature-specific processing (via adaptive mixture modules for numerical and categorical features) and cross-column interactions (via joint attention), achieving strong performance across eight datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and complex cross-column dependencies. Existing variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints but leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone.

Method: FUSE (Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange) applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. The paper also characterizes excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bounds continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk.

Result: Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

Conclusion: FUSE explicitly separates feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions, yielding improved tabular data generation performance.

Abstract: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and their complex cross-column dependencies. Variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints via factorized distributions, yet leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone. We introduce Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange (FUSE) to explicitly separate these roles. FUSE applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. We also characterize the excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bound the continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk. Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

</details>


### [201] [$\boldsymbolλ$-Orthogonality Regularization for Compatible Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16664)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Ioannis Patras,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 λ-正交正则化方法，在仿射变换中引入松弛的正交约束，以在保持原始表示空间的同时实现分布特定的适配，确保模型更新后的兼容性和零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示适配方法存在两难：仿射变换适配性好但会显著改变原始表示，正交变换保持结构但适配性有限。需要在适配下游分布的同时保留新学习的表示空间，并确保模型更新前后的兼容性。

Method: 在仿射变换中引入 λ-正交正则化（一种松弛的正交约束），在保留原始表示的同时实现分布特定的适配。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的实验表明，该方法能够保持模型的零样本性能，并确保模型更新后的兼容性。

Conclusion: λ-正交正则化有效平衡了适配性与表示保留，为跨模型表示兼容提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval systems rely on representations learned by increasingly powerful models. However, due to the high training cost and inconsistencies in learned representations, there is significant interest in facilitating communication between representations and ensuring compatibility across independently trained neural networks. In the literature, two primary approaches are commonly used to adapt different learned representations: affine transformations, which adapt well to specific distributions but can significantly alter the original representation, and orthogonal transformations, which preserve the original structure with strict geometric constraints but limit adaptability. A key challenge is adapting the latent spaces of updated models to align with those of previous models on downstream distributions while preserving the newly learned representation spaces. In this paper, we impose a relaxed orthogonality constraint, namely $λ$-Orthogonality regularization, while learning an affine transformation, to obtain distribution-specific adaptation while retaining the original learned representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and datasets validate our approach, demonstrating that it preserves the model's zero-shot performance and ensures compatibility across model updates. Code available at: \href{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda_orthogonality.git}{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda\_orthogonality}.

</details>


### [202] [From Optimal Actions to World Models: Identifiability of Transition Kernels in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07301)
*Neal Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究从最优动作（而非Q值）中恢复马尔可夫决策过程转移概率的可识别性问题。对于状态-动作奖励r(s,a)，存在一个n(n-1)维的转移核族产生相同的最优动作；对于依赖于下一状态的奖励r(s,a,s')，通常能唯一确定转移核（除单动作状态外）；对于仅状态奖励r(s)，可识别性最弱：两个转移核等价当且仅当每个确定性策略对同一组奖励最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解从观察到的最优策略（动作选择）中能推断出关于MDP动力学的什么信息，这与Letcher等人从数值Q值恢复动力学的问题类似，但此处只观察最优动作而非数值。

Method: 理论推导：针对不同形式的奖励函数（r(s,a)、r(s)、r(s,a,s')），分析两个转移概率核产生相同最优动作的条件，给出充要条件并用线性代数刻画等价类。

Result: 对于r(s,a)，两个转移核等价当且仅当存在可逆矩阵L满足L1=1，使得Q_{s,a} = (P_{s,a} + (1/γ) e_s^T (L-I)) L^{-1}，且正核附近有n(n-1)维族。对于r(s,a,s')，至少有两个动作的状态的转移行可唯一确定，单动作状态可能隐藏。对于r(s)，等价条件更弱：每个确定性策略对相同奖励集最优。

Conclusion: 奖励函数的形式显著影响从最优动作中恢复转移概率的能力：状态-动作奖励无法唯一确定转移核，依赖于下一状态的奖励几乎可以唯一确定，而仅状态奖励的识别能力最差。

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>


### [203] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 模块中 SwiGLU 激活函数的开放正尾是否必要，并引入闭尾替代方案 MemGLU（基于忆阻分支几何）。在 9M 和 30M 参数规模的预训练实验（三颗种子）中，MemGLU 的验证 NLL 与 SwiGLU 相差约 0.1%；两个模型的门控使用方式不同但损失相近。结论：在测试尺度下，SwiGLU 的开放正尾并非必需，模型会根据预训练时提供的门控几何自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验 SwiGLU 激活函数中开放正尾（open positive tail）对 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 是否必要，探索更简单的替代门控函数。

Method: 提出 MemGLU——一种基于忆阻分支几何（memristive branch geometry）的闭尾门控函数，作为 SwiGLU 的对照。在 9M 和 30M 参数规模下进行配对预训练实验（每种设置三个随机种子），比较 MemGLU 与 SwiGLU 的验证负对数似然（NLL），并分析训练后的 SwiGLU 检查点对正尾抑制的敏感性以及两个模型的门控使用差异。

Result: MemGLU 与 SwiGLU 的验证 NLL 相差约 0.1%；训练好的 SwiGLU 检查点对正尾抑制敏感；两个模型的门控使用方式不同但损失相近。

Conclusion: 在测试的 9M 和 30M 参数尺度下，SwiGLU 的开放正尾对 decoder-only 语言模型 FFN 并非必要；模型会依据预训练时得到的门控几何进行自适应调整。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [204] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的GNN层GLIDE，用于动态时间序列预测，通过路径消息传递和动静传播分离机制，解决了现有模型在高时序相关性波动（TCV）场景下性能退化的问题，在合成和真实基准上平均提升45.6%，最高达85.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN模型通常假设静态图拓扑，但实际中时间序列的成对相关性会随时间剧烈变化。作者发现许多流行模型（包括Transformer）在高TCV环境下泛化能力差，甚至不如简单的结构无关基线，因此需要一种能适应动态拓扑的GNN架构。

Method: 首先提出TCV（时序相关性波动）指标来量化潜在结构的分布演化，并建立TCV与性能退化之间的联系。然后提出GLIDE层，包含两个关键设计：(D1)基于路径的消息传递——捕获路径级邻域信息；(D2)静态与动态传播分离——通过局部静态近似识别最优动力学。这两者共同提升动态拓扑下的学习能力，同时保持静态场景的鲁棒性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，GLIDE在静态和动态设置下平均性能提升达45.6%，最大增益达到85.7%。

Conclusion: GLIDE有效克服了传统GNN在动态时序相关性下的局限，通过路径消息传递和动静分离机制实现了对动态图拓扑的鲁棒建模，显著提升了多变量时间序列预测的性能。

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [205] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对无经验回放和无目标网络的并行化深度强化学习算法 PQN，系统研究了视觉编码器的 CNN 架构设计空间，提出了复合架构 Aftab，在 Atari-57 和 Procgen Hard 基准上取得了显著性能提升，并开源了完整框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管并行化 Q-Network (PQN) 算法通过简化设置实现了稳定的离线策略学习，但无缓冲区场景下视觉编码器的表征能力和参数效率仍未得到充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，优化 PQN 的视觉编码器设计。

Method: 本文设计了八种不同的 CNN 拓扑结构，在严格参数约束下优化样本效率；研究了 Hadamax 编码范式以及分布头、集成头、决斗头等先进 Q 学习扩展的影响；最终提出复合架构 Aftab，整合了上述最优设计。

Result: 在 Atari-57 基准上，Aftab 的 IQM 人类标准化分数达到 6.479，相比标准 PQN 基线的改善概率为 0.86；在 Procgen Hard 基准上，IQM 标准化分数为 0.418（基线为 0.382），验证了其分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: Aftab 在保持无缓冲并行优化简洁性和内存效率的同时，建立了概率上更优的无模型强化学习结构参考，为高效、可泛化的视觉编码器设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [206] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FPRC-PQ的新方法，用于学习异构表示。与传统的特征拼接不同，该方法将每个表示视为拥有自身坐标系和残差的类型化对象，并通过有序的算子组合进行学习，保留或消除表示的身份。在3.67M中国A股数据上，该方法将净收益从13.52%提升至19.10%，夏普比率从1.42提升至2.09，优于多种对照方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的异构表示学习通常采用特征拼接，这会抹去每个表示的身份信息（即来源），并产生误差。作者认为，每个表示应该被视为一个拥有自身坐标系和未解释残差的类型化对象，学习过程应该是有序的算子组合，保留或消除类型信息，从而更有效地利用异构表示。

Method: 该方法的核心是“放松-聚合-闭合”（relax-aggregate-close）过程。首先，每个字段（表示）通过在其自身坐标系中拟合残差进行放松校正。然后，校正后的字段在一个固定均值处汇合，这是唯一的身份消除边界。最后，一个共享学习器仅对聚合后的新残差进行闭合。整个组合精确地分解为表示、局部残差估计和残差之残差估计。聚合器是一个学习到的控制变量接口，具有总体方差缩减功能。此外，还引入了一个反思性反刍算子，通过正交投影而非网格搜索来固定增益。

Result: 在2023-2026年间的3.67M中国A股股票-日观测数据上，在冻结的时点协议下，该方法将扣除成本后的收益率从13.52%提高到19.10%，夏普比率从1.42提高到2.09。与匹配容量、统一残差、无身份两阶段和仅成对等控制方法相比，该方法均表现更优。

Conclusion: 性能提升并非源于更多特征或更多树，而是因为在表示身份仍然可用的情况下，明确地处理了残差所有权和组合方式。这强调了在异构表示学习中保留身份信息并显式组合残差的重要性。

Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

</details>


### [207] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 OmniDecVAE，一种能够从任意多模态时间序列中学习可分解、可解释且多用途表示的统一框架，在包含最多30个模态的人体活动识别任务上，同时实现了高精度分类、可分解表示学习、多模态融合与生成建模，且模型轻量（4.1M参数）支持实时推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态可穿戴计算方法未能同时满足任务分类、可分解表示学习、多模态融合和生成建模这四项全栈需求，缺乏统一的、可扩展的轻量级解决方案。

Method: OmniDecVAE 扩展了 DecVAE，通过多视图自监督分解损失学习模态条件化的时频隐子空间，并采用共享的非对称自编码器架构，实现任意数量模态的统一处理。

Result: 在多达30个模态的 HAR 任务中，相比 Transformer 和 VAE 基线，活动识别准确率提升1.01%，身份识别提升6.75%；生成数据质量上，MAE 改善76.84%，MMD 改善13.85%；模型参数仅4.1M，具备实时推理能力。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAE 展示了作为智能边缘可穿戴和临床健康应用的轻量级统一模型的潜力，通过增强的表示能力、模态不变的参数规模和实时延迟，在一个模型中统一了处理需求与能力。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [208] [FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07393)
*Deepank Girish,Yi Hao Chan,Yubin Zheng,Sukrit Gupta,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDOSE，一种面向动态功能连接（dFC）的多站点fMRI联邦学习框架，通过模引导的塔克分解和最优传输原型对齐，在ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II和ADHD-200三个数据集上 outperforms 现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点fMRI数据存在统计异质性（尤其站点差异），现有联邦学习方法仅依赖静态功能连接（FC）而丢失了脑网络动态信息，且难以处理高维dFC张量。

Method: 提出FedDOSE框架，包含模引导的塔克分解块（Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition）高效编码高维dFC张量并捕捉模块级时空模式；在各站点生成类别原型，通过最优传输（OT）重心和Procrustes分析在全局层面对齐原型。

Result: 在ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II和ADHD-200三个多站点静息态fMRI数据集上，针对ASD和ADHD诊断任务，FedDOSE优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: FedDOSE能有效从多站点数据中学习鲁棒表示，实现可靠的分析。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

</details>


### [209] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种通过最大化预测分布熵来校准LLM置信度的方法，采用双层优化框架并利用一阶近似实现高效训练，在多项选择和开放生成问答任务上取得良好校准效果，尤其在域外泛化上表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐常使LLM过度自信且校准不良，传统事后温度缩放高度依赖领域（一个领域拟合的温度无法泛化到其他领域），因此需要在训练阶段修改模型参数以改善校准。

Method: 以最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，通过双层优化实现：下层在参数化损失下训练模型，上层选择损失超参数以最大化熵；为适应LLM规模，采用高效一阶近似避免显式二阶计算。

Result: 在多项选择和开放生成式问答实验表明，该方法使LLM校准良好，尤其在域外泛化方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵最大化双层优化方法能有效改善LLM的校准性能，并解决传统温度缩放的领域依赖问题，提升域外泛化能力。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [210] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），通过端到端的终点预测目标训练，避免递归 rollout 的误差累积，显著提升长时域预测精度，并论证训练目标而非模型结构是长时域准确性的主要决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型通常使用局部几步预测目标训练，但在推理时通过递归 rollout 进行长时域预测，导致训练与部署不匹配：局部损失无法区分不同下游影响力的转移，小误差在递归中放大，长时域精度受限。

Method: 提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），一种非递归架构，将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入，并通过一次前向传播直接预测终点观测。该设计避免递归 rollout 和梯度传播，使得端到端长时域训练可行，克服了自回归训练的不稳定性。

Result: 在连续控制和像素级基准上，DPWM 在长时域终点预测上显著优于递归基线，且优势随预测长度增加而扩大；递归基线在使用相同终点目标重新训练后同样受益，证明训练目标而非网络结构是长时域精度的关键。

Conclusion: 世界模型应在其最终使用的时域尺度上进行训练和评估，研究重心应从局部转移建模转向长时域预测准确性。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [211] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper investigates the mechanistic vulnerabilities of safety alignment in Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs). It shows that safety alignment in DLLMs is sparse and transferable from autoregressive predecessors, enabling efficient transfer attacks via safety neuron mapping and pruning. The authors propose SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that uses weighted safety neuron loss to steer diffusion away from safety regions, achieving high attack success rates with low generation cost across open and proprietary models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The internal safety mechanisms of Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) are poorly understood, despite their increasing adoption. This work aims to expose mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment, both to understand their safety properties and to develop effective attack methods.

Method: The authors first demonstrate that safety alignment in DLLMs is sparse and transferable: DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same safety neuron footprint, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Building on this, they introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that uses a weighted safety neuron loss to steer the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions.

Result: Self-pruning increases ASR from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream. Transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM. SN-Guided Diffusion achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC=1.0) and transfer ASR up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, with only 20 generation episodes per prompt.

Conclusion: The work reveals fundamental safety vulnerabilities in DLLMs due to sparse and transferable safety alignment. The proposed SN-Guided Diffusion framework demonstrates highly effective and efficient jailbreak attacks, achieving competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost compared to prior methods.

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [Can MLLMs Decode the Creative Leap? Introducing C4 for Cross-Concept Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06501)
*Ming Wang,Yuqing Zhang,Tingna Xie,Xiangju Li,Xiaocui Yang,Daling Wang,Shi Feng,Yifei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C4框架，一种受认知启发的成语跨概念创造力评估方法，通过编码-解码范式构建评估集（C4-Eval），并在多个MLLM上测试，发现当前模型在跨概念理解上存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的创造力在多个领域很重要，但现有评估多依赖准确率任务，缺乏对创造性理解的度量。跨概念理解是核心认知能力，但缺乏系统评估方法。

Method: 提出C4框架，将项目构建视为跨概念编码（将目标槽映射为可意象的替代概念，通过桥接路径），模型推理视为跨概念解码。构建了C4-Eval评估集（184个合成项+37个人工创作项），手动构建跨概念关系、桥接路径和推理过程。每个项实例化为5种任务设置，共884个主要答案恢复案例。

Result: 在10个MLLM上评估，最强闭源模型主要准确率50.7%和48.0%，开源模型显著更低。候选约束显著提升准确率，但桥接提示和解释请求增益有限。表明当前MLLM在解码创造性编码含义方面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 当前MLLM在跨概念创造力解码方面能力有限，开源模型与闭源模型差距明显，需要进一步提升模型对创造性编码含义的理解能力。

Abstract: Creative capabilities of MLLMs matter in design, communication, education, and human--AI collaboration, yet remain difficult to evaluate because explicit targets and reward signals are scarce compared with accuracy-oriented tasks. Cross-concept understanding is a core cognitive capacity underlying receptive creativity. It enables a perceiver to recover intended meaning from non-obvious but meaningful conceptual relations. We operationalize item construction as cross-concept encoding and model inference as cross-concept decoding. We introduce C4, a cognition-inspired evaluation framework for Chengyu (Chinese idiom)-based Cross-Concept Creativity. Its encoding component maps target slots to imageable substitute concepts along bridge paths in a manually annotated and third-party-reviewed cross-concept network, enabling batch generation with explicit structure, difficulty indexed by bridge count and depth, and exact answers. Using this framework, we instantiate the C4 Evaluation Set (C4-Eval), comprising 184 synthetic items and 37 human-created cross-concept chengyu figures collected from online sources. We manually construct and review cross-concept relations, bridge paths, and reasoning processes for the collected figures. Each C4-Eval item is instantiated in five task settings, yielding 884 primary answer-recovery cases. Across ten evaluated MLLMs, the strongest closed models reach 50.7% and 48.0% primary accuracy, while open-source models remain substantially lower. Candidate constraints improve accuracy sharply, but bridge hints and explanation requests provide only modest gains. These results expose a substantial gap in how current MLLMs decode creatively encoded meaning through cross-concept relations. The code is in the supplementary material.

</details>


### [213] [From Cheap Fakes to Pure Synthesis: Addressing the New Era of T2V Fake News Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06732)
*Yifeng Luo,Yupeng Li,Liang Lan,Tian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到视频（T2V）生成模型带来的纯合成假新闻视频威胁，将其定义为三元分类任务（真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造），构建了首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，并提出了推理引导的R-T2V框架，通过条件化理由生成和监督微调，融合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹，在10个基线模型上取得了最优性能，准确率比第二名高12.20个百分点，宏F1高8.46个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T2V生成模型能够从零合成假新闻视频，超越了以往拼接现有素材的“廉价伪造”威胁。现有数据集缺乏纯合成假新闻视频，且直接使用T2V模型根据假新闻描述生成视频会导致检测退化到单模态捷径和语义-视觉退化问题。因此需要构建包含纯合成伪造的新数据集和相应检测方法。

Method: （1）将T2V假新闻视频检测重新定义为三元分类任务：真实、廉价伪造、纯合成伪造；（2）构建首个纯合成假新闻视频数据集PS-FNVD，包含两类伪造事件：编造事件+对齐欺骗（Type1）和真实事件+虚假视觉来源（Type2），防止模型利用单模态捷径；（3）提出推理引导的R-T2V框架，通过条件化理由生成和监督微调，整合高层语义逻辑与低层物理生成痕迹来预测三元真实性标签。

Result: 在10个主流基线模型上的大量实验表明，R-T2V框架达到了最先进性能，准确率比第二名基线高出12.20个百分点，宏F1高出8.46个百分点。

Conclusion: 本文提出的三元分类框架、数据集PS-FNVD以及R-T2V方法有效解决了T2V生成假新闻视频检测中的单模态捷径问题，为应对新型合成伪造威胁提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent text-to-video (T2V) generation models enable fake news videos to be synthesized from scratch, shifting the threat beyond cheap fakes assembled from existing footage. Such news videos can closely match fabricated narratives, creating a modality alignment trap for existing detectors. Existing datasets lack pure synthesis fake news videos. Although directly prompting T2V models with descriptions of fake news videos can yield perfectly aligned samples, it reduces the fake news video detection (FNVD) to unimodal shortcuts and causes semantic-visual degeneration. To counter this, we formulate T2V-FNVD as a novel ternary classification task with three labels (real, cheap fake, and pure synthesis fake) and construct the first pure synthesis fake news video dataset (PS-FNVD). PS-FNVD includes fabricated events with aligned deception (Type 1) and true events with false visual provenance (Type 2), preventing models from exploiting unimodal shortcuts. Furthermore, we propose the Reasoning-guided T2V-FNVD (R-T2V) framework. Trained through conditioned rationale generation and supervised fine-tuning, R-T2V integrates high-level semantic logic with low-level physical generative traces to predict the ternary veracity label. Extensive experiments across 10 prevailing baselines show that R-T2V achieves the state-of-the-art performance, outperforming the second-best baseline by 12.20 percentage points in accuracy and 8.46 percentage points in macro $F_1$.

</details>


### [214] [DocMemo: Dynamic Evidence Discovery via Probabilistic Memory-Guided Retrieval for Multi-Modal Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07067)
*Hanshu Yao,Janfeng Zhong,Niu Lian,Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DocMemo 是一种基于记忆引导的长文档推理框架，通过三层检索状态（文档模式记忆、页面信念记忆、问题情景记忆）和贝叶斯页面信念更新（汤普森采样、空间邻近传播、结构感知自适应粒度访问）实现动态证据探索，在三个基准上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长文档理解系统受限于静态检索（单轮方法固定 top-k 页面，难以从早期错误中恢复）和脆弱的跨轮记忆（迭代方法未研究跨轮状态的传播机制，难以追踪页面相关性的动态变化），因此需要一种能够动态探索证据的框架。

Method: DocMemo 维护三层检索状态：文档模式记忆（结构先验）、页面信念记忆（动态相关性估计）和问题情景记忆（查询特定推理轨迹）。推理时通过贝叶斯页面信念更新（含汤普森采样）、空间邻近传播和结构感知自适应粒度证据访问来持续优化跨轮页面选择，并以细粒度视觉区域补充页面级证据。

Result: 在三个长文档理解基准上取得最先进的性能，验证了结构化记忆和动态页面信念更新的有效性。

Conclusion: DocMemo 通过记忆引导的框架有效解决了长文档推理中证据稀疏和跨轮状态追踪难题，动态页面信念更新和三层记忆设计是关键贡献。

Abstract: Long-document understanding requires locating sparse and heterogeneous evidence across hundreds of pages, yet existing systems remain limited by static retrieval and fragile cross-round memory. Mainstream single-round methods commit to a fixed top-$k$ page set at the outset and struggle to recover from early retrieval errors; recent iterative approaches allow multi-round evidence acquisition, but they do not investigate the propagation mechanism of cross-round states, making it difficult to track the dynamic changes in page relevance. To address these limitations, we propose DocMemo, a memory-guided framework that formulates long-document reasoning as dynamic evidence exploration. DocMemo maintains a tri-level retrieval state consisting of Document Schema Memory, Page Belief Memory, and Question Episodic Memory, which respectively capture structural priors, dynamic relevance estimation, and query-specific reasoning trajectories. During reasoning, DocMemo continuously refines cross-round page selection through Bayesian page belief updating with Thompson sampling, spatial proximity propagation, and structure-aware adaptive-granularity evidence access, while supplementing page-level evidence with fine-grained visual regions. Experiments on 3 benchmarks show that DocMemo achieves state-of-the-art performance and validate the efficacy of structured memory and dynamic page belief updating. Code is available at https://github.com/Harrygof/DocMemo.

</details>


### [215] [Towards Multi-Label Graph Foundation Models: from Single-Vector Representation Learning to Multi-Semantic Basis Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06394)
*Dongxiao He,Jiayu Zhang,Jitao Zhao,Yi Wang,Di Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种多语义基底图基础模型（MSB-GFM），用于跨域多标签节点分类。该模型通过多语义基底表示学习范式将多标签节点建模为语义基底的适应性组合，并设计语义-结构双通道架构与域对抗训练，实现跨域知识迁移，克服了现有图基础模型基于单标签假设导致的语义纠缠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图基础模型（GFMs）基于单标签假设，每个节点仅用一个单一表示嵌入，这导致多标签节点中多个语义被纠缠在一起，难以同时区分多个标签。此外，现有方法多局限于同域场景，缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出多语义基底表示学习范式，将每个多标签节点表示为多个语义基底的适应性组合，从而灵活建模多重语义。同时设计语义-结构双通道架构，结合域对抗训练，实现跨域知识迁移。

Result: 大量实验表明所提模型在跨域多标签节点分类任务上具有有效性。

Conclusion: MSB-GFM通过多语义基底表示和双通道域对抗架构，有效解决了现有图基础模型在多标签场景下的语义纠缠问题，并实现了跨域泛化，为多标签节点分类提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Multi-label node classification is an important yet challenging task in graph learning, where nodes exhibit multiple semantics simultaneously. Existing methods for multi-label node classification can effectively model multiple labels, while only considering in-domain scenarios where the model needs to be trained and tested within the same graph domain, resulting in limited cross-domain generalization. Recently, Graph Foundation Models (GFMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for learning transferable graph representations across diverse graph domains and downstream tasks. However, existing GFMs are built upon single-label assumption, where all nodes are arbitrarily regarded as containing only one class of semantic and embedded into a single representation. For multi-label nodes, such a representation essentially approximates multiple semantics with a single point in the representation space, inevitably leading to semantic entanglement and making simultaneous discrimination of multiple labels difficult. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Semantic Basis Graph Foundation Model (MSB-GFM), a framework for cross-domain multi-label node classification. Specifically, we introduce a multi-semantic basis representation learning paradigm that models each multi-label node as an adaptive composition of semantic bases, thereby enabling flexible representational capacity for modeling multiple semantics. Furthermore, we develop a semantic-structure dual-channel architecture with domain adversarial training for effective cross-domain knowledge transfer. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our model.

</details>


### [216] [EntropyMoE: Entropy-Aware Sparse Expert Routing for Tokenizer-Free LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06398)
*Bo Liu,Muxuab Yu,Yu Zhang,Pengfei Gao,Yongping Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EntropyMoE 是一种面向动态字节块的混合专家（MoE）架构，利用块熵作为路由信号，在字节级大语言模型中实现稀疏条件计算，在保持下游性能的同时达到更低的 bits-per-byte。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的字节级大语言模型将字节分组为动态大小的块，但对每个块都应用相同的密集前馈计算，无法根据块的语义和粒度差异自适应调整模型容量。需要一种能够根据块特征动态分配计算资源的稀疏架构。

Method: EntropyMoE 将全局块 Transformer 中的密集前馈模块替换为 Top-K 专家层。每个动态块作为专家路由的基本单元，其字节覆盖范围决定工作负载贡献。路由器直接基于块熵选择专家，利用与动态块构建相同的粒度信号来组织稀疏计算；块熵和长度共同定义特征空间以调控专家专业化。

Result: 在 matched dense 和 sparse 基线对比中，EntropyMoE 取得了最低的 held-out bits-per-byte，同时下游准确率相当。证明块熵是有效的路由坐标，并将 MoE 建模扩展到了基于 tokenizer 的表示之外。

Conclusion: 块熵可作为稀疏条件计算的有效路由信号，EntropyMoE 成功将混合专家建模应用于无 tokenizer 的字节级表示，为未来高效字节级语言模型的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent byte-level large language models (LLMs) have made tokenizer-free modeling increasingly competitive by grouping bytes into dynamically sized patches. However, existing byte-patch architectures still apply the same dense feed-forward computation to every patch. This uniform computation cannot adapt model capacity to variations in patch semantics and granularity. We address this limitation with EntropyMoE, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture designed for dynamic byte patches. EntropyMoE replaces the dense feed-forward modules in the global patch Transformer with Top-K expert layers. Each dynamic patch serves as the basic unit of expert routing, and its byte coverage determines its contribution to workload accounting. The router selects experts directly from patch entropy, using the same granularity signal that underlies dynamic patch construction to organize sparse computation. Patch entropy and length jointly define the feature space for regulating expert specialization. Experiments show that EntropyMoE achieves the lowest held-out bits-per-byte among matched dense and sparse baselines while maintaining comparable downstream accuracy. These results establish patch entropy as an effective routing coordinate for sparse conditional computation and extend Mixture-of-Experts modeling beyond tokenizer-based representations.

</details>


### [217] [Beyond Routing Weights: Faithful Response-Level Interpretation of Mixture-of-Experts Reward Models via Contribution Contrast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06400)
*Yifan Wang,Jinyi Mu,Mayank Jobanputra,Yu Wang,Soyoung Oh,Isabel Valera,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了贡献对比（CoCo）方法，用于对混合专家（MoE）奖励模型进行响应级解释，通过选择被选和拒绝的响应对中贡献差异最大的样本来忠实刻画专家的角色，比基于路由权重、分数或稀疏自编码器的方法更一致、忠实和专业化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE奖励模型虽然通过路由权重将提示分配给专家，但路由权重仅反映专家接收了哪些提示，无法揭示专家如何评判响应，因此需要一种能同时捕获路由和偏好判断行为的解释方法。

Method: 提出贡献对比（CoCo）方法，利用被选-拒绝响应对的贡献对比度（即专家在判别时的贡献差异）来识别和解释专家的行为角色，从而更完整地刻画专家的功能。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估中，CoCo相比基于路由权重、分数以及稀疏自编码器的替代方法，能产生更一致、忠实和专门化的解释，同时保持竞争力的奖励建模准确度。

Conclusion: 这是首次系统研究MoE奖励模型的解释方法，CoCo为理解专家行为提供了更全面的视角。

Abstract: Reward models are central to learning from human preferences, yet identifying what drives their predictions remains challenging. Recent sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) reward models seek to improve interpretability by routing prompts to specialized experts and characterizing experts through examples with high routing weights. However, routing weights only reveal which prompts an expert $\textit{receives}$, not how it $\textit{judges}$ responses, providing only a partial account of expert behavior. We therefore propose $\textbf{Co}$ntribution-$\textbf{Co}$ntrast ($\textbf{CoCo}$) response-level interpretation, which faithfully characterizes experts' roles using chosen-rejected response pairs with the largest contribution contrasts, jointly capturing routing and preference behavior. Across automatic and human evaluations, CoCo yields more coherent, faithful, and specialized interpretations than router-based, score-based, and sparse autoencoder-based alternatives while maintaining competitive reward modeling accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of interpretation methods for MoE reward models.

</details>


### [218] [Interpretable Unsupervised Community Detection with LLM-Symbolized Structured Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06402)
*Aoting Zeng,Kai Wang,Jianwei Wang,Yuxiang Sun,Yizhang He,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LUCID，一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的、可解释的、无需训练的无监督社区检测方法。受自然系统中相变动力学的启发，LUCID 设计为四阶段流水线：局部视角社区初始化、多因素社区合并、多粒度社区细化、全局视角社区选择。LLM 诱导出形式化规则，将隐含知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑结构。在真实世界数据集上的实验表明，LUCID 作为无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社区检测方法存在局限：经典目标驱动方法难以处理复杂图结构，深度学习方法虽提升性能但牺牲可解释性且依赖标注数据。大语言模型（LLM）具备强大推理能力和世界知识，有望实现可解释、无标签依赖的社区检测。本文旨在利用 LLM 的优势，设计一种可解释、无需训练、无监督的社区检测方法。

Method: LUCID 方法受相变动力学启发，包含四个阶段：
1. 局部视角社区初始化：利用 k-ego 上下文和无监督节点角色编码局部图结构。
2. 多因素社区合并：使用 LLM 诱导的规则迭代合并局部社区。
3. 多粒度社区细化：并行应用 LLM 诱导的从粗到细的规则以减少边界噪声。
4. 全局视角社区选择：基于拓扑紧凑性和边界清晰度识别高质量社区。
整个流程中，LLM 诱导形式化规则，将隐含知识转化为显式可解释的逻辑结构。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，LUCID 作为无监督方法达到了最先进的性能，并持续优于领先的无监督和半监督基线方法。

Conclusion: LUCID 是一种可解释、无需训练、无监督的社区检测方法，通过 LLM 诱导规则有效整合世界知识和推理能力，在复杂图上取得优异性能，为社区检测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Community detection is a fundamental task in graph analytics that aims to identify cohesive groups of entities with similar behaviors or interests. Classic objective-driven methods struggle with complex graph structures, while deep-learning approaches improve performance at the expense of interpretability and rely on labeled data and training. Large language models (LLMs), with strong reasoning capabilities and world knowledge, are promising for interpretable, label-free community detection. To leverage these strengths, we propose LUCID, an LLM-guided, interpretable, training-free, and unsupervised community detection method. Inspired by phase-transition kinetics in natural systems, where complex structures emerge through initialization, merging, refinement, and selection, LUCID is designed as a four-stage pipeline. Within this pipeline, the LLM induces formal rules that translate implicit knowledge into explicit and interpretable logical structures. Specifically, (1) the Local-View Community Initialization stage encodes local graph structures using k-ego contexts and unsupervised node roles; (2) the Multi-factor Community Merge stage uses LLM-induced rules to iteratively merge local communities; (3) the Multi-grain Community Refinement stage applies LLM-induced coarse-to-fine rules in parallel to reduce boundary noise; and (4) the Global-view Community Selection stage identifies high-quality communities based on topological compactness and boundary clarity. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that LUCID, as an unsupervised approach, achieves state-of-the-art performance and consistently outperforms leading unsupervised and semi-supervised baselines.

</details>


### [219] [ADIAS: Automated Design of Interactive Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06410)
*Lekang Jiang,Bohan Tang,Stephan Goetz,Yiwen Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ADIAS是一个基于问题中心智能体优化的全代码智能体设计框架，通过持久性问题状态显式携带修复进展，联合提出修复目标和修改方向，在五个交互式基准测试中平均超越最强基线25.2%，消融实验显示移除问题状态或换回候选中心策略性能下降高达40.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有候选中心的自动化智能体设计方法中，跨轮次经验围绕候选智能体组织，修复进展隐式化，导致修复目标低效、部分进展难以整合、无效干预在轮次间传播。因此提出显式的问题中心优化。

Method: 提出问题中心智能体优化（Issue-centric Agent Optimization），其中修复进展作为显式的持久性问题状态（包含问题标识、生命周期状态、支持证据、干预-结果历史）在轮次间传递，指导优化。实例化为ADIAS框架，使用问题引导优化（Issue-guided Optimization）联合提出修复目标和修改方向，进行全代码修改。

Result: 在五个交互式基准测试中，ADIAS平均超越最强基线25.2%，并在四种骨干模型上取得一致提升。消融实验显示，移除持久性问题状态或替换为候选中心策略会导致性能下降高达40.7%。

Conclusion: 问题中心优化相比候选中心方法更有效，显式的持久性问题状态能提升修复效率和效果。

Abstract: Automated agent design improves agent harnesses through iterative revision, evaluation, and feedback summarization. Existing methods are largely candidate-centric: cross-round experience is organized around candidate agents, which leaves the repair progress implicit. This causes inefficient repair targeting, slow consolidation of partial progress, and propagation of ineffective interventions across rounds. Therefore, we formulate issue-centric agent optimization, in which repair progress is carried forward as an explicit persistent issue state to guide optimization, rather than re-derived from candidate history in each round. We instantiate the formulation in ADIAS, a framework for automated full-code agent design with two mechanisms. A persistent issue state maintains stable issue identities, lifecycle status, supporting evidence, and intervention-outcome histories. Issue-guided optimization uses this state to jointly propose repair targets and revision directions for subsequent focused full-code modification. Across five interactive benchmarks, ADIAS outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.2% on average and achieves consistent gains across four backbone models. Controlled ablations further show that removing persistent issue state or replacing issue-centric revision with candidate-centric policies leads to performance drops of up to 40.7%.

</details>


### [220] [Learning to Predict Middle-Layer Attention in MLLMs for Visual Token Prunin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06411)
*Yuyao Sun,Tao Deng,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Hao Geng,Minjun Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出中间层注意力预测（MAP），通过问题对比教师选择识别样本特定教师层，并将其注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，从而在进入语言模型前剪枝视觉令牌，实现高效多模态大模型推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）性能强大但推理成本高，因为需要处理大量视觉令牌。视觉令牌剪枝可降低成本，但需要准确的令牌重要性估计。现有方法使用固定中间层的文本到视觉注意力来指导剪枝，但存在两个问题：1）最关注问题的层因样本而异，固定层次优；2）获取中间层注意力需先经过多层语言模型，此时已消耗大量计算。因此需要一种无需预计算注意力图、且能动态选择剪枝层的轻量级方法。

Method: 提出中间层注意力预测（MAP）。首先通过问题对比教师选择（Question Contrastive Teacher Selection），对比原始问题和参考问题下的注意力分布，识别对当前样本最关键的教师层。然后将该层的注意力蒸馏到轻量级预测器中，该预测器从多模态输入特征直接估计视觉令牌重要性。推理时，将预测的重要性分数与多样性准则结合，在第一个语言模型层之前剪枝视觉令牌，无需实际计算注意力图。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT-7B的十个基准测试上，MAP仅保留5.56%的视觉令牌即可达到未剪枝模型97.5%的性能，实现3.09倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: MAP通过样本自适应的教师层选择和轻量级注意力预测，在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅减少视觉令牌数量，显著加速多模态大模型推理，且兼容现有推理加速技术。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance across diverse vision-language tasks, but their efficiency is limited by the cost of processing numerous visual tokens. Visual token pruning can reduce this cost, but requires accurate token importance estimates. Recent studies have demonstrated that text-to-vision attention from middle language model layers can effectively guide visual token pruning, typically using attention from a predefined middle layer to select the visual tokens to retain. Two problems therefore remain. First, our analysis shows that the layer whose attention is most responsive to the question varies substantially across samples, making a fixed layer suboptimal. Second, obtaining attention from the appropriate middle layer requires processing numerous visual tokens through several language model layers, by which point considerable computation has already been spent. To address both problems, we propose Middle-layer Attention Prediction (MAP), which uses Question Contrastive Teacher Selection to identify a sample-specific teacher layer by contrasting attention under the original and reference questions, and distills attention from the selected layer into a lightweight predictor that estimates visual token importance from multi-modal input features. During inference, MAP combines the predicted importance scores with a diversity criterion to prune visual tokens before the first language model layer. Thus, MAP requires no attention maps for pruning and remains compatible with existing inference acceleration techniques. Across ten benchmarks on LLaVA-NeXT-7B, MAP retains 97.5% of the unpruned model performance with only 5.56% of the visual tokens, yielding a 3.09x end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [221] [WebGrader: Training LLMs for Web Development with Self-Evolving Programmatic Grader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06474)
*Boshui Chen,Huiping Liu,Shaolei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出WebGrader，一种自演进的程序化评分器，自动为网站生成需求推导交互流（Flow Contract），在真实浏览器中执行并收集多模态证据，通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证技能，从而为强化学习提供可靠奖励，显著提升大模型生成网站的功能成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练大模型生成完整网站时，奖励设计是瓶颈：手工编写浏览器脚本成本高，而VLM或GUI-agent评分器可能未观察到关键状态就提前判定结果，导致奖励不准确。

Method: WebGrader首先从网站请求中自主推导所需的交互流，将其表示为可执行的Flow Contract；然后在真实浏览器中加载生成项目，将目标动作与源代码和实时DOM对齐，沿同一浏览器轨迹收集视觉、DOM、响应和持久化状态证据；通过残差驱动的离线循环发现可复用的验证器技能，在不相交的验证页面上筛选，冻结技能图谱后用于策略训练。整体上将测试规划、动作对齐、证据收集和语义判断分离。

Result: 在WebGen-Bench上，WebGrader训练8B策略达到52.01%的功能成功率，比匹配的外观+脚本奖励高7.88个百分点，并超越o4-mini和DeepSeek-v4-flash。在WG-core-250上，策略达到44.953满分，超越Qwen3-Coder-480B。

Conclusion: WebGrader通过分离测试规划、动作对齐、证据收集和语义判断，仅在观察到请求的状态转换后才给出通过判定，从而提供更可靠的RL奖励，有效提升大模型网站生成的功能正确性。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly generate complete websites from natural-language descriptions, and reinforcement learning has become a central approach to closing their remaining functional gap. This training regime is bottlenecked by reward design. Hand-authored browser scripts are executable yet costly to write for open-ended requirements, while VLM and GUI-agent graders scale but may issue verdicts before observing the decisive state. We propose WebGrader, a self-evolving programmatic grader that autonomously derives the required interaction flows from each website request, represents each flow as an executable Flow Contract, and uses its execution outcome as an RL reward. WebGrader materializes the generated project in a live browser, grounds target actions against the source code and live DOM, and collects visual, DOM, response, and persistent-state evidence along the same browser trajectory. A residual-driven offline loop then discovers reusable verifier skills, screens them on disjoint validation pages, and freezes the promoted skill graph before policy training. By separating test planning, action grounding, evidence collection, and semantic judgment, WebGrader issues a Pass verdict only after observing the requested transition. On WebGen-Bench, WebGrader trains an 8B policy to a 52.01% functional success rate, outperforming a matched appearance-plus-script reward by 7.88 points and surpassing o4-mini and DeepSeek-v4-flash. On WG-core-250, the policy reaches a Full Score of 44.953 and surpasses Qwen3-Coder-480B.

</details>


### [222] [KNOWPLAN: Knowledge-Driven AI Agents for Smart Degree Pathway Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06530)
*Shuheng Cao,Weijia Zhang,Jiaqi Wu,Xiyun Hu,Yat Yang,Juqy Chen,Zhaoxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnowPlan is a two-stage degree planning system that enforces a strict extraction-first boundary: CatalogBrowse independently crawls university sources (catalogs, PDFs, JSON endpoints) to produce provenance-linked JSON documents without any user profile, terminating on completeness certificates rather than reward thresholds; DegreeMap then consumes only those documents to compile a typed requirement hypergraph and lexicographically optimize with CP-SAT across feasibility, horizon, load, risk, utility, and option value. Across 100 universities, CatalogBrowse achieves 96.2% recall at 47% less source access, DegreeMap holds 100% feasibility with +0.066 utility gain, and the full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a 0.015 utility gap to the privileged gold graph.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Degree planning requires two sequential problems: reconstructing the curriculum from heterogeneous, schema-less university sources, then optimizing a student-specific path under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirements. Coupling the two stages lets each failure mode hide the other—a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. This motivates a decoupled architecture with an explicit extraction-first boundary.

Method: KnowPlan decomposes degree planning into two isolated stages. (1) CatalogBrowse: an exploration-only crawler with no access to user profiles; it scores actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over finite atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically via platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness. Outputs three provenance-linked JSON documents. (2) DegreeMap: consumes only those documents, compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph, and lexicographically optimizes with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load/risk, personalized utility, and option value, where each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum within solver budget.

Result: Across a 100-university broad track and six-school dense track: CatalogBrowse achieves 96.2% inventory recall and 88.7% masked-source recovery at 47% less source access than exhaustive crawling. DegreeMap maintains 100.0% hard feasibility while improving personalized utility by +0.066 over the strongest baseline. The full pipeline certifies 99.5% of requests with a utility gap of only 0.015 to the privileged gold graph (which has perfect information).

Conclusion: The strict decoupling between extraction and planning is effective: it prevents feedback loops where a planner's incomplete knowledge biases further crawling, and allows each stage to be evaluated independently. The closure certificate (rather than reward threshold) enables the system to know when it has complete information, and the lexicographic optimization ensures certifiable plans within solver budget. The small utility gap (0.015) to the gold standard suggests the extraction-first approach loses minimal information while saving significant compute resources.

Abstract: Planning a degree from official university sources requires solving two problems in order. The institution's curriculum must first be reconstructed from catalogs, departmental pages, JSON endpoints, and PDFs that share no schema, and only then can a student-specific path be optimized under prerequisite logic and overlapping requirement constraints. Coupling the two lets each failure mode hide the other, because a planner that drives its own crawling never learns facts its current plan does not need. We present KnowPlan, which enforces an extraction-first boundary and measures the interface between the stages rather than assuming it. CatalogBrowse explores with no access to any user profile. It scores legal actions by lower-confidence expected marginal gain over a finite set of atomic catalog obligations per unit of source access, parses deterministically through platform adapters with a span-constrained clause-to-AST model fallback, and terminates on a closure certificate over index, schema, provenance, and reference completeness instead of a reward threshold. Its output contract is three provenance-linked JSON documents. DegreeMap consumes only those documents. It compiles them into a typed requirement hypergraph and optimizes lexicographically with CP-SAT over hard feasibility, completion horizon, load and risk, personalized utility, and option value, so that each stage optimizes inside the previous stage's proven optimum and stays certifiable within the solver budget.
